; is_timer_interrupt
&12cb a9 01    LDA #&01
&12cd 8d 21 fe STA &fe21 ; video ULA palette register               # Set colour 0 to cyan for surface of water
&12d0 a0 18    LDY #&18
; delay_loop                                                        # Delay for one line of raster
&12d2 88       DEY                                                  # 2 cycles
&12d3 d0 fd    BNE &12d2 ; delay_loop                               # 3 cycles if branch taken, 2 if not; 119 cycle delay
&12d5 a9 03    LDA #&03
&12d7 8d 21 fe STA &fe21 ; video ULA palette register               # Set colour 0 to blue for water
&12da 4c a7 13 JMP &13a7 ; leave_after_restoring_registers
; is_vsync_interrupt                                                # Otherwise, the interrupt was caused by a v-sync
&12dd ad e4 14 LDA &14e4 ; waterline_timer_high                     # Zero if the waterline is not on screen
&12e0 f0 09    BEQ &12eb ; skip_setting_timer
&12e2 ac e3 14 LDY &14e3 ; waterline_timer_low
&12e5 8c 44 fe STY &fe44 ; System VIA timer 1 counter LSB
&12e8 8d 45 fe STA &fe45 ; System VIA timer 1 counter MSB
; skip_setting_timer
&12eb a9 07    LDA #&07
&12ed 8d 21 fe STA &fe21 ; video ULA palette register               # Set colour 0 to black for absence of water
&12f0 ee dc 11 INC &11dc ; vsync_state
&12f3 ad d3 14 LDA &14d3 ; palette_registers_need_updating          # Non-zero if palette registers need updating
&12f6 f0 06    BEQ &12fe ; skip_updating_palette_registers
&12f8 20 ac 9a JSR &9aac ; update_palette_registers                 # Returns Y = 0
&12fb 8c d3 14 STY &14d3 ; palette_registers_need_updating          # Set to zero to indicate palette doesn't need updating
; skip_updating_palette_registers
&12fe a9 7f    LDA #&7f                                             # Set top bit as input, low seven bits as output
&1300 8d 43 fe STA &fe43 ; System VIA data direction register A
&1303 a9 03    LDA #&03                                             # Disable keyboard auto-scan
&1305 8d 40 fe STA &fe40 ; System VIA port B input/output register
&1308 ae ed 11 LDX &11ed ; suppress_checking_for_keypresses         # Zero to suppress checking for all but pause
&130b f0 02    BEQ &130f ; check_for_keypresses_loop
&130d a2 26    LDX #&26
; check_for_keypresses_loop                                         # For each key,
&130f bd 3c 12 LDA &123c,X ; action_keycodes_table
&1312 8d 4f fe STA &fe4f ; System VIA input/output register A
&1315 ad 4f fe LDA &fe4f ; System VIA input/output register A
&1318 2a       ROL A                                                # Set carry if key pressed
&1319 7e 63 12 ROR &1263,X ; action_keys_pressed                    # Set &80 if key pressed
&131c ca       DEX
&131d 10 f0    BPL &130f ; check_for_keypresses_loop
&131f a9 0b    LDA #&0b                                             # Enable keyboard auto-scan
&1321 8d 40 fe STA &fe40 ; System VIA port B input/output register
&1324 e8       INX ; 0
&1325 8e ed 11 STX &11ed ; suppress_checking_for_keypresses         # Set to zero to suppress checking until actions processed
&1328 2c d2 14 BIT &14d2 ; suppress_updating_sound                  # Positive if game paused
&132b 10 7a    BPL &13a7 ; leave_after_restoring_registers
&132d 20 8c 12 JSR &128c ; update_game_time_or_player_deaths        # X = 0 here, so update game time
&1330 a9 ff    LDA #&ff                                             # Set all bits as output
&1332 8d 43 fe STA &fe43 ; System VIA data direction register A
&1335 ae 92 11 LDX &1192 ; number_of_sound_channels
; update_sound_channel_loop                                         # Loop through X = 3 to 0 for sound channels
&1338 20 ae 13 JSR &13ae ; update_sound_envelope                    # Update volume envelope
&133b b0 0a    BCS &1347 ; skip_fading                              # Returns carry set if envelope hasn't ended
&133d bd a4 11 LDA &11a4,X ; sound_channels_volume
&1340 e9 01    SBC #&01                                             # Fade sound at end of duration
&1342 90 47    BCC &138b ; skip_setting_frequency
&1344 9d a4 11 STA &11a4,X ; sound_channels_volume
; skip_fading
&1347 e8       INX                                                  # X += 4, i.e. use frequency envelope
&1348 e8       INX
&1349 e8       INX
&134a e8       INX
&134b 20 ae 13 JSR &13ae ; update_sound_envelope                    # Update frequency envelope
&134e ca       DEX                                                  # Returns carry set if sound hasn't ended
&134f ca       DEX                                                  #         A = channel frequency
&1350 ca       DEX                                                  #         zero set at end of envelope stage
&1351 ca       DEX                                                  # Restore X to original value
&1352 90 37    BCC &138b ; skip_setting_frequency
&1354 d0 04    BNE &135a ; consider_setting_frequency
&1356 c5 23    CMP &23 ; previous_frequency
&1358 f0 31    BEQ &138b ; skip_setting_frequency               
; consider_setting_frequency
&135a 49 ff    EOR #&ff                                             # Frequency played = 4000000 / (32 * value written)
;                                                                   # so invert to keep lower numbers meaning lower pitches
&135c a0 04    LDY #&04
&135e 84 23    STY &23 ; frequency_high                             # Only bottom two bits will be used, so effectively zero
; find_frequency_range_loop                                         # Convert A into a value, depending on which range it is in:
&1360 88       DEY                                                  #     &00 - &3f -> &20 - &5f, Y = 0 -> &020 - &05f
&1361 d9 94 11 CMP &1194,Y ; frequency_ranges_limit_table           #     &40 - &83 -> &30 - &73, Y = 1 -> &060 - &0e6
&1364 90 fa    BCC &1360 ; find_frequency_range_loop                #     &84 - &b5 -> &3a - &6b, Y = 2 -> &0e8 - &1ac
&1366 f9 98 11 SBC &1198,Y ; frequency_ranges_base_table            #     &b6 - &ff -> &36 - &7f, Y = 3 -> &1b0 - &1fc
; shift_frequency_loop
&1369 88       DEY
&136a 30 06    BMI &1372 ; set_frequency
&136c 0a       ASL A
&136d 26 23    ROL &23 ; frequency_high
&136f 4c 69 13 JMP &1369 ; shift_frequency_loop
; set_frequency
&1372 48       PHA ; frequency_low
&1373 29 0f    AND #&0f                                             # 84218421 -> ....8421
&1375 1d 9c 11 ORA &119c,X ; sound_channels_set_frequency_byte_table
&1378 20 f9 13 JSR &13f9 ; write_byte_to_sound_chip                 # Set four least significant bits of frequency for channel
&137b 68       PLA ; frequency_low
&137c e0 00    CPX #&00
&137e f0 0b    BEQ &138b ; skip_setting_frequency
&1380 46 23    LSR &23 ; frequency_high
&1382 6a       ROR A                                                # 84218421 -> t8421842
&1383 46 23    LSR &23 ; frequency_high
&1385 6a       ROR A                                                #          -> tt842184
&1386 4a       LSR A                                                #          -> 0tt84218
&1387 4a       LSR A                                                #          -> 00tt8421
&1388 20 f9 13 JSR &13f9 ; write_byte_to_sound_chip                 # Set six most significant bits of frequency for channel
; skip_setting_frequency
&138b bd a4 11 LDA &11a4,X ; sound_channels_volume
&138e fd d4 11 SBC &11d4,X ; sound_channels_volume_reduction
&1391 b0 02    BCS &1395 ; skip_floor
&1393 a9 00    LDA #&00
; skip_floor
&1395 2d 8a 12 AND &128a ; sound_enabled                            # &ff if sound enabled, &00 if sound disabled
&1398 49 ff    EOR #&ff
&139a 4a       LSR A
&139b 4a       LSR A
&139c 4a       LSR A
&139d 4a       LSR A
&139e 1d a0 11 ORA &11a0,X ; sound_channels_set_volume_byte_table
&13a1 20 f9 13 JSR &13f9 ; write_byte_to_sound_chip                 # Set volume for channel
&13a4 ca       DEX
&13a5 10 91    BPL &1338 ; update_sound_channel_loop
; leave_after_restoring_registers
&13a7 68       PLA ; tmp_x
&13a8 aa       TAX
&13a9 68       PLA ; tmp_y
&13aa a8       TAY
&13ab a5 fc    LDA &fc ; irq_accumulator
&13ad 40       RTI

; update_sound_envelope                                             # Called with X = channel + 0 for volume
&13ae 18       CLC                                                  #             X = channel + 4 for frequency
&13af bd c4 11 LDA &11c4,X ; sound_channels_volume_duration         # Are there any stages of the envelope remaining?
&13b2 f0 44    BEQ &13f8 ; leave                                    # Leave with carry clear to indicate envelope has ended
&13b4 bc ac 11 LDY &11ac,X ; sound_channels_volume_stage_offset
&13b7 bd b4 11 LDA &11b4,X ; sound_channels_volume_stage_duration   # Non-zero if this stage hasn't ended
&13ba d0 2d    BNE &13e9 ; skip_starting_stage
; start_stage
&13bc bd bc 11 LDA &11bc,X ; sound_channels_volume_loops_remaining  # Non-zero if this loop hasn't ended
&13bf d0 05    BNE &13c6 ; not_end_of_loop
&13c1 de c4 11 DEC &11c4,X ; sound_channels_volume_duration
&13c4 f0 32    BEQ &13f8 ; leave                                    # Leave at end of envelope
; not_end_of_loop
&13c6 c8       INY
&13c7 b9 e8 2d LDA &2de8,Y ; envelopes_table                        # Get first byte of stage data
&13ca 10 15    BPL &13e1 ; not_loop                                 # If positive, use as duration of stage
; is_loop
&13cc de bc 11 DEC &11bc,X ; sound_channels_volume_loops_remaining  # Negative indicates start or end of a loop
&13cf 10 0a    BPL &13db ; skip_starting_loop                       # Has the current loop ended?
; start_loop
&13d1 29 7f    AND #&7f                                             # Low seven bits indicate number of loops
&13d3 9d bc 11 STA &11bc,X ; sound_channels_volume_loops_remaining
&13d6 c8       INY                                                  # Next byte is start of loop
&13d7 98       TYA
&13d8 9d cc 11 STA &11cc,X ; sound_channels_volume_loop_offset
; skip_starting_loop
&13db bc cc 11 LDY &11cc,X ; sound_channels_volume_loop_offset      # Return to start of loop
&13de b9 e8 2d LDA &2de8,Y ; envelopes_table
; not_loop
&13e1 9d b4 11 STA &11b4,X ; sound_channels_volume_stage_duration
&13e4 c8       INY                                                  # Move to second byte of stage data
&13e5 98       TYA
&13e6 9d ac 11 STA &11ac,X ; sound_channels_volume_stage_offset
; skip_starting_stage
&13e9 bd a4 11 LDA &11a4,X ; sound_channels_volume
&13ec 85 23    STA &23 ; previous_volume
&13ee 79 e8 2d ADC &2de8,Y ; envelopes_table                        # Second byte of stage data is added to value
&13f1 9d a4 11 STA &11a4,X ; sound_channels_volume                  # Leave with A = volume or frequency value
&13f4 de b4 11 DEC &11b4,X ; sound_channels_volume_stage_duration   # Leave with zero set at end of an envelope stage
&13f7 38       SEC                                                  # Leave with carry set to indicate envelope hasn't ended
; leave
&13f8 60       RTS

; write_byte_to_sound_chip 
&13f9 8d 4f fe STA &fe4f ; System VIA input/output register A
&13fc a9 00    LDA #&00                                             # Set sound chip write pin low
&13fe 8d 40 fe STA &fe40 ; System VIA port B input/output register
&1401 a9 22    LDA #&22 ; 00100010
; delay_loop_one
&1403 4a       LSR A                                                # 2 cycles
&1404 90 fd    BCC &1403 ; delay_loop_one                           # 3 cycles if branch taken, 2 if not
;                                                                   # Don't branch on second pass; 9 cycle delay
&1406 8d 40 fe STA &fe40 ; System VIA port B input/output register  # Set sound chip write pin high (A = 8 here)
; delay_loop_two
&1409 4a       LSR A
&140a 90 fd    BCC &1409 ; delay_loop_two                           # Don't branch on fourth pass; 19 cycle delay
&140c 60       RTS

# Sounds
# ======
# Calls to play_sound and play_sound_on_channel_zero are followed by a four
# byte parameter block. These specify envelopes and starting values for volume
# (first two bytes) and frequency (second two bytes). These are as follows:
#
# 84218421 ........ envelope to use (offset into envelopes_table - 1)
# ........ 8421.... initial value of volume or frequency
# ........ ....8421 duration of envelope in stages or loops

; play_sound_on_channel_zero
&140d 38       SEC                                                  # Set carry to use channel zero
&140e 24 18    BIT &18 ; (nop)
; play_sound
#140f          CLC                                                  # Clear carry to use channels one, two or three
&1410 85 98    STA &98 ; tmp_a
&1412 86 99    STX &99 ; tmp_x
&1414 84 9a    STY &9a ; tmp_y
&1416 08       PHP ; carry set if channel zero
&1417 68       PLA ; carry set if channel zero                      # Parameters follow function call
&1418 aa       TAX
&1419 68       PLA ; return_address_low
&141a 85 9b    STA &9b ; parameters_address_low
&141c 18       CLC
&141d 69 04    ADC #&04                                             # Four bytes of parameters
&141f a8       TAY
&1420 68       PLA ; return_address_high
&1421 85 9c    STA &9c ; parameters_address_high
&1423 69 00    ADC #&00
&1425 48       PHA ; return_address_high                            # Return to code after end of parameters
&1426 98       TYA
&1427 48       PHA ; return_address_low
&1428 8a       TXA
&1429 48       PHA ; carry set
&142a 20 c0 35 JSR &35c0 ; get_object_distance_from_screen_centre
&142d c9 10    CMP #&10                                             # Don't play a sound if too distant
&142f b0 78    BCS &14a9 ; leave_after_restoring_registers_and_flags
&1431 0a       ASL A
&1432 0a       ASL A
&1433 0a       ASL A
&1434 0a       ASL A
&1435 a8       TAY ; volume reduction                               # More distant objects are quieter
&1436 28       PLP ; carry set if channel zero
&1437 90 09    BCC &1442 ; not_channel_zero
; is_channel_zero
&1439 a2 00    LDX #&00
&143b ad c4 11 LDA &11c4 ; sound_channels_volume_duration + 0       # Zero if no sound is being played on channel
&143e f0 39    BEQ &1479 ; play_sound_on_channel
&1440 b0 22    BCS &1464 ; find_quieter_channel_loop                # Always branches
; not_channel_zero
&1442 ae 92 11 LDX &1192 ; number_of_sound_channels
; find_free_channel_loop
&1445 bd c4 11 LDA &11c4,X ; sound_channels_volume_duration         # Zero if no sound being played on channel
&1448 f0 2f    BEQ &1479 ; play_sound_on_channel
&144a ca       DEX
&144b d0 f8    BNE &1445 ; find_free_channel_loop                   # Check channels three, two and one
&144d ad d9 11 LDA &11d9 ; sound_channels_parameters_address_low + 1
&1450 4d da 11 EOR &11da ; sound_channels_parameters_address_low + 2
&1453 4d db 11 EOR &11db ; sound_channels_parameters_address_low + 3
&1456 ae 92 11 LDX &1192 ; number_of_sound_channels
; find_similar_channel_loop
&1459 dd d8 11 CMP &11d8,X ; sound_channels_parameters_address_low  # Are two channels playing the same sound?
;                                                                   # If so, X is the remaining channel
&145c f0 15    BEQ &1473 ; is_similar
&145e ca       DEX
&145f d0 f8    BNE &1459 ; find_similar_channel_loop
&1461 ae 92 11 LDX &1192 ; number_of_sound_channels
; find_quieter_channel_loop
&1464 98       TYA ; volume reduction
&1465 dd d5 11 CMP &11d5,X ; sound_channels_volume_reduction + 1
&1468 f0 0f    BEQ &1479 ; play_sound_on_channel                    # Branch if same
&146a 90 0d    BCC &1479 ; play_sound_on_channel                    # or nearer distance
&146c ca       DEX
&146d f0 3b    BEQ &14aa ; leave_after_restoring_registers          # Don't play sound if further away
&146f 10 f3    BPL &1464 ; find_quieter_channel_loop
&1471 30 37    BMI &14aa ; leave_after_restoring_registers          # Always branches (for channel 0)
; is_similar
&1473 ca       DEX                                                  # Use one of the two channels
&1474 d0 03    BNE &1479 ; play_sound_on_channel
&1476 ae 92 11 LDX &1192 ; number_of_sound_channels                 # If X was 1, channel 3 is one of them
; play_sound_on_channel
&1479 98       TYA ; volume reduction
&147a 9d d4 11 STA &11d4,X ; sound_channels_volume_reduction
&147d a5 9b    LDA &9b ; parameters_address_low
&147f 9d d8 11 STA &11d8,X ; sound_channels_parameters_address_low
&1482 a0 01    LDY #&01                                             # parameters_address is address of parameters - 1
&1484 08       PHP ; flags
&1485 78       SEI
; set_envelopes_from_parameters_loop                                # Start with X = 0, Y = 1 for volume envelope
&1486 b1 9b    LDA (&9b),Y ; parameters_address                     # First (or third) byte of parameters sets offset into
&1488 9d ac 11 STA &11ac,X ; sound_channels_volume_stage_offset     # envelope table, i.e. which envelope to use
&148b c8       INY
&148c b1 9b    LDA (&9b),Y ; parameters_address                     # Second (or fourth) byte of parameters
&148e 29 f0    AND #&f0
&1490 9d a4 11 STA &11a4,X ; sound_channels_volume                  # Top nibble sets initial value of envelope
&1493 51 9b    EOR (&9b),Y ; parameters_address
&1495 9d c4 11 STA &11c4,X ; sound_channels_volume_duration         # Bottom nibble sets duration of envelope
&1498 c8       INY
&1499 a9 00    LDA #&00
&149b 9d b4 11 STA &11b4,X ; sound_channels_volume_stage_duration   # Set to zero to update envelope on next update
&149e 9d bc 11 STA &11bc,X ; sound_channels_volume_loops_remaining
&14a1 e8       INX                                                  # Repeat with X = 4, Y = 3 for frequency envelope
&14a2 e8       INX
&14a3 e8       INX
&14a4 e8       INX
&14a5 e0 08    CPX #&08
&14a7 90 dd    BCC &1486 ; set_envelopes_from_parameters_loop
; leave_after_restoring_registers_and_flags
&14a9 28       PLP ; flags
; leave_after_restoring_registers
&14aa a5 98    LDA &98 ; tmp_a
&14ac a6 99    LDX &99 ; tmp_x
&14ae a4 9a    LDY &9a ; tmp_y
&14b0 38       SEC                                                  # This is used at &2d9c, &9f62, &a023 and &a9db
; handle_pressing_shift                                             # SHIFT by itself does nothing
&14b1 60       RTS

; play_middle_beep
&14b2 20 0f 14 JSR &140f ; play_sound
&14b5 17 e3 2f 72                                                   # Play sound for middle beep
&14b9 60       RTS

; play_high_beep

; handle_preparing_game_for_save
&14eb 2c 89 12 BIT &1289 ; action_keys_pressed + &26 (SHIFT)        # Negative if SHIFT pressed

; prepare_game_for_save
&14f0 20 b5 39 JSR &39b5 ; check_copy_protection                    # Hangs if demo mode
&14f3 f8       SED
; prepare_game_for_save_without_copy_protection_check
&14f4 78       SEI
&14f5 a5 da    LDA &da ; rnd_state + 1
&14f7 45 dc    EOR &dc ; rnd_state + 3
&14f9 29 7f    AND #&7f
&14fb 49 65    EOR #&65
&14fd 8d 2d 08 STA &082d ; copy_protection_third_byte               # permuted_rnd_pair
&1500 38       SEC
&1501 a0 07    LDY #&07
; copy_variables_loop                                               # Copy &00d9 - &00df to &07f8 - &07fe
&1503 b9 d8 00 LDA &00d8,Y ; rnd_state - 1                          #      &00d9 - &00dc to &07f8 - &07fb rnd_state
&1506 99 f7 07 STA &07f7,Y ; game_state - 1                         #              &00dd to &07fc         player_object_held
&1509 88       DEY                                                  #              &00de to &07fd         player_angle
&150a d0 f7    BNE &1503 ; copy_variables_loop                      #              &00df to &07fe         player_facing
; encrypt_game_state
&150c a9 6e    LDA #&6e
&150e 85 9d    STA &9d ; key
&1510 a9 92    LDA #&92
; encrypt_game_state_loop                                           # Encrypt &07f8 - &5bf7 to &2c00 - &7fff
&1512 65 9d    ADC &9d ; key
&1514 69 15    ADC #&15
&1516 85 9d    STA &9d ; key
&1518 b9 f8 07 LDA &07f8,Y ; game_state
#     actually LDA source_address,Y
&151b 48       PHA ; byte of game state
&151c 4d 52 0b EOR &0b52 ; game_state_checksum_one
&151f 8d 52 0b STA &0b52 ; game_state_checksum_one
&1522 68       PLA ; byte of game state
&1523 45 9d    EOR &9d ; key
&1525 48       PHA ; encrypted byte of game state
&1526 4d 75 0b EOR &0b75 ; game_state_checksum_two
&1529 8d 75 0b STA &0b75 ; game_state_checksum_two
&152c 68       PLA ; encrypted byte of game state
&152d 99 00 2c STA &2c00,Y ; saved_game_state
#     actually STA target_address,Y
&1530 c8       INY
&1531 d0 df    BNE &1512 ; encrypt_game_state_loop
&1533 ee 1a 15 INC &151a ; source_address_high
&1536 ee 2f 15 INC &152f ; target_address_high
&1539 10 d7    BPL &1512 ; encrypt_game_state_loop
&153b 4c ea 28 JMP &28ea ; wipe_memory_then_infinite_loop
; leave
&153e 60       RTS

; consider_how_to_scroll_screen
&153f 4e 76 0b LSR &0b76 ; secondary_object_update_mode             # Clear top bit to consider only next secondary object
&1542 4e 91 11 LSR &1191 ; suppress_tile_scrolling              *** # Clear top bit to allow tile scrolling next time
&1545 2c 91 11 BIT &1191 ; suppress_tile_scrolling              ***
&1548 50 07    BVC &1551 ; not_suppressing_tile_scrolling       *** # If top bit was set,
&154a a9 00    LDA #&00                                         ***
&154c 85 cf    STA &cf ; screen_tile_sections_to_scroll_x       *** # Set to zero to suppress tile scrolling
&154e 85 d1    STA &d1 ; screen_tile_sections_to_scroll_y       *** # in both directions
&1550 60       RTS                                              ***
; not_suppressing_tile_scrolling                                ***
&1551 20 bb 22 JSR &22bb ; get_this_object_centre                   # Get the player's centre
; consider_how_to_scroll_screen_in_x
&1554 a2 00    LDX #&00                                             # Calculate for x
&1556 20 f6 15 JSR &15f6 ; calculate_amount_of_scrolling_needed_in_direction
&1559 85 a2    STA &a2 ; amount_of_scrolling_needed_in_x            # In &20 fraction sections, eight per tile
&155b 20 bb 32 JSR &32bb ; make_positive
&155e 85 9d    STA &9d ; absolute_amount_of_scrolling_needed_in_x
&1560 c9 0c    CMP #&0c                                             # If the screen needs to scroll more than one and a
&1562 b0 4e    BCS &15b2 ; redraw_screen                            # half tiles horizontally, redraw it
&1564 c9 02    CMP #&02
&1566 90 14    BCC &157c ; set_tile_sections_to_scroll_x
&1568 f0 08    BEQ &1572 ; skip_checking_half_tile_alignment
&156a a0 04    LDY #&04
&156c a5 c7    LDA &c7 ; screen_origin_x_fraction
&156e 29 7f    AND #&7f
&1570 f0 09    BEQ &157b ; set_tile_sections_to_scroll_x_from_Y     # If aligned with a full or half tile, scroll half a tile
; skip_checking_half_tile_alignment
&1572 a0 02    LDY #&02
&1574 a5 c7    LDA &c7 ; screen_origin_x_fraction
&1576 29 20    AND #&20
&1578 f0 01    BEQ &157b ; set_tile_sections_to_scroll_x_from_Y     # If aligned with a quarter tile, scroll quarter of a tile
&157a 88       DEY ; 1                                              # Otherwise, scroll an eighth of a tile
; set_tile_sections_to_scroll_x_from_Y
&157b 98       TYA
; set_tile_sections_to_scroll_x
&157c 24 a2    BIT &a2 ; amount_of_scrolling_needed_in_x            # Use sign of amount of scrolling required
&157e 20 bd 32 JSR &32bd ; invert_if_negative
&1581 85 cf    STA &cf ; screen_tile_sections_to_scroll_x           # This may not be used if y scrolling needed is greater
; consider_how_to_scroll_screen_in_y
&1583 a2 02    LDX #&02                                             # Calculate for y
&1585 20 f6 15 JSR &15f6 ; calculate_amount_of_scrolling_needed_in_direction
&1588 a8       TAY ; y scrolling needed
&1589 20 bd 32 JSR &32bd ; invert_if_negative
&158c c5 9d    CMP &9d ; absolute_amount_of_scrolling_needed_in_x   # If scrolling needed is greater in x than y,
&158e 90 1d    BCC &15ad ; zero_screen_tile_sections_to_scroll_y    # scroll horizontally, not vertically
&1590 c9 0c    CMP #&0c                                             # If the screen needs to scroll more than one and a
&1592 b0 1e    BCS &15b2 ; redraw_screen                            # half tiles vertically, redraw it
&1594 c9 02    CMP #&02
&1596 98       TYA ; y scrolling needed
&1597 90 0d    BCC &15a6 ; set_screen_tile_sections_to_scroll_y     # Scroll a single section if needed vertically
&1599 a9 01    LDA #&01                                             # If more sections needed, only scroll one at a time
&159b 24 c9    BIT &c9 ; screen_origin_y_fraction
&159d 70 02    BVS &15a1 ; not_aligned                              # unless aligned to a full or half tile,
&159f a9 02    LDA #&02                                             # in which case, scroll two sections
; not_aligned
&15a1 c0 00    CPY #&00                                             # Use sign of amount of scrolling required
&15a3 20 bd 32 JSR &32bd ; invert_if_negative
; set_screen_tile_sections_to_scroll_y
&15a6 85 d1    STA &d1 ; screen_tile_sections_to_scroll_y           # Scroll vertically, not horizontally
&15a8 a9 00    LDA #&00
&15aa 85 cf    STA &cf ; screen_tile_sections_to_scroll_x
&15ac 60       RTS
; zero_screen_tile_sections_to_scroll_y
&15ad a9 00    LDA #&00                                             # Scroll horizontally, not vertically
&15af 85 d1    STA &d1 ; screen_tile_sections_to_scroll_y
&15b1 60       RTS
; redraw_screen
&15b2 a9 80    LDA #&80
&15b4 85 c7    STA &c7 ; screen_origin_x_fraction                   # These are single values, not pairs for current and
&15b6 85 c9    STA &c9 ; screen_origin_y_fraction                   # previous sprite plotting, like screen_start_*
&15b8 a5 8d    LDA &8d ; this_object_centre_y
&15ba 38       SEC
&15bb e9 03    SBC #&03                                         *** # Set top of screen to player y - 2.5 tiles
&15bd 85 ca    STA &ca ; screen_origin_y

&15c3 85 c8    STA &c8 ; screen_origin_x

&15d3 a5 ca    LDA &ca ; screen_origin_y
&15d5 18       CLC
&15d6 69 08    ADC #&08                                         *** # Starting at the bottom of the screen, eight tiles down
&15d8 85 ca    STA &ca ; screen_origin_y
&15da a2 10    LDX #&10                                         *** # 16 * 2 = 32 sections = 8 tiles
; redraw_screen_loop
&15dc 86 ab    STX &ab ; rows_remaining
&15de 20 43 16 JSR &1643 ; update_screen_variables
&15e1 20 eb 36 JSR &36eb ; prepare_screen_for_scrolling             # Wipe strip of screen and cache tiles

&15e7 a6 ab    LDX &ab ; rows_remaining
&15e9 ca       DEX
&15ea d0 f0    BNE &15dc ; redraw_screen_loop
&15ec 86 d1    STX &d1 ; screen_tile_sections_to_scroll_y           # Set to zero to indicate no scrolling needed
&15ee a9 f0    LDA #&f0
&15f0 85 6e    STA &6e ; offscreen_flags                            # Set top four bits to suppress calculating sprite variables for player
&15f2 8d 76 0b STA &0b76 ; secondary_object_update_mode             # Set top bit to consider all secondary objects on redraw
&15f5 60       RTS

; calculate_amount_of_scrolling_needed_in_direction                 # Called with X = 0 for x, X = 2 for y
&15f6 2c cf 19 BIT &19cf ; ship_moving                              # Negative if ship is moving at end of game
&15f9 10 09    BPL &1604 ; not_moving_ship
&15fb 8a       TXA
&15fc 49 02    EOR #&02
&15fe d0 3f    BNE &163f ; leave                                    # Leave if calculating x, with A = 2 to scroll right
&1600 a9 3b    LDA #&3b
&1602 85 8d    STA &8d ; this_object_centre_y                       # Fix player y position at end of game
; not_moving_ship
&1604 b5 87    LDA &87,X ; this_object_centre_x_fraction
&1606 d5 c7    CMP &c7,X ; screen_origin_x_fraction
&1608 08       PHP ; relative x fraction carry
&1609 b5 43    LDA &43,X ; this_object_velocity_x                   # Consider the player's velocity
&160b e0 00    CPX #&00
&160d f0 03    BEQ &1612 ; not_y
&160f c9 80    CMP #&80                                             # If y, divide by &10; &40 (0.25 tile/update) -> 4
&1611 6a       ROR A
; not_y
&1612 20 dc 32 JSR &32dc ; divide_by_eight                          # If x, divide by &08; &40 (0.25 tile/update) -> 8
&1615 dd 40 16 CMP &1640,X ; screen_scrolling_velocity_x
&1618 f0 0b    BEQ &1625 ; skip_changing_amount_owing               # If this isn't the same as the scrolling velocity,
&161a 10 06    BPL &1622 ; increase_amount_owing                    # adjust scrolling velocity towards it; this keeps the
;                                                                   # scrolling from starting or stopping abruptly
; decrease_amount_owing
&161c de 40 16 DEC &1640,X ; screen_scrolling_velocity_x
&161f de 40 16 DEC &1640,X ; screen_scrolling_velocity_x
; increase_amount_owing
&1622 fe 40 16 INC &1640,X ; screen_scrolling_velocity_x
; skip_changing_amount_owing
&1625 bd 40 16 LDA &1640,X ; screen_scrolling_velocity_x            # Scrolling velocity is in &20 fraction sections for x
;                                                                   #                          &40 fraction sections for y
&1628 20 dc 32 JSR &32dc ; divide_by_eight                          # Calculate corresponding number of tiles for x
;                                                                   #                     but number of half tiles for y
&162b 7d dd 14 ADC &14dd,X ; screen_scroll_offset_x                 # Add any viewport scroll
&162e 18       CLC
&162f 75 8b    ADC &8b,X ; this_object_centre_x
&1631 28       PLP ; relative x fraction carry                      # Account for difference in fractions
&1632 f5 c8    SBC &c8,X ; screen_origin_x
&1634 38       SEC
&1635 fd dc 14 SBC &14dc,X ; screen_centre_offset_x                 # player_x - centre_x + scrolling_velocity_x
;                                                                   # Will be used as screen_tile_sections_to_scroll_x
;                                                                   # if this is the component needing most scrolling
&1638 d5 cf    CMP &cf,X ; screen_tile_sections_to_scroll_x
&163a 10 03    BPL &163f ; leave                                    # Leave if scrolling more to right/down than previously
&163c 18       CLC
&163d 69 01    ADC #&01
; leave
&163f 60       RTS

&1746 85 09    STA &09 ; tile_flip
&1748 45 08    EOR &08 ; tile_type_and_flip
&174a c9 09    CMP #&09 ; TILE_CHECK_TERTIARY_OBJECT_RANGE_EIGHT + 1
&174c b0 48    BCS &1796 ; skip_checking_tertiary_objects           # Tile type will be replaced at &1785 if <= &09
; check_for_tertiary_objects
&174e 85 9d    STA &9d ; tile_type
&1750 a8       TAY
&1751 be d4 05 LDX &05d4,Y ; tile_tertiary_object_ranges_table + 1  # Get offset of last tertiary object of next type, + 1
&1754 bd ef 05 LDA &05ef,X ; tertiary_objects_x
&1757 48       PHA ; previous_tertiary_object_x
&1758 a5 95    LDA &95 ; tile_x
&175a 9d ef 05 STA &05ef,X ; tertiary_objects_x                     # Temporarily use its value as a final one to check
&175d be d3 05 LDX &05d3,Y ; tile_tertiary_object_ranges_table      # Get offset of first tertiary object of this type
&1760 ca       DEX
; check_for_tertiary_objects_loop                                   # For each tertiary object of this type,
&1761 e8       INX
&1762 dd ef 05 CMP &05ef,X ; tertiary_objects_x                     # Is its x coordinate the same as the tile?
&1765 d0 fa    BNE &1761 ; check_for_tertiary_objects_loop
&1767 8a       TXA ; tertiary_object_offset_in_range
&1768 d9 d4 05 CMP &05d4,Y ; tile_tertiary_object_ranges_table + 1  # Was a tertiary object found?
&176b b0 11    BCS &177e ; add_feature_tile                         # If not, add a landscape feature
&176d 79 dd 05 ADC &05dd,Y ; tertiary_objects_data_offset
&1770 85 bd    STA &bd ; this_object_tertiary_data_offset           # Not all tertiary objects have a data byte
&1772 18       CLC
&1773 79 e6 05 ADC &05e6,Y ; tertiary_objects_type_offset
&1776 85 be    STA &be ; this_object_tertiary_type_offset           # Not all tertiary objects have a type byte
&1778 bd ee 06 LDA &06ee,X ; tertiary_objects_tile_and_flip
&177b 4c 85 17 JMP &1785 ; set_tile_type_and_flip                   # Use tile for tertiary object
; add_feature_tile
&177e a6 9d    LDX &9d ; tile_type                                  # Use the tile type to determine a feature to add
&1780 bd 74 11 LDA &1174,X ; feature_tiles_table
&1783 45 09    EOR &09 ; tile_flip                                  # keeping the flip given by the algorithm or map data
; set_tile_type_and_flip
&1785 85 08    STA &08 ; tile_type_and_flip
&1787 be d4 05 LDX &05d4,Y ; tile_tertiary_object_ranges_table + 1
&178a 68       PLA ; previous_tertiary_object_x
&178b 9d ef 05 STA &05ef,X ; tertiary_objects_x                     # Restore previous value of first object of next type
&178e a5 08    LDA &08 ; tile_type_and_flip
&1790 29 c0    AND #&c0 ; TILE_FLIP_HORIZONTAL | TILE_FLIP_VERTICAL
&1792 85 09    STA &09 ; tile_flip
&1794 45 08    EOR &08 ; tile_type_and_flip                         # Remove flip flags
; skip_checking_tertiary_objects
&1796 85 08    STA &08 ; tile_type                                  # Tile types are now those that will be used in the game
&1798 c9 10    CMP #&10 ; TILE_MUSHROOMS + 1
&179a b0 14    BCS &17b0 ; leave                                    # Does this tile have an update routine?
&179c ba       TSX
&179d 86 26    STX &26 ; stack_pointer_to_leave_to_if_unable_to_create_primary_object # Unnecessary code; overwritten at &1a0f
&179f aa       TAX ; tile type
&17a0 bd 32 04 LDA &0432,X ; object_types_update_routine_addresses_high_table
&17a3 24 2d    BIT &2d ; tile_processing_mode                       # Should the tile update routine be called for this function?
&17a5 f0 07    BEQ &17ae ; skip_calling_tile_update_routine         # If so, corresponding bit in high nibble will be set
&17a7 29 0f    AND #&0f                                             # Low nibble is high byte of update routine address offset
&17a9 a4 bd    LDY &bd ; this_object_tertiary_data_offset
&17ab 20 0e 1a JSR &1a0e ; call_tile_update_routine                 # This may convert a tertiary object into a primary one
; skip_calling_tile_update_routine
&17ae a5 08    LDA &08 ; tile_type                                  # Leave with A = tile type
&17b0 60       RTS

; get_tile
&17b1 a5 97    LDA &97 ; tile_y                                     # Calculate a triangular function of x and y, f1
&17b3 aa       TAX
&17b4 4a       LSR A
&17b5 45 95    EOR &95 ; tile_x
&17b7 29 f8    AND #&f8
&17b9 4a       LSR A
&17ba 65 95    ADC &95 ; tile_x
&17bc 4a       LSR A
&17bd 65 97    ADC &97 ; tile_y
&17bf 85 9d    STA &9d ; f1_tile_xy
&17c1 8a       TXA ; tile_y                                         # Use f1 and y to determine whether to use mapped data
&17c2 c9 79    CMP #&79
&17c4 90 06    BCC &17cc ; skip_subtraction
&17c6 c9 bf    CMP #&bf
&17c8 90 50    BCC &181a ; get_tile_from_algorithm_skipping_check
&17ca e9 46    SBC #&46
; skip_subtraction
&17cc c9 48    CMP #&48
&17ce b0 06    BCS &17d6 ; skip_addition
&17d0 c9 3e    CMP #&3e
&17d2 b0 46    BCS &181a ; get_tile_from_algorithm_skipping_check
&17d4 69 0a    ADC #&0a
; skip_addition
&17d6 85 10    STA &10 ; f2_tile_xy                                 # Calculate three permuted versions of f1
&17d8 29 a8    AND #&a8
&17da 49 6f    EOR #&6f
&17dc 4a       LSR A
&17dd 65 95    ADC &95 ; tile_x
&17df 49 60    EOR #&60
&17e1 69 28    ADC #&28
&17e3 85 0f    STA &0f ; f3_tile_xy
&17e5 29 38    AND #&38
&17e7 49 a4    EOR #&a4
&17e9 65 10    ADC &10 ; f2_tile_xy
&17eb 85 10    STA &10 ; f2_tile_xy
&17ed a8       TAY
&17ee 49 2c    EOR #&2c
&17f0 65 0f    ADC &0f ; f3_tile_xy                                 # A = f4_tile_xy
&17f2 c0 20    CPY #&20
&17f4 b0 24    BCS &181a ; get_tile_from_algorithm_skipping_check   # Use f3 to determine whether to use mapped data
&17f6 c9 20    CMP #&20
&17f8 b0 1c    BCS &1816 ; get_tile_from_algorithm
; get_tile_from_map_data                                            # Use f2 and f4 to determine offset into map data
&17fa c6 00    DEC &00 ; tile_was_from_map_data                     # Set top bit to indicate tile was from map data
&17fc a8       TAY ; f4_tile_xy
&17fd 0a       ASL A
&17fe 0a       ASL A
&17ff 0a       ASL A
&1800 45 10    EOR &10 ; f2_tile_xy
&1802 85 a4    STA &a4 ; map_data_address_low
&1804 98       TYA ; f4_tile_xy
&1805 29 03    AND #&03
&1807 69 af    ADC #&af ; &4fec = map_data
&1809 85 a5    STA &a5 ; map_data_address_high
&180b a0 ec    LDY #&ec
&180d b1 a4    LDA (&a4),Y ; map_data_address                       # Get tile type and flip from map data
&180f 60       RTS

; to_get_tile_for_surface
&1810 4c 5b 19 JMP &195b ; get_tile_for_surface

; to_leave_with_empty_tile
&1813 4c fc 18 JMP &18fc ; leave_with_empty_tile

; get_tile_from_algorithm                                           # A = f4_tile_xy, X = tile_y
&1816 c9 3d    CMP #&3d
&1818 90 f9    BCC &1813 ; to_leave_with_empty_tile                 # Add irregular caverns near mapped data
; get_tile_from_algorithm_skipping_check
&181a e0 4e    CPX #&4e                                             # If y < &4e, i.e. above surface, use TILE_SPACE
&181c 90 f5    BCC &1813 ; to_leave_with_empty_tile
&181e f0 f0    BEQ &1810 ; to_get_tile_for_surface
&1820 e0 4f    CPX #&4f
&1822 d0 0e    BNE &1832 ; is_below_surface
; is_one_below_surface                                              # If y = &4f, i.e. one below surface, use solid layer
&1824 a5 95    LDA &95 ; tile_x                                     # except where removed by mapped data or irregular cavern
&1826 c9 40    CMP #&40
&1828 f0 05    BEQ &182f ; leave_with_leaf
&182a a0 01    LDY #&01                                             # Use TILE_EARTH (earth_tiles_rotation_table + 1)
&182c 4c 4b 19 JMP &194b ; to_leave_with_tile_from_table
; leave_with_leaf
&182f a9 62    LDA #&62 ; TILE_LEAF | TILE_FLIP_VERTICAL            # Set (&40, &4f) to be a vertically flipped leaf
&1831 60       RTS                                                  # ((&40, &4e) is set to TILE_LEAF by tertiary object &1b)
; is_below_surface
&1832 a4 9d    LDY &9d ; f1_tile_xy
&1834 a9 00    LDA #&00                                             # Use earth to fill in the sides and bottom of world
&1836 85 9d    STA &9d ; f1_tile_xy
&1838 a5 95    LDA &95 ; tile_x
&183a 24 97    BIT &97 ; tile_y
&183c 30 07    BMI &1845 ; is_bottom_half_of_world
; is_top_half_of_world                                              # If y < &80 (top half of world, top third of ground)
&183e 69 1d    ADC #&1d
&1840 c9 5e    CMP #&5e                                             # Set x = &00 - &40 and &e3 - &ff to be solid
&1842 4c 49 18 JMP &1849 ; check_if_side_of_world
; is_bottom_half_of_world                                           # If y >= &80,
&1845 69 07    ADC #&07
&1847 c9 2b    CMP #&2b                                             # Set x = &00 - &23 and &f9 - &ff to be solid
; check_if_side_of_world
&1849 90 55    BCC &18a0 ; to_leave_with_earth_or_stone             # i.e. fill in different sized sides of the world
&184b 98       TYA ; f1_tile_xy
&184c 29 e8    AND #&e8                                             # Using f1 to generate a complicated pattern,
&184e c5 97    CMP &97 ; tile_y
&1850 90 4e    BCC &18a0 ; to_leave_with_earth_or_stone             # set the bottom and other parts of the world to be earth
&1852 84 9d    STY &9d ; f1_tile_xy                                 # Otherwise, use f1 to determine whether to use earth or stone
&1854 8a       TXA ; tile_y                                         # Calculate another function of x and y, f5
&1855 0a       ASL A
&1856 65 97    ADC &97 ; tile_y
&1858 4a       LSR A
&1859 65 97    ADC &97 ; tile_y
&185b 29 e0    AND #&e0
&185d 65 95    ADC &95 ; tile_x
&185f 29 e8    AND #&e8
&1861 d0 13    BNE &1876 ; consider_passages_and_caverns            # Use f5 to determine where to add square caverns
; is_square_cavern
&1863 a5 97    LDA &97 ; tile_y
&1865 10 ac    BPL &1813 ; to_leave_with_empty_tile
; is_windy_square_cavern                                            # which are windy in the bottom half of the world
&1867 a5 95    LDA &95 ; tile_x
&1869 4a       LSR A
&186a 4a       LSR A
&186b 4a       LSR A
&186c aa       TAX
&186d a9 0e    LDA #&0e ; TILE_VARIABLE_WIND
&186f e0 0a    CPX #&0a                                             # Is this one of the two square caverns below the
&1871 d0 02    BNE &1875 ; skip_flip                                # west stone door? If so, use a constant downdraft
&1873 a9 8e    LDA #&8e ; TILE_VARIABLE_WIND | TILE_FLIP_HORIZONTAL
; skip_flip
&1875 60       RTS

; consider_passages_and_caverns                                     # Consider horizontal, vertical and sloping passages
&1876 98       TYA ; f1_tile_xy                                     # Calculate another permuted version of f1, f6
&1877 4a       LSR A
&1878 4a       LSR A
&1879 29 30    AND #&30
&187b 4a       LSR A
&187c 65 95    ADC &95 ; tile_x
&187e 4a       LSR A
&187f 45 95    EOR &95 ; tile_x
&1881 4a       LSR A
&1882 45 95    EOR &95 ; tile_x
&1884 65 95    ADC &95 ; tile_x
&1886 29 fd    AND #&fd
&1888 45 95    EOR &95 ; tile_x
&188a 29 07    AND #&07

&188e a5 95    LDA &95 ; tile_x

&1892 4a       LSR A
&1893 65 97    ADC &97 ; tile_y
&1895 29 30    AND #&30
&1897 f0 03    BEQ &189c ; not_vertical_shaft
; leave_with_vertical_shaft
&1899 a9 08    LDA #&08 ; TILE_CHECK_TERTIARY_OBJECT_RANGE_EIGHT
&189b 60       RTS
; not_vertical_shaft
&189c e0 52    CPX #&52                                             # Fill in a horizontal passage that would otherwise
&189e b0 03    BCS &18a3 ; not_close_to_surface                     # appear close to the surface
; to_leave_with_earth_or_stone
&18a0 4c 40 19 JMP &1940 ; leave_with_earth_or_stone
; not_close_to_surface
&18a3 98       TYA ; f1_tile_xy                                     # Calculate another permuted version of f1, f7
&18a4 29 68    AND #&68
&18a6 65 97    ADC &97 ; tile_y
&18a8 4a       LSR A
&18a9 65 97    ADC &97 ; tile_y
&18ab 4a       LSR A
&18ac 65 97    ADC &97 ; tile_y
&18ae 29 fc    AND #&fc
&18b0 45 97    EOR &97 ; tile_y
&18b2 29 17    AND #&17
&18b4 d0 4d    BNE &1903 ; consider_sloping_passage                 # Use f7 to determine where to add sloped passages
; consider_horizontal_passage
&18b6 98       TYA ; f1_tile_xy                                     # Calculate two permuted version of f1, f8 and f9
&18b7 65 95    ADC &95 ; tile_x
&18b9 29 50    AND #&50
&18bb f0 3f    BEQ &18fc ; leave_with_empty_tile                    # Use f8 to determine where to add empty horizontal passages
&18bd 25 95    AND &95 ; tile_x
&18bf 4a       LSR A
&18c0 4a       LSR A
&18c1 65 97    ADC &97 ; tile_y
&18c3 4a       LSR A
&18c4 4a       LSR A
&18c5 29 0f    AND #&0f
&18c7 c9 08    CMP #&08
&18c9 90 08    BCC &18d3 ; is_horizontal_passage_type_one           # Use f9 to determine where to add horizontal passages with features
; is_horizontal_passage_type_two
&18cb 24 9d    BIT &9d ; f1_tile_xy
&18cd 50 16    BVC &18e5 ; use_passage_feature
&18cf 09 04    ORA #&04
&18d1 d0 12    BNE &18e5 ; use_passage_feature                      # Always branches
; is_horizontal_passage_type_one
&18d3 85 9c    STA &9c ; special_type
&18d5 49 05    EOR #&05                                             # Use f9 to determine type of some special features
&18d7 c9 06    CMP #&06
&18d9 a5 9c    LDA &9c ; special_type
&18db b0 08    BCS &18e5 ; use_passage_feature
&18dd 98       TYA ; f1_tile_xy                                     # Calculate another permuted version of f1, f10
&18de 4a       LSR A
&18df 65 97    ADC &97 ; tile_y
&18e1 45 95    EOR &95 ; tile_x
&18e3 29 07    AND #&07                                             # Use f10 to determine type of some special features
; use_passage_feature
&18e5 18       CLC
&18e6 69 1d    ADC #&1d ; horizontal_passage_feature_tiles_table - earth_tiles_rotation_table
&18e8 48       PHA ; tile offset
&18e9 20 6a 19 JSR &196a ; calculate_slope_function_for_tile_x_y    # Returns carry clear if passage middle or edge
&18ec 68       PLA ; tile offset
&18ed 90 0d    BCC &18fc ; leave_with_empty_tile                    # Don't add features where crossed by sloping passage
&18ef a8       TAY
&18f0 b9 47 11 LDA &1147,Y ; horizontal_passage_feature_tiles_table - &1d
&18f3 a4 97    LDY &97 ; tile_y
&18f5 c0 e0    CPY #&e0
&18f7 d0 02    BNE &18fb ; skip_flip
&18f9 49 40    EOR #&40 ; TILE_FLIP_VERTICAL                        # Flip mushrooms in flooded passage at bottom right of world
; skip_flip
&18fb 60       RTS
; leave_with_empty_tile
&18fc a0 00    LDY #&00                                             # Use TILE_SPACE (earth_tiles_rotation_table + 0)
; leave_with_tile_from_table
&18fe 38       SEC
&18ff b9 47 11 LDA &1147,Y ; earth_tiles_rotation_table
&1902 60       RTS

; consider_sloping_passage
&1903 20 6a 19 JSR &196a ; calculate_slope_function_for_tile_x_y    # Returns carry clear if passage, Y = slope type
&1906 b0 38    BCS &1940 ; leave_with_earth_or_stone
&1908 c0 00    CPY #&00                                             # Y = 0 if empty centre of sloping passage
&190a f0 f2    BEQ &18fe ; leave_with_tile_from_table               # Leave with TILE_SPACE
; is_sloping_passage_edge
&190c a5 9d    LDA &9d ; f1_tile_xy                                 # Calculate another permuted version of f1, f11
&190e 48       PHA ; f1_tile_xy
&190f 84 9c    STY &9c ; slope_type
&1911 2a       ROL A                                                # 84218421 -> 8 4218421.
&1912 2a       ROL A                                                #          -> 4 218421.8
&1913 2a       ROL A                                                #          -> 2 18421.84
&1914 29 01    AND #&01                                             #          ->   .......4
&1916 2a       ROL A                                                #          ->   ......4.
&1917 a8       TAY                                                  # Y = 0 or 2, depending on &40 of f1
&1918 68       PLA ; f1_tile_xy
&1919 65 95    ADC &95 ; tile_x
&191b 2a       ROL A
&191c 45 97    EOR &97 ; tile_y
&191e 29 1a    AND #&1a
&1920 d0 15    BNE &1937 ; use_slope                                # Use f11 to determine where to add sloping passage features
; use_sloping_passage_feature
&1922 98       TYA
&1923 a4 9c    LDY &9c ; slope_type
&1925 59 4e 11 EOR &114e,Y ; tile_rotations_table - 2
&1928 29 7f    AND #&7f
&192a c9 40    CMP #&40                                             # Set carry if TILE_FLIP_VERTICAL
&192c 2a       ROL A                                                # Set &01 if TILE_FLIP_VERTICAL
&192d 29 07    AND #&07
&192f aa       TAX                                                  # X = (slope_type * 2) + (1 if TILE_FLIP_VERTICAL)
&1930 bd 58 11 LDA &1158,X ; sloping_passage_feature_tiles_table
&1933 59 4e 11 EOR &114e,Y ; tile_rotations_table - 2               # Apply rotation to slope
&1936 60       RTS
; use_slope
&1937 b9 54 11 LDA &1154,Y ; slope_tiles_table
&193a a4 9c    LDY &9c ; slope_type
&193c 59 4e 11 EOR &114e,Y ; tile_rotations_table - 2               # Apply rotation to slope
&193f 60       RTS

; leave_with_earth_or_stone
&1940 a5 9d    LDA &9d ; f1_tile_xy                                 # Zero if filling sides or bottom or world
&1942 4a       LSR A                                                # 84218421 -> .8421842
&1943 4a       LSR A                                                #          -> ..842184
&1944 4a       LSR A                                                #          -> ...84218
&1945 29 0e    AND #&0e                                             #          -> ....421.
&1947 4a       LSR A                                                #          -> .....421
&1948 69 01    ADC #&01                                             # i.e. convert f1 into an offset between &01 and &08
&194a a8       TAY
; to_leave_with_tile_from_table
&194b 4c fe 18 JMP &18fe ; leave_with_tile_from_table

; consider_adding_surface_feature
&194e 65 95    ADC &95 ; tile_x                                     # Use a function of x to determine which surface feature to add

&1951 2a       ROL A                                                #          -> 4 218421.8
&1952 2a       ROL A                                                #          -> 2 18421.84

; get_tile_for_surface
&195b a0 19    LDY #&19                                             # Use TILE_SPACE (earth_tiles_rotation_table + &19)

&195f 4a       LSR A

&1962 29 17    AND #&17
&1964 d0 e8    BNE &194e ; consider_adding_surface_feature          # Use a function of x to determine where to add surface features
&1966 66 9d    ROR &9d ; f1_tile_xy
&1968 6a       ROR A                                                # Set &80 (TILE_HORIZONTAL_FLIP) depending on f1
&1969 60       RTS

; calculate_slope_function_for_tile_x_y
&196a 8a       TXA ; tile_y
&196b 4a       LSR A
&196c 45 97    EOR &97 ; tile_y
&196e 29 06    AND #&06
&1970 d0 23    BNE &1995 ; skip_sloping_cavern
; consider_adding_sloping_cavern
&1972 98       TYA ; f1_tile_xy                                     # Use f1 to determine function
&1973 a0 02    LDY #&02
&1975 29 20    AND #&20                                             # 84218421 -> ..2....
&1977 0a       ASL A                                                #          -> .4.....
&1978 0a       ASL A                                                #          -> 8......
&1979 49 e5    EOR #&e5 ; SBC                                       # i.e. either &65 (ADC) or &e5 (SBC)
&197b 8d 85 19 STA &1985 ; sloping_cavern_function_opcode
&197e 30 02    BMI &1982 ; skip_flip
&1980 a0 04    LDY #&04
; skip_flip
&1982 8a       TXA ; tile_y                                         # Use a function of X and Y to determine where to add sloping caverns
&1983 69 16    ADC #&16
; sloping_cavern_function_opcode
&1985 65 95    ADC &95 ; tile_x
#     or       SBC &23 ; tile_x
&1987 29 5f    AND #&5f
&1989 aa       TAX
&198a ca       DEX
&198b e0 0c    CPX #&0c
&198d 90 38    BCC &19c7 ; leave_with_zero_Y                        # Leave with zero to indicate middle of sloping cavern
&198f f0 38    BEQ &19c9 ; leave_with_carry_clear                   # Leave with Y non-zero to indicate edge of sloping cavern
&1991 c8       INY
&1992 e8       INX
&1993 f0 34    BEQ &19c9 ; leave_with_carry_clear                   # Leave with Y non-zero to indicate edge of sloping cavern
; skip_sloping_cavern
&1995 a5 95    LDA &95 ; tile_x
&1997 4a       LSR A                                                # 84218421 -> .8421842 1
&1998 4a       LSR A                                                #          -> ..842184 2
&1999 4a       LSR A                                                #          -> ...84218 4
&199a 4a       LSR A                                                #          -> ....8421 8
&199b b0 29    BCS &19c6 ; leave                                    # Break sloping passages every eight tiles
&199d a9 01    LDA #&01                                             # Use a function of x and y to determine where to add / sloping passages
&199f 65 95    ADC &95 ; tile_x
&19a1 65 97    ADC &97 ; tile_y
&19a3 29 8f    AND #&8f
&19a5 c9 01    CMP #&01
&19a7 f0 1e    BEQ &19c7 ; leave_with_zero_Y                        # Leave with Y zero to indicate middle of passage
&19a9 aa       TAX                                                  # Use a function of x and y to determine where to add \ sloping passages
&19aa 38       SEC
&19ab a5 97    LDA &97 ; tile_y
&19ad e5 95    SBC &95 ; tile_x
&19af 29 2f    AND #&2f
&19b1 c9 01    CMP #&01
&19b3 f0 12    BEQ &19c7 ; leave_with_zero_Y                        # Leave with Y zero to indicate middle of passage
&19b5 a0 02    LDY #&02
&19b7 c9 02    CMP #&02
&19b9 f0 0e    BEQ &19c9 ; leave_with_carry_clear                   # Leave with Y non-zero to indicate edge of passage
&19bb c8       INY ; 3
&19bc 90 0b    BCC &19c9 ; leave_with_carry_clear                   # Leave with Y non-zero to indicate edge of passage
&19be c8       INY ; 4
&19bf e0 02    CPX #&02
&19c1 f0 06    BEQ &19c9 ; leave_with_carry_clear                   # Leave with Y non-zero to indicate edge of passage
&19c3 c8       INY ; 5
&19c4 90 03    BCC &19c9 ; leave_with_carry_clear                   # Leave with Y non-zero to indicate edge of passage
; leave                                                             # Leave with carry set to indicate solid tile
&19c6 60       RTS
; leave_with_zero_Y
&19c7 a0 00    LDY #&00                                             # Leave with zero to indicate empty tile

; apply_acceleration_to_velocities
&1f34 a2 02    LDX #&02
; apply_acceleration_to_velocities_loop                             # Loop through X = 2 for y, X = 0 for x
&1f36 e0 02    CPX #&02                                             # Set carry if y, clear carry if x
;                                                                   # i.e. add one to y acceleration for gravity
&1f38 b5 40    LDA &40,X ; this_object_acceleration_x
&1f3a 08       PHP ; acceleration sign
&1f3b 75 43    ADC &43,X ; this_object_velocity_x
&1f3d 20 e6 32 JSR &32e6 ; prevent_overflow
&1f40 a8       TAY ; new velocity
&1f41 28       PLP ; acceleration sign
&1f42 20 bd 32 JSR &32bd ; invert_if_negative
&1f45 e9 3f    SBC #&3f
&1f47 c9 40    CMP #&40
&1f49 b0 12    BCS &1f5d ; skip_limiting_velocity
&1f4b b4 43    LDY &43,X ; this_object_velocity_x

&1f4e 20 bd 32 JSR &32bd ; invert_if_negative
&1f51 c9 40    CMP #&40
&1f53 b0 08    BCS &1f5d ; skip_limiting_velocity
&1f55 a9 40    LDA #&40                                             # Limit velocity to +/- &40
&1f57 c0 00    CPY #&00                                             # Keep sign of velocity
&1f59 20 bd 32 JSR &32bd ; invert_if_negative
&1f5c a8       TAY ; new velocity
; skip_limiting_velocity
&1f5d 24 c3    BIT &c3 ; every_sixteen_frames
&1f5f 10 08    BPL &1f69 ; skip_applying_inertia
&1f61 98       TYA ; new velocity
&1f62 f0 05    BEQ &1f69 ; skip_applying_inertia                    # If the velocity is not zero,
&1f64 10 02    BPL &1f68 ; apply_inertia_to_positive_velocities     # reduce its absolute value by one to apply inertia
; apply_inertia_to_negative_velocities
&1f66 c8       INY
&1f67 c9 88    CMP #&88 ; (nop)
; apply_intertia_to_positive_velocities
#1f68          DEY
; skip_applying_inertia
&1f69 94 43    STY &43,X ; this_object_velocity_x
&1f6b ca       DEX
&1f6c ca       DEX
&1f6d 10 c7    BPL &1f36 ; apply_acceleration_to_velocities_loop
&1f6f 60       RTS

; plot_pixel
&200e a5 93    LDA &93 ; pixel_y_fraction                           # Convert pixel_y into row on screen
&2010 46 94    LSR &94 ; pixel_y
&2012 6a       ROR A
&2013 46 94    LSR &94 ; pixel_y
&2015 6a       ROR A
&2016 46 94    LSR &94 ; pixel_y
&2018 6a       ROR A
&2019 aa       TAX                                                  # X = screen row
&201a 29 07    AND #&07
&201c a8       TAY                                                  # Y = row in group
&201d a5 91    LDA &91 ; pixel_x_fraction
&201f 29 e0    AND #&e0                                             # Round pixel_x to &20 fraction (one byte)
&2021 65 b2    ADC &b2 ; scaled_screen_start_offset_low             # &20 fraction = 8 byte column of two pixels
&2023 85 8f    STA &8f ; screen_address_low                         # so divide by 4 (&20 / 8) to give screen address
&2025 8a       TXA
&2026 29 f8    AND #&f8                                             # hhh84218 -> hhh84... i.e. group
&2028 45 92    EOR &92 ; pixel_x
&202a 65 b3    ADC &b3 ; scaled_screen_start_offset_high
&202c 6a       ROR A                                                # hhh84... -> Chhh84..
&202d 66 8f    ROR &8f ; screen_address_low
&202f 4a       LSR A                                                #          -> .Chhh84.
&2030 66 8f    ROR &8f ; screen_address_low
&2032 a6 99    LDX &99 ; pixel_colour
; plot_pixel_without_fully_calculating_screen_address
&2034 09 40    ORA #&40 ; &4000 = screen_memory                 *** # Wrap around end of screen memory
&2036 85 90    STA &90 ; screen_address_high
; plot_pixel_without_calculating_screen_address
&2038 a5 91    LDA &91 ; pixel_x_fraction
&203a 29 10    AND #&10
&203c c9 10    CMP #&10
&203e a9 aa    LDA #&aa ; left pixel
&2040 90 01    BCC &2043 ; not_right_pixel
&2042 4a       LSR A                                                # Convert to right pixel
; not_right_pixel
&2043 3d 7b 1e AND &1e7b,X ; colour_3_pixel_values_table
&2046 51 8f    EOR (&8f),Y ; screen_address
&2048 91 8f    STA (&8f),Y ; screen_address
&204a 60       RTS

; plot_second_pixel

&204c 10 ea    BPL &2038 ; plot_pixel_without_calculating_screen_address

&204f a5 8f    LDA &8f ; screen_address_low
&2051 e9 f8    SBC #&f8                                             # Move up a group
&2053 85 8f    STA &8f ; screen_address_low
&2055 a5 90    LDA &90 ; screen_address_high
&2057 e9 01    SBC #&01
&2059 10 d9    BPL &2034 ; plot_pixel_without_fully_calculating_screen_address # Always branches

; plot_or_unplot_particle_using_colour_A

; plot_or_unplot_particle

&205e bd 07 29 LDA &2907,X ; particles_x_fraction
&2061 e5 c7    SBC &c7 ; screen_origin_x_fraction

&2068 e5 c8    SBC &c8 ; screen_origin_x

&206c b0 42    BCS &20b0 ; leave                                    # Leave with carry set if off left or right of screen
&206e 85 92    STA &92 ; pixel_x
&2070 bd 08 29 LDA &2908,X ; particles_y_fraction

&2077 a8       TAY ; pixel_y_fraction

&2081 c0 00    CPY #&00 ; pixel_y_fraction
&2083 d0 05    BNE &208a ; is_below_top_of_screen
; is_on_top_edge_of_screen
&2085 24 a3    BIT &a3 ; particle_colour_and_flags                  # &80 set if unplotting halves of double-heigh particles
&2087 30 21    BMI &20aa ; crosses_top_or_bottom_edge_of_screen
&2089 60       RTS                                                  # Leave with carry set if offscreen
; is_below_top_of_screen

&208c 90 08    BCC &2096 ; is_above_bottom_of_screen
&208e d0 20    BNE &20b0 ; leave

&2092 f0 0c    BEQ &20a0 ; is_just_below_bottom_edge_of_screen
&2094 b0 1a    BCS &20b0 ; leave
; is_above_bottom_of_screen
&2096 84 93    STY &93 ; pixel_y_fraction

&209b 24 a3    BIT &a3 ; particle_colour_and_flags                  # &40 set if particle is double height
&209d 70 ac    BVS &204b ; plot_second_pixel
&209f 60       RTS
; is_just_below_bottom_edge_of_screen
&20a0 24 a3    BIT &a3 ; particle_colour_and_flags

&20a4 10 0a    BPL &20b0 ; leave                                    # &80 set if unplotting halves of double-heigh particles
&20a6 c6 94    DEC &94 ; pixel_y
&20a8 a0 f8    LDY #&f8
; crosses_top_or_bottom_edge_of_screen
&20aa 84 93    STY &93 ; pixel_y_fraction
