&32c4 60       RTS

; keep_with_range                                                   # Called with A = centre value, Y = range
&32c5 84 9c    STY &9c ; range
&32c7 85 9d    STA &9d ; value
&32c9 20 bb 32 JSR &32bb ; make_positive
&32cc c5 9c    CMP &9c ; range
&32ce 90 06    BCC &32d6 ; leave_with_value                         # Leave with value if -range < value < range
&32d0 98       TYA ; range
&32d1 24 9d    BIT &9d ; value

; throwing_velocities_by_weight_table
;      0  1  2  3  4  5  6
&3339 20 20 20 20 20 10 08                                          # Very heavy objects can't be thrown as far

&3358 20 8a 23 JSR &238a ; calculate_vector_from_magnitude_and_angle

; skip_adding_y_velocity

&3372 9d e6 08 STA &08e6,X ; objects_velocity_x
&3375 60       RTS

; calculate_firing_vector_from_aiming_angle

&337f 69 40    ADC #&40 ; magnitude
&3381 20 8a 23 JSR &238a ; calculate_vector_from_magnitude_and_angle
; calculate_firing_vector_from_this_object_velocity

&3391 90 10    BCC &33a3 ; skip_ceiling
&3393 a5 43    LDA &43 ; this_object_velocity_x
&3395 20 bd 32 JSR &32bd ; invert_if_negative
&3398 69 20    ADC #&20                                             # use the largest of (object x speed + &20) and &50
&339a 20 e6 32 JSR &32e6 ; prevent_overflow

&339f b0 02    BCS &33a3 ; skip_ceiling

&33a7 85 b4    STA &b4 ; vector_x
&33a9 60       RTS

; use_vector_between_object_centres_scaled_by_firing_velocity

&33ac 4a       LSR A
&33ad 4a       LSR A
; use_vector_between_object_centres                                 # Called with A = magnitude
&33ae 85 a2    STA &a2 ; firing_velocity
&33b0 20 d3 22 JSR &22d3 ; calculate_angle_of_object_X_to_this_object
&33b3 a5 b7    LDA &b7 ; magnitude
&33b5 85 84    STA &84 ; distance_fraction
&33b7 a5 a2    LDA &a2 ; firing_velocity
&33b9 4c 8a 23 JMP &238a ; calculate_vector_from_magnitude_and_angle

; calculate_firing_vector_from_distance                             # Called with A = firing_velocity_times_four
;                                                                   #             X = target object
;                                                                   # Start by calculating vector to target
&33bc 20 aa 33 JSR &33aa ; use_vector_between_object_centres_scaled_by_firing_velocity
&33bf a5 b8    LDA &b8 ; relative_tiles_log
&33c1 c9 06    CMP #&06                                             # If objects are 16 or more tiles apart in either x or y,
&33c3 b0 46    BCS &340b ; leave                                    # Leave with carry set to indicate couldn't calculate
&33c5 46 a2    LSR &a2 ; firing_velocity
&33c7 46 a2    LSR &a2 ; firing_velocity
&33c9 06 84    ASL &84 ; distance_fraction                          # This is the (approximate) magnitude of the vector
&33cb a9 00    LDA #&00
&33cd a0 08    LDY #&08
&33cf 06 84    ASL &84 ; distance_fraction
; division_loop                                                     # Divide (4 * magnitude) / (firing_velocity / 4)
&33d1 2a       ROL A ; distance                                     # i.e. ~16 * magnitude / firing_velocity
&33d2 c5 a2    CMP &a2 ; firing_velocity
&33d4 90 02    BCC &33d8 ; skip_subtraction
&33d6 e5 a2    SBC &a2 ; firing_velocity
; skip_subtraction
&33d8 26 84    ROL &84 ; distance_fraction                          # Store result progressively in low bits of distance_fraction
&33da 88       DEY
&33db d0 f4    BNE &33d1 ; division_loop
; scale_result
&33dd a5 b8    LDA &b8 ; relative_tiles_log
&33df 18       CLC
&33e0 69 04    ADC #&04 ; * 16                                      # Multiply by another 16
&33e2 a8       TAY
&33e3 a9 00    LDA #&00
; scaling_loop                                                      # then scale by the approximate distance in tiles
&33e5 06 84    ASL &84 ; distance_fraction
&33e7 2a       ROL A
&33e8 88       DEY
&33e9 d0 fa    BNE &33e5 ; scaling_loop
; apply_upwards_velocity_depending_on_distance
&33eb 49 ff    EOR #&ff                                             # Invert high byte of result to add upwards velocity
&33ed 38       SEC                                                  # to account for the projectile falling over distance
&33ee 65 b6    ADC &b6 ; vector_y                                   # Add more upwards velocity the further away target is
&33f0 38       SEC
&33f1 70 18    BVS &340b ; leave                                    # Leave with carry set to indicate couldn't calculate
&33f3 85 b6    STA &b6 ; vector_y                                   # Set firing vector y from angle and distance
&33f5 20 bd 32 JSR &32bd ; invert_if_negative
&33f8 a8       TAY ; absolute vector_y
&33f9 bd e6 08 LDA &08e6,X ; objects_velocity_x
&33fc 65 b4    ADC &b4 ; vector_x
&33fe 20 e6 32 JSR &32e6 ; prevent_overflow
&3401 85 b4    STA &b4 ; vector_x                                   # Set firing vector x from angle and target x velocity
&3403 20 bd 32 JSR &32bd ; invert_if_negative
&3406 20 28 3c JSR &3c28 ; get_maximum_of_A_and_Y
&3409 c5 a3    CMP &a3 ; firing_velocity_times_four                 # Leave with carry set if that would mean firing too fast
; leave
&340b 60       RTS                                                  # Leave with carry clear if vector calculated

; create_lightning

; create_child_object

&3424 38       SEC

&3427 98       TYA
&3428 aa       TAX                                                  # X = child object slot
&3429 a5 39    LDA &39 ; this_object_y_flip                         # &80 set if vertically flipped
&342b 29 80    AND #&80
&342d 4a       LSR A                                                # Set &40 if vertically flipped
;                                                                   # i.e. child inherits vertical flip of parent

&3430 99 c6 08 STA &08c6,Y ; objects_flags
&3433 20 96 34 JSR &3496 ; set_object_X_velocities_from_vector
&3436 bc 70 08 LDY &0870,X ; objects_sprite                         # Y = sprite
&3439 38       SEC
&343a a5 3c    LDA &3c  this_object_height
&343c f9 89 be SBC &be89,Y ; sprites_height_and_vertical_flip_table
&343f 4a       LSR A
&3440 65 51    ADC &51 ; this_object_y_fraction                     # Align child object with vertical centre of parent
&3442 9d a3 08 STA &08a3,X ; objects_y_fraction
&3445 a5 55    LDA &55 ; this_object_y
&3447 69 00    ADC #&00
&3449 9d b4 08 STA &08b4,X ; objects_y
&344c a5 43    LDA &43 ; this_object_velocity_x                     # parent x velocity - child x velocity
&344e 38       SEC
&344f e5 b4    SBC &b4 ; vector_x
&3451 20 e6 32 JSR &32e6 ; prevent_overflow

&3456 45 43    EOR &43 ; this_object_velocity_x
&3458 18       CLC
&3459 08       PHP ; negative if parent and child moving in same direction in x
&345a a5 9d    LDA &9d ; relative_velocity_x

; is_moving_right_relative_to_parent
&345e a5 3a    LDA &3a ; this_object_width                          # Object width minus one pixel
&3460 69 18    ADC #&18                                             # Add half pixel gap
&3462 a0 01    LDY #&01                                             # Overflow to next tile
&3464 d0 07    BNE &346d ; consider_overflow_or_underflow           # Always branches
; is_moving_left_relative_to_parent
&3466 a9 e9    LDA #&e9 ; - &17 (carry is clear, so actually - &18) # Subtract half pixel gap, and width of child
&3468 f9 0c be SBC &be0c,Y ; sprites_width_and_horizontal_flip_table
&346b a0 ff    LDY #&ff                                             # Underflow to previous tile
; consider_overflow_or_underflow
&346d b0 01    BCS &3470 ; skip_overflow_or_underflow
&346f 88       DEY
; skip_overflow_or_underflow
&3470 18       CLC
&3471 65 4f    ADC &4f ; this_object_x_fraction

&3476 65 53    ADC &53 ; this_object_x
&3478 a8       TAY
&3479 a5 9d    LDA &9d ; relative_velocity_x

; is_moving_in_opposite_direction
&347e a9 01    LDA #&01
&3480 e5 b4    SBC &b4 ; vector_x
; is_moving_in_same_direction

&3484 10 01    BPL &3487 ; skip_overflow
&3486 88       DEY
; skip_underflow
&3487 18       CLC
&3488 65 9c    ADC &9c ; child_x_fraction                           # Move child away from parent
&348a 9d 80 08 STA &0880,X ; objects_x_fraction

&348f c8       INY
; skip_overflow

&3495 60       RTS                                                  #            X = child object slot

; accelerate_all_objects                                            # Accelerate all object to / away from this one
&34a1 a9 ff    LDA #&ff
; accelerate_all_objects_within_angle                               # Called with A = angle range, &b5 = centre angle
&34a3 85 a1    STA &a1 ; angle_range
&34a5 a5 b5    LDA &b5 ; angle
&34a7 85 a0    STA &a0 ; centre_angle
&34a9 a2 0f    LDX #&0f
; accelerate_all_objects_loop                                       # For each object,
&34ab e4 aa    CPX &aa ; this_object
&34ad f0 5c    BEQ &350b ; consider_next_object                     # Don't affect the source
&34af a5 35    LDA &35 ; acceleration_power                         # Used as distance, in &20 fractions
&34b1 20 03 36 JSR &3603 ; check_for_obstruction_between_objects_A  # Returns carry set if no line of sight
;                                                                   # also calculates angle and distance
&34b4 b0 55    BCS &350b ; consider_next_object
&34b6 a5 b5    LDA &b5 ; angle
&34b8 45 29    EOR &29 ; acceleration_sign                          # &80 set if accelerating towards source
&34ba 85 b5    STA &b5 ; angle
&34bc e5 a0    SBC &a0 ; centre_angle
&34be c5 a1    CMP &a1 ; angle_range
&34c0 90 06    BCC &34c8 ; is_within_angle_range
&34c2 49 ff    EOR #&ff
&34c4 c5 a1    CMP &a1 ; angle_range
&34c6 b0 43    BCS &350b ; consider_next_object                     # Is the target within the angle range?
; is_within_angle_range
&34c8 bc 60 08 LDY &0860,X ; objects_type
&34cb b9 54 03 LDA &0354,Y ; object_types_flags_table
&34ce 29 07    AND #&07 ; OBJECT_TYPE_FLAG_WEIGHT_MASK
&34d0 c9 07    CMP #&07
&34d2 66 24    ROR &24 ; static                                     # Set &80 if target is static
&34d4 0a       ASL A                                                # Heavier objects are accelerated less
&34d5 69 08    ADC #&08 ; 1 tile
&34d7 65 83    ADC &83 ; distance
&34d9 e5 35    SBC &35 ; acceleration_power
&34db 49 ff    EOR #&ff                                             # power - (target weight * 2 + 8 + distance)
&34dd b0 2c    BCS &350b ; consider_next_object                     # Branch if target is too distant for its weight, or static
&34df 24 28    BIT &28 ; acceleration_damages_targets               # &80 set to damage targets
&34e1 10 0e    BPL &34f1 ; skip_damaging_target
&34e3 c9 04    CMP #&04
&34e5 90 0a    BCC &34f1 ; skip_damaging_target                     # Light acceleration doesn't damage target
&34e7 86 9d    STX &9d ; target
&34e9 a4 9d    LDY &9d ; target
&34eb 48       PHA ; acceleration * 2
&34ec 0a       ASL A
&34ed 20 d5 24 JSR &24d5 ; damage_object                            # Damage target by acceleration
&34f0 68       PLA ; acceleration * 2
; skip_damaging_target
&34f1 24 24    BIT &24  static                                      # &80 if target is static
&34f3 30 16    BMI &350b ; consider_next_object                     # Static targets aren't accelerated
&34f5 4a       LSR A                                                # acceleration = power - (weight_distance) / 2
&34f6 20 8a 23 JSR &238a ; calculate_vector_from_magnitude_and_angle # Returns A = vector_y
&34f9 7d f6 08 ADC &08f6,X ; objects_velocity_y
&34fc 70 03    BVS &3501 ; skip_accelerating_y                      # Apply to target velocity unless that overflows
&34fe 9d f6 08 STA &08f6,X ; objects_velocity_y
; skip_accelerating_y
&3501 a5 b4    LDA &b4 ; vector_x
&3503 7d e6 08 ADC &08e6,X ; objects_velocity_x
&3506 70 03    BVS &350b ; skip_accelerating_x
&3508 9d e6 08 STA &08e6,X ; objects_velocity_x
; skip_accelerating_x
; consider_next_object
&350b ca       DEX
&350c 10 9d    BPL &34ab ; accelerate_all_objects_loop
&350e 46 28    LSR &28 ; acceleration_damages_targets               # Clear &80 to not damage targets by default
&3510 a9 28    LDA #&28 ; 5 tiles
&3512 85 35    STA &35 ; acceleration_power                         # Set default acceleration power
&3514 e8       INX ; 0
&3515 86 29    STX &29 ; acceleration_sign                          # Set to positive to accelerate away from source
&3517 60       RTS

&351d 8d 42 35 STA &3542 ; player_pockets_used

&3523 30 6f    BMI &3594 ; leave

&352d b0 65    BCS &3594 ; leave                                    # Leave with carry set to indicate object still held
&352f b9 60 08 LDA &0860,Y ; objects_type

; not_power_pod

#     actually CPX number_of_pockets_to_use

&3547 48       PHA
; shuffle_pockets_loop                                              # Make space in first pocket for stored object

; leave_with_carry_clear

; gain_energy_and_flash_if_damaged
&359f a0 1e    LDY #&1e                                             # Give object a minimum energy of 30
; gain_energy_Y_and_flash_if_damaged

; update_player_angle_facing_and_sprite
&37fc 20 c3 2c JSR &2cc3 ; double_accelerations

; is_overflow
&380b a2 00    LDX #&00 ; jetpack
&380d 20 c1 2d JSR &2dc1 ; check_reliability                        # Reliability of jetpack is tested

# Copy protection check
# =====================
# Pass if:
# permuted_seed ^ expected_word_obfuscated_second_checksum ^ entered_word_second_checksum ^ permuted_rnd_pair = 0
#
# Given:
#     word_number = permuted_seed ^ rnd_pair, so permuted_seed = word_number ^ rnd_pair
#     permuted_seed < &80, so top bit of word_number = top bit of rnd_pair
#     expected_word_obfuscated_second_checksum = expected_word_second_checksum ^ (word_number & &7f) ^ &65
#     permuted_rnd_pair = (rnd_pair & &7f) ^ &65
#
# Pass if:
# word_number ^ rnd_pair ^ expected_word_second_checksum ^ (word_number & &7f) ^ &65 ^ entered_word_second_checksum ^ (rnd_pair & &7f) ^ &65 = 0
# (word_number ^ (word_number & &7f)) ^ (rnd_pair ^ (rnd_pair & &7f)) ^ (&65 ^ &65) ^ expected_word_second_checksum ^ entered_word_second_checksum = 0
# (top bit of word_number) ^ (top bit of rnd_pair) ^ expected_word_second_checksum ^ entered_word_second_checksum = 0
# expected_word_second_checksum ^ entered_word_second_checksum = 0
#
# i.e. if entered word is expected word

; check_copy_protection
&39b5 a0 44    LDY #&44
&39b7 a2 02    LDX #&02
&39b9 20 a5 31 JSR &31a5 ; permute_copy_protection_seed             # Returns A = permuted_seed
; check_copy_protection_loop                                        # Loop through X = &02, Y = &44, &0ba7 ; copy_protection_first_byte
;                                                                   #                       expected_word_obfuscated_second_checksum
&39bc 59 63 0b EOR &0b63,Y ; copy_protection_first_byte - &44       #              X = &01, Y = &45, &0ba8 ; copy_protection_second_byte
;                                                                   #                       entered_word_second_checksum
&39bf c8       INY
&39c0 ca       DEX
&39c1 d0 f9    BNE &39bc ; check_copy_protection_loop               
&39c3 59 e7 07 EOR &07e7,Y ; copy_protection_third_byte - &46       # permuted_rnd_pair
&39c6 d0 ed    BNE &39b5 ; check_copy_protection                    # Infinite loop if values not as expected
&39c8 60       RTS

# NPC walking types
# =================
# &00 : player
# &01 : frogmen
# &02 : imps, fluffy
# &03 : green slime
# &04 : robots
# &05 : (unused)

; find_nearest_object_probabilities_table                           # Probabilities of considering object as find

&3c9e 8d 8d 3c STA &3c8d ; primary_type
&3ca1 8c 8e 3c STY &3c8e ; secondary_type_or_range
&3ca4 a2 ff    LDX #&ff
&3ca6 8e 8c 3c STX &3c8c ; nearest_object                           # Set to negative to indicate no object by default
&3ca9 8e 8f 3c STX &3c8f ; nearest_object_distance
&3cac e8       INX ; 0
&3cad 86 a0    STX &a0 ; finds
&3caf 86 9f    STX &9f ; count
&3cb1 20 b6 25 JSR &25b6 ; rnd
&3cb4 29 0f    AND #&0f
&3cb6 85 a3    STA &a3 ; random_shuffle
&3cb8 a0 0f    LDY #&0f
; find_or_count_objects_loop                                        # For each object, shuffled randomly,
&3cba 84 9e    STY &9e ; object_to_check
&3cbc 98       TYA
&3cbd 45 a3    EOR &a3 ; random_shuffle
&3cbf a8       TAY
&3cc0 b9 b4 08 LDA &08b4,Y ; objects_y                              # Zero if no object in slot
&3cc3 f0 6b    BEQ &3d30 ; consider_next_object
&3cc5 c4 aa    CPY &aa ; this_object
&3cc7 f0 67    BEQ &3d30 ; consider_next_object                     # Don't consider this object
&3cc9 b9 60 08 LDA &0860,Y ; objects_type
&3ccc d0 05    BNE &3cd3 ; not_player
; is_player
&3cce 2c 90 3c BIT &3c90 ; primary_type_includes_player             # Negative to include player
&3cd1 30 05    BMI &3cd8 ; is_suitable_type
; not_player
&3cd3 cd 8d 3c CMP &3c8d ; primary_type
&3cd6 d0 06    BNE &3cde ; not_suitable_type
; is_suitable_type
&3cd8 a5 a0    LDA &a0 ; finds
&3cda 09 01    ORA #&01                                             # Set &01 to indicate primary type found
&3cdc d0 14    BNE &3cf2 ; set_finds                                # Always branches
; not_suitable_type
&3cde 2c 8e 3c BIT &3c8e ; secondary_type_or_range                  # Negative to check object ranges
&3ce1 10 06    BPL &3ce9 ; skip_checking_range
&3ce3 20 df 2d JSR &2ddf ; get_range_for_object_type_A              # Returns X = range
&3ce6 8a       TXA
&3ce7 09 80    ORA #&80                                             # Set &80 to consider range
; skip_checking_range
&3ce9 cd 8e 3c CMP &3c8e ; secondary_type_or_range
&3cec d0 42    BNE &3d30 ; consider_next_object
&3cee a5 a0    LDA &a0 ; finds
&3cf0 29 02    AND #&02                                             # Clear &01 to indicate secondary type found
; set_finds
&3cf2 29 03    AND #&03
&3cf4 85 a0    STA &a0 ; finds
&3cf6 aa       TAX
&3cf7 e6 9f    INC &9f ; count
&3cf9 24 a1    BIT &a1 ; finding_or_counting                        # Top bit set if counting objects
&3cfb 30 33    BMI &3d30 ; consider_next_object                     # If so, consider all objects
; is_finding                                                        # A matching object has been found
&3cfd 20 b6 25 JSR &25b6 ; rnd
&3d00 dd 88 3c CMP &3c88,X ; find_nearest_object_probabilities_table
&3d03 b0 2b    BCS &3d30 ; consider_next_object
&3d05 98       TYA
&3d06 aa       TAX
&3d07 24 9b    BIT &9b ; finding_mode                               # Negative if ignoring obstructions
&3d09 10 0e    BPL &3d19 ; is_considering_obstructions
; is_ignoring_obstructions
&3d0b a9 00    LDA #&00                                             # Zero to not check for obstructions
&3d0d 20 03 36 JSR &3603 ; check_for_obstruction_between_objects    # Determine distance to object
&3d10 a5 83    LDA &83 ; distance
&3d12 cd 8f 3c CMP &3c8f ; nearest_object_distance                  # Is this object nearer than the previous find?
&3d15 90 11    BCC &3d28 ; use_as_nearest_object
&3d17 b0 17    BCS &3d30 ; consider_next_object                     # Always branches
; is_considering_obstructions
&3d19 20 b6 25 JSR &25b6 ; rnd
&3d1c 29 4f    AND #&4f                                             # Use random distance up to 10 tiles
&3d1e 4d 8f 3c EOR &3c8f ; nearest_object_distance
&3d21 20 03 36 JSR &3603 ; check_for_obstruction_between_objects    # Returns carry clear if no obstructions within distance
&3d24 b0 0a    BCS &3d30 ; consider_next_object
&3d26 a5 83    LDA &83 ; distance
; use_as_nearest_object
&3d28 8d 8f 3c STA &3c8f ; nearest_object_distance
&3d2b 06 a0    ASL &a0 ; finds                                      # Carry is clear here; ......21 -> .....210
&3d2d 8e 8c 3c STX &3c8c ; nearest_object
; consider_next_object
&3d30 a4 9e    LDY &9e ; object_to_check
&3d32 88       DEY
&3d33 10 85    BPL &3cba ; find_or_count_objects_loop
&3d35 46 a0    LSR &a0 ; finds                                      # ......21 -> .......2 1
&3d37 46 a0    LSR &a0 ; finds                                      #          -> ........ 2
&3d39 ae 8c 3c LDX &3c8c ; nearest_object                           # Leave with carry set if last find was primary type 
&3d3c 60       RTS                                                  # Leave with X = nearest object

; check_if_object_is_at_target
&3d3d a5 16    LDA &16 ; this_object_ty
&3d3f c5 55    CMP &55 ; this_object_y
&3d41 d0 04    BNE &3d47 ; leave                                    # Leave with not equal if object not at target
&3d43 a5 14    LDA &14 ; this_object_tx
&3d45 c5 53    CMP &53 ; this_object_x
; leave
&3d47 60       RTS                                                  # Leave with equal if object at target

; route_angle_randomness
&3d48 00

; route_angle_base
&3d49 00

; route_best_distance
&3d4a 00

; route_best_angle
&3d4b 00

; route_attempts_remaining
&3d4c 00

; check_if_object_has_target
&3d4d a6 0e    LDX &0e ; this_object_target_object
&3d4f bd b4 08 LDA &08b4,X ; objects_y                              # Zero if no object in slot
&3d52 d0 06    BNE &3d5a ; is_present
; not_present                                                       # If the target is no longer present,
&3d54 a6 aa    LDX &aa ; this_object
&3d56 86 0e    STX &0e ; this_object_target_object                  # set this object's target to be itself, i.e. no target
&3d58 86 3e    STX &3e ; this_object_target_object_and_flags
; is_present
&3d5a e4 aa    CPX &aa ; this_object                                # Leave with not equal if object has target
&3d5c 60       RTS

; consider_if_npc_can_see_target
&3d5d a5 07    LDA &07 ; this_object_frame_counter_sixteen          # Every sixteen frames,
&3d5f d0 2b    BNE &3d8c ; leave
&3d61 20 4d 3d JSR &3d4d ; check_if_object_has_target               # Returns not equal if object has target
&3d64 f0 37    BEQ &3d9d ; reduce_targeting_directness
&3d66 20 01 36 JSR &3601 ; check_for_obstruction_between_objects_80 # Returns carry set if no line of sight
&3d69 b0 19    BCS &3d84 ; is_unable_to_see_target
; is_able_to_see_target                                             # If NPC has target and it can see it,
&3d6b a5 3e    LDA &3e ; this_object_target_object_and_flags        # set maximum directness to head there directly
&3d6d 09 c0    ORA #&c0 ; TARGET_FLAG_DIRECTNESS_TWO | TARGET_FLAG_DIRECTNESS_ONE
&3d6f 85 3e    STA &3e ; this_object_target_object_and_flags
&3d71 29 20    AND #&20 ; TARGET_FLAG_AVOIDING
&3d73 d0 03    BNE &3d78 ; is_avoiding
&3d75 4c b2 28 JMP &28b2 ; set_this_object_tx_ty_from_object_X_x_y  # Use target object to set target position
; is_avoiding                                                       # unless avoiding,
&3d78 20 d3 22 JSR &22d3 ; calculate_angle_of_object_X_to_this_object
&3d7b 49 80    EOR #&80 ; 180 degrees                               # in which case, head in opposite direction
&3d7d 85 b5    STA &b5 ; angle
&3d7f a9 7f    LDA #&7f ; ~180 degrees
&3d81 4c 0e 3e JMP &3e0e ; find_route_to_target_with_angle_range_A
; is_unable_to_see_target
&3d84 a5 3e    LDA &3e ; this_object_target_object_and_flags        # If TARGET_FLAG_DIRECTNESS_TWO set,
&3d86 10 04    BPL &3d8c ; leave
&3d88 29 bf    AND #&bf ; !TARGET_FLAG_DIRECTNESS_ONE               # clear TARGET_FLAG_DIRECTNESS_ONE
&3d8a 85 3e    STA &3e ; this_object_target_object_and_flags        # i.e. drop from directness level three to two
; leave
&3d8c 60       RTS

; consider_updating_npc_path
&3d8d 20 5d 3d JSR &3d5d ; consider_if_npc_can_see_target           # Set directness flags depending on obstruction
&3d90 24 3e    BIT &3e ; this_object_target_object_and_flags
&3d92 30 04    BMI &3d98 ; use_direct_path                          # Use direct path if directness levels two or three
&3d94 50 39    BVC &3dcf ; use_relaxed_path                         # Use relaxed path if level zero
&3d96 70 0f    BVS &3da7 ; use_slightly_relaxed_path                # Use slightly relaxed path if level one; always branches

; use_direct_path                                                   # If directness levels two or three,
&3d98 20 ff 3d JSR &3dff ; check_if_npc_path_update_needed_ignoring_collisions # Returns zero every 8 or 64 frames
&3d9b d0 09    BNE &3da6 ; leave                                    # Otherwise, continue along path towards target
; reduce_targeting_directness
&3d9d 38       SEC
&3d9e a5 3e    LDA &3e ; this_object_target_object_and_flags
&3da0 e9 40    SBC #&40 ; TARGET_FLAG_DIRECTNESS_ONE                # Reduce directness by a level
&3da2 90 02    BCC &3da6 ; leave
&3da4 85 3e    STA &3e ; this_object_target_object_and_flags
; leave
&3da6 60       RTS

; use_slightly_relaxed_path                                         # If directness level one,
&3da7 20 b6 25 JSR &25b6 ; rnd
&3daa 29 03    AND #&03
&3dac f0 21    BEQ &3dcf ; use_relaxed_path                         # 1 in 4 chance of using relaxed path
&3dae 4a       LSR A
&3daf 05 da    ORA &da ; rnd_state + 1
&3db1 f0 ea    BEQ &3d9d ; lessen_targeting_enthusiasm              # 1 in 512 chance of dropping to level zero
&3db3 20 fb 3d JSR &3dfb ; check_if_npc_path_update_needed          # Returns zero every 8 or 64 frames
&3db6 d0 53    BNE &3e0b ; leave
&3db8 a6 0e    LDX &0e ; this_object_target_object
&3dba a9 20    LDA #&20 ; magnitude
&3dbc 20 ae 33 JSR &33ae ; use_vector_between_object_centres
&3dbf a5 3e    LDA &3e ; this_object_target_object_and_flags
&3dc1 29 20    AND #&20 ; TARGET_FLAG_AVOIDING
&3dc3 f0 06    BEQ &3dcb ; not_avoiding                             # Otherwise use a direct path towards target
; is_avoiding
&3dc5 a5 b5    LDA &b5 ; angle
&3dc7 49 80    EOR #&80 ; 180 degrees                               # or away from target, if avoiding
&3dc9 85 b5    STA &b5 ; angle
; not_avoiding
&3dcb a9 3f    LDA #&3f ; 90 degrees
&3dcd d0 3f    BNE &3e0e ; find_route_to_target_with_angle_range_A  # Always branches

; use_relaxed_path                                                  # If directness level zero,
&3dcf 20 fb 3d JSR &3dfb ; check_if_npc_path_update_needed          # Returns zero every 8 or 64 frames
&3dd2 d0 37    BNE &3e0b ; leave
&3dd4 20 b6 25 JSR &25b6 ; rnd
&3dd7 29 07    AND #&07
&3dd9 e9 03    SBC #&03
&3ddb 65 43    ADC &43 ; this_object_velocity_x                     # Perturb x velocity slightly
&3ddd 85 b4    STA &b4 ; vector_x
&3ddf a5 d9    LDA &d9 ; rnd_state
&3de1 29 07    AND #&07
&3de3 e9 03    SBC #&03
&3de5 65 45    ADC &45 ; this_object_velocity_y                     # Perturb y velocity slightly
&3de7 20 05 23 JSR &2305 ; calculate_angle_from_vector_x_and_A      # Use perturbed velocities to generate an angle
&3dea a9 ff    LDA #&ff ; ~360 degrees
&3dec a6 da    LDX &da ; rnd_state + 1
&3dee e0 08    CPX #&08                                             # 1 in 32 chance of using 360 degree range
&3df0 90 06    BCC &3df8 ; to_find_route_to_target_with_angle_range_A
&3df2 4a       LSR A ; &7f ; ~180 degrees                           # 7 in 32 chance of using 180 degree range
&3df3 e0 40    CPX #&40
&3df5 90 01    BCC &3df8 ; to_find_route_to_target_with_angle_range_A
&3df7 4a       LSR A ; &3f ; ~90 degrees                            # 3 in 4 chance of using 90 degree range
; to_find_route_to_target_with_angle_range_A
&3df8 4c 0e 3e JMP &3e0e ; find_route_to_target_with_angle_range_A

; check_if_npc_path_update_needed
&3dfb 24 1b    BIT &1b ; this_object_tile_top_or_bottom_collision   # &80 set if object hit tiles above or below
&3dfd 30 07    BMI &3e06 ; leave_with_zero_every_eight_frames       # Every 8 frames, if hit tiles above or below
; check_if_npc_path_update_needed_ignoring_collisions
&3dff a0 3f    LDY #&3f                                             # Every 64 frames, if not at target
&3e01 20 3d 3d JSR &3d3d ; check_if_object_is_at_target             # Returns equal if object is at target
&3e04 d0 02    BNE &3e08 ; not_at_target
; leave_with_zero_every_eight_frames
&3e06 a0 07    LDY #&07                                             # Every 8 frames, if at target
; not_at_target
&3e08 98       TYA
&3e09 24 06    BIT &06 ; this_object_frame_counter
; leave
&3e0b 60       RTS                                                  # Leave with zero if update needed

; find_route_to_target                                              # Unused entry point
&3e0c a9 ff    LDA #&ff
; find_route_to_target_with_angle_range_A                           # Called with A = range of angles to consider
&3e0e 8d 48 3d STA &3d48 ; route_angle_randomness
&3e11 4a       LSR A
&3e12 85 9d    STA &9d ; half_randomness

&3e16 e5 9d    SBC &9d ; half_randomness
&3e18 8d 49 3d STA &3d49 ; route_angle_base

&3e1d 8d 4c 3d STA &3d4c ; route_attempts_remaining
&3e20 8d 4a 3d STA &3d4a ; route_best_distance
; find_route_to_target_loop                                         # For each of four attempts,
&3e23 20 b6 25 JSR &25b6 ; rnd
&3e26 2d 48 3d AND &3d48 ; route_angle_randomness                   # Pick a random angle with the range
&3e29 6d 49 3d ADC &3d49 ; route_angle_base
&3e2c 85 b5    STA &b5 ; angle
&3e2e 48       PHA ; angle_to_check
&3e2f a9 20    LDA #&20 ; magnitude
&3e31 20 8a 23 JSR &238a ; calculate_vector_from_magnitude_and_angle
&3e34 20 b6 25 JSR &25b6 ; rnd
&3e37 29 1f    AND #&1f                                             # Use random distance for line of sight check
&3e39 69 10    ADC #&10 ; 16 * &20 fraction = 2 tiles               # between 2 and 6 tiles
&3e3b 4e ff 35 LSR &35ff ; waterline_blocks_line_of_sight           # Clear top bit to ignore waterline
&3e3e 20 3c 36 JSR &363c ; check_for_obstruction_along_vector       # Returns carry clear if line of sight
&3e41 68       PLA ; angle_to_check
&3e42 90 28    BCC &3e6c ; set_target                               # If no obstructions, use this angle
&3e44 a6 83    LDX &83 ; distance
&3e46 ec 4a 3d CPX &3d4a ; route_best_distance                      # Otherwise, is it unobstructed for longer?
&3e49 90 06    BCC &3e51 ; not_longer
&3e4b 8e 4a 3d STX &3d4a ; route_best_distance                      # If so, note as best so far
&3e4e 8d 4b 3d STA &3d4b ; route_best_angle
; not_longer
&3e51 ce 4c 3d DEC &3d4c ; route_attempts_remaining
&3e54 d0 cd    BNE &3e23 ; find_route_to_target_loop
&3e56 ad 4b 3d LDA &3d4b ; route_best_angle                         # Use the best angle found
&3e59 85 b5    STA &b5 ; angle
&3e5b a9 20    LDA #&20 ; magnitude
&3e5d 20 8a 23 JSR &238a ; calculate_vector_from_magnitude_and_angle
&3e60 ad 4a 3d LDA &3d4a ; route_best_distance
&3e63 c9 0a    CMP #&0a ; 10 * &20 fraction = 1.25 tiles            # provided it is long enough
&3e65 90 0d    BCC &3e74 ; leave
&3e67 e9 08    SBC #&08 ; 8 * &20 fraction = 1 tile                 # Stop one tile short of obstruction
&3e69 20 3c 36 JSR &363c ; check_for_obstruction_along_vector       # Calculate tile just short of obstruction
; set_target
; set_this_object_tx_ty_from_tile_x_y
&3e6c a5 95    LDA &95 ; tile_x
&3e6e 85 14    STA &14 ; this_object_tx
&3e70 a5 97    LDA &97 ; tile_y
&3e72 85 16    STA &16 ; this_object_ty

&3e74 60       RTS

; play_sample                                                   *** # Called with X = sample number
&99ec 08       PHP                                              ***
&99ed 98       TYA                                              ***
&99ee 48       PHA ; tmp_y                                      ***
&99ef ec 48 9a CPX &9a48 ; current_sample                       ***
&99f2 b0 4a    BCS &9a3e ; leave_after_restoring_Y              *** # Screams take priority over robots
&99f4 20 c0 35 JSR &35c0 ; get_object_distance_from_screen_centre *
&99f7 c9 20    CMP #&20                                         ***
&99f9 b0 43    BCS &9a3e ; leave_after_restoring_Y              ***
&99fb 4a       LSR A                                            ***
&99fc 09 90    ORA #&90 ; channel 3 volume                      *** # Set volume depending on object distance
&99fe 8d 46 9a STA &9a46 ; sample_volume_base_byte              ***
&9a01 8e 48 9a STX &9a48 ; current_sample                       ***
&9a04 8a       TXA                                              ***
&9a05 0a       ASL A                                            ***
&9a06 aa       TAX                                              ***
&9a07 78       SEI                                              ***
&9a08 a9 02    LDA #&02                                         *** # Reduce the number of channels available for sounds
&9a0a 8d 92 11 STA &1192 ; number_of_sound_channels             ***
&9a0d a9 00    LDA #&00                                         ***
&9a0f 8d c7 11 STA &11c7 ; sound_channels_volume_duration + 3   *** # Set to zero to stop any sound on channel 3
&9a12 18       CLC                                              ***
&9a13 bd 00 81 LDA &8100,X ; sample_address_offsets_table       ***
&9a16 69 00    ADC #&00                                         ***
&9a18 8d 5c 9a STA &9a5c ; sample_address_low                   ***
&9a1b bd 01 81 LDA &8101,X ; sample_address_offsets_table       ***
&9a1e 69 81    ADC #&81 ; &8100 = samples_base                  ***
&9a20 8d 5d 9a STA &9a5d ; sample_address_high                  ***
&9a23 a9 c0    LDA #&c0                                         *** # Enable User VIA timer 1 interrupts
&9a25 8d 6e fe STA &fe6e ; User VIA interrupt enable register   ***
&9a28 a9 81    LDA #&81                                         *** # Set low bits of frequency for channel 3 to 0001
&9a2a 20 f9 13 JSR &13f9 ; write_byte_to_sound_chip             ***
&9a2d a9 00    LDA #&00                                         *** # Set high bits of frequency for channel 3 to 000000
&9a2f 20 f9 13 JSR &13f9 ; write_byte_to_sound_chip             *** # Frequency played = 4000000 / (32 * value written)
&9a32 a2 01    LDX #&01                                         *** # i.e. use maximum possible frequency. Sample is played
&9a34 8e 64 fe STX &fe64 ; User VIA timer 1 counter LSB         *** # by alternating periods of this and periods of silence
&9a37 ca       DEX ; 0                                          ***
&9a38 8e 65 fe STX &fe65 ; User VIA timer 1 counter MSB         ***
&9a3b 20 5b 9a JSR &9a5b ; update_sample_from_current_byte      ***
; leave_after_restoring_Y                                       ***
&9a3e 68       PLA ; tmp_y                                      ***
&9a3f a8       TAY                                              ***
&9a40 28       PLP                                              ***
&9a41 60       RTS                                              ***

; sample_delay
&9a42 00                                                        ***

; sample_volume_byte
&9a43 00                                                        ***

; sample_byte
&9a44 00                                                        ***

; sample_address_nibble
&9a45 00                                                        ***

; sample_volume_base_byte
&9a46 00                                                        ***

; unused
&9a47 00                                                        ***

; current_sample
&9a48 ff                                                        ***

; update_sample                                                 ***
&9a49 2c d2 14 BIT &14d2 ; suppress_updating_sound              *** # Positive if game paused
&9a4c 10 0d    BPL &9a5b ; update_sample_from_current_byte      ***
&9a4e 2c 45 9a BIT &9a45 ; sample_address_nibble                *** # Positive if new byte of data needed
&9a51 30 28    BMI &9a7b ; use_low_nibble                       ***
; is_new_byte                                                   ***
&9a53 ee 5c 9a INC &9a5c ; sample_address_low                   ***
&9a56 d0 03    BNE &9a5b ; skip_page                            ***
&9a58 ee 5d 9a INC &9a5d ; sample_address_high                  ***
; skip_page                                                     ***
; update_sample_from_current_byte                               ***
&9a5b ad 00 81 LDA &8100                                        *** # Get byte of sample data
#     actually LDA sample_address                               ***
&9a5e f0 34    BEQ &9a94 ; end_of_sample                        *** # Zero indicates end of sample
&9a60 8d 44 9a STA &9a44 ; sample_byte                          ***
&9a63 29 0f    AND #&0f                                         ***
&9a65 c9 0f    CMP #&0f                                         *** # Set carry if low nibble is &f
&9a67 ad 44 9a LDA &9a44 ; sample_byte                          ***
&9a6a b0 0a    BCS &9a76 ; use_whole_byte_for_silence           *** # If so, use whole byte as length of silence
; use_high_nibble                                               ***
&9a6c 4a       LSR A                                            *** # Otherwise, use high nibble as length of silence
&9a6d 4a       LSR A                                            ***
&9a6e 4a       LSR A                                            ***
&9a6f 4a       LSR A                                            ***
&9a70 f0 09    BEQ &9a7b ; use_low_nibble_for_sound             *** # Unless zero, in which case skip silence
&9a72 38       SEC                                              ***
&9a73 6e 45 9a ROR &9a45 ; sample_address_nibble                *** # Set to negative to indicate new byte not needed next time
; use_whole_byte_for_silence                                    ***
&9a76 a0 9f    LDY #&9f ; channel 3 volume 0                    ***
&9a78 4c 86 9a JMP &9a86 ; set_sample_delay                     ***
; use_low_nibble_for_sound                                      ***
&9a7b ad 44 9a LDA &9a44 ; sample_byte                          *** # Use low nibble as length of sound
&9a7e 29 0f    AND #&0f                                         ***
&9a80 4e 45 9a LSR &9a45 ; sample_address_nibble                *** # Set to positive to indicate new byte needed next time
&9a83 ac 46 9a LDY &9a46 ; sample_volume_base_byte              ***
; set_sample_delay                                              ***
&9a86 8d 42 9a STA &9a42 ; sample_delay                         ***
&9a89 2c 8a 12 BIT &128a ; sound_enabled                        *** # Negative if sound enabled
&9a8c 30 02    BMI &9a90 ; not_muted                            ***
&9a8e a0 9f    LDY #&9f ; channel 3 volume 0                    ***
; not_muted                                                     ***
&9a90 8c 43 9a STY &9a43 ; sample_volume_byte                   ***
&9a93 60       RTS                                              ***
; end_of_sample                                                 ***
&9a94 a9 7f    LDA #&7f                                         ***
&9a96 8d 6e fe STA &fe6e ; User VIA interrupt enable register   *** # Disable all User VIA interrupts
&9a99 a9 00    LDA #&00                                         ***
&9a9b 8d a7 11 STA &11a7 ; sound_channels_volume + 3            ***
&9a9e 8d c7 11 STA &11c7 ; sound_channels_volume_duration + 3   ***
&9aa1 a9 03    LDA #&03                                         *** # Restore the number of channels available for sounds
&9aa3 8d 92 11 STA &1192 ; number_of_sound_channels             ***
&9aa6 a9 ff    LDA #&ff                                         ***
&9aa8 8d 48 9a STA &9a48 ; current_sample                       *** # Set to negative to indicate no sample being played
&9aab 60       RTS                                              ***

; update_palette_registers
&9aac a0 10    LDY #&10
; update_palette_registers_loop
&9aae b9 dc 11 LDA &11dc,Y ; palette_registers_table - 1
&9ab1 8d 21 fe STA &fe21 ; video ULA palette register
&9ab4 88       DEY
&9ab5 d0 f7    BNE &9aae ; update_palette_registers_loop
&9ab7 60       RTS

; update_routines_base

# Tile update routines
# ====================

# Nest and pipe tiles (TILE_NEST and TILE_PIPE)
# =============================================
# Tertiary data byte is as follows:
#
# 8....... if set, nest or pipe has bush (used as home target for imps)
# .42184.. number of creatures to spawn
# ......21 if non-zero, nest or pipe is inactive; suppress spawning
#
# Tertiary type byte sets type of spawn
#
# Tertiary tile byte sets immediacy of spawning:
#
# 8....... if set, consider spawning when nest or pipe is plotted
#
# update_nest_or_pipe_tile is called when plotting tiles, processing collisions and during events

; update_nest_or_pipe_tile                                          # Called with Y = this_object_tertiary_data_offset
&9ab8 f0 6b    BEQ &9b25 ; leave_with_carry_set                     # Leave if Y = 0, i.e. no tertiary object for tile
&9aba a2 05    LDX #&05                                             # Five slots needed when spawning
&9abc a5 2d    LDA &2d ; tile_processing_mode
&9abe 29 90    AND #&90 ; TILE_PROCESSING_FLAG_PLOTTING | TILE_PROCESSING_FLAG_EVENTS
&9ac0 f0 23    BEQ &9ae5 ; consider_spawning
&9ac2 10 02    BPL &9ac6 ; is_event
; is_plotting_tile
&9ac4 a2 00    LDX #&00                                             # Zero slots needs when first plotting tiles
; is_event
&9ac6 a5 09    LDA &09 ; tile_flip
&9ac8 0a       ASL A                                                # Set carry if flipped horizontally
;                                                                   # If so, consider spawning creature immediately
&9ac9 b9 86 09 LDA &0986,Y ; tertiary_objects_data                  # &80 clear if object has already become primary
&9acc 10 1c    BPL &9aea ; spawn_object_if_carry_set
&9ace 08       PHP ; negative, carry set if tile flipped horizontally
&9acf 8a       TXA
&9ad0 48       PHA ; tmp_x
&9ad1 a9 40    LDA #&40 ; OBJECT_BUSH
&9ad3 20 df 9c JSR &9cdf ; create_primary_object_from_tertiary      # Create bush object if it doesn't already exist
&9ad6 a9 40    LDA #&40
&9ad8 99 a3 08 STA &08a3,Y ; objects_y_fraction
&9adb 99 80 08 STA &0880,Y ; objects_x_fraction
&9ade a4 bd    LDY &bd ; this_object_tertiary_data_offset
&9ae0 68       PLA ; tmp_x
&9ae1 aa       TAX
&9ae2 28       PLP ; negative, carry set if tile flipped horizontally
&9ae3 30 05    BMI &9aea ; spawn_object_if_carry_set                # Always branches
; consider_spawning
&9ae5 20 b6 25 JSR &25b6 ; rnd
&9ae8 c9 f7    CMP #&f7                                             # 1 in 32 chance of nest or pipe spawning
; spawn_object_if_carry_set
&9aea 90 39    BCC &9b25 ; leave_with_carry_set
; spawn_object
&9aec 86 a2    STX &a2 ; slots needed
&9aee b9 86 09 LDA &0986,Y ; tertiary_objects_data
&9af1 85 a3    STA &a3 ; data
&9af3 0a       ASL A                                                # 84218421 -> 8 4218421.
&9af4 c9 08    CMP #&08
&9af6 90 2d    BCC &9b25 ; leave_with_carry_set                     # Does the nest contain at least one creature?
&9af8 29 06    AND #&06                                             #               .....21.
&9afa d0 29    BNE &9b25 ; leave_with_carry_set                     # Is the nest active?
&9afc a6 be    LDX &be ; this_object_tertiary_type_offset
&9afe bd 71 0a LDA &0a71,X ; tertiary_objects_type
&9b01 a4 a2    LDY &a2 ; slots_needed
&9b03 85 a2    STA &a2 ; new_object_type
&9b05 20 f1 9c JSR &9cf1 ; create_primary_object_from_tertiary_if_Y_slots_free # Leaves update routine if can't create object
;                                                                   # Otherwise, returns carry clear
&9b08 a5 a3    LDA &a3 ; data
&9b0a e9 03    SBC #&03                                             # Carry is clear here, so subtract four
&9b0c 9d 86 09 STA &0986,X ; tertiary_objects_data                  # to reduce number of creatures it contains
&9b0f a9 05    LDA #&05 ; OBJECT_FLAG_NEWLY_CREATED | OBJECT_FLAG_NOT_PLOTTED
&9b11 99 c6 08 STA &08c6,Y ; objects_flags
&9b14 a6 a2    LDX &a2 ; new_object_type
&9b16 bd 8a 02 LDA &028a,X ; object_types_sprite_table
&9b19 aa       TAX
&9b1a bd 0c be LDA &be0c,X ; sprites_width_and_horizontal_flip_table
&9b1d 49 ff    EOR #&ff
&9b1f 4a       LSR A                                                # Centre spawn with nest or pipe
&9b20 99 80 08 STA &0880,Y ; objects_x_fraction
&9b23 18       CLC                                                  # Not used
; leave
&9b24 60       RTS
; leave_with_carry_set
&9b25 38       SEC                                                  # Not used
&9b26 60       RTS

# Engine tiles (TILE_ENGINE)
# ==========================
# Tertiary data byte is as follows:
#
# 8....... if set, engine fire needs primary object creating
# ......21 if non-zero, engine is inactive
#
# update_engine_tile is called when plotting tiles, processing collisions and during events

; update_engine_tile                                                # Called with Y = this_object_tertiary_data_offset
&9b27 f0 fb    BEQ &9b24 ; leave                                    # Leave if Y = 0, i.e. no tertiary object for tile
&9b29 a9 3b    LDA #&3b ; OBJECT_ENGINE_FIRE
&9b2b 4c df 9c JMP &9cdf ; create_primary_object_from_tertiary      # Create engine fire object if it doesn't already exist

# Door tiles (TILE_METAL_DOOR and TILE_STONE_DOOR)
# ================================================
# Tertiary data byte is as follows:
#
# 8....... if set, door needs primary object creating
# .421.... door colour
# ....8... if set, door is slow, is being or has been destroyed (DOOR_FLAG_SLOW_OR_DESTROYED)
# .....4.. if set, door is moving (DOOR_FLAG_MOVING)
# ......2. if set, door is opening; if clear, door is closing (DOOR_FLAG_OPENING)
# .......1 if set, door is locked (DOOR_FLAG_LOCKED)
#
# Tertiary tile byte sets door orientation:
#
# 84...... &00 or &c0 horizontal door
#          &40 or &80 vertical door
#
# objects_state is used for tile x or tile y
# objects_tx is used for open fraction
# objects_ty is used for orientation
#
# update_stone_door_tile is called when plotting tiles, checking for obstruction, processing collisions and during events
# update_metal_door_tile is called when plotting tiles, checking for obstruction, processing collisions and during events

; door_tiles_table                                                  # Tiles that are equivalent in obstruction
&9b2e 17 ; &00 (closed horizontal door) : TILE_SPACESHIP_WALL_HORIZONTAL_QUARTER
&9b2f 19 ; &01 (open horizontal door)   : TILE_SPACE
&9b30 2a ; &02 (closed vertical door)   : TILE_STONE_SLOPE_SEVENTY_EIGHT
&9b31 19 ; &03 (open vertical door)     : TILE_SPACE

; update_stone_door_tile                                            # Called with Y = this_object_tertiary_data_offset
&9b32 a9 3e    LDA #&3e ; OBJECT_HORIZONTAL_STONE_DOOR
&9b34 2c a9 3c BIT &3ca9 ; (nop)
; update_metal_door_tile                                            # Called with Y = this_object_tertiary_data_offset
#9b35          LDA #&3c ; OBJECT_HORIZONTAL_METAL_DOOR
&9b37 85 9c    STA &9c ; door_type
&9b39 cc 00 36 CPY &3600 ; door_to_suppress                         # Don't create door during obstruction check
&9b3c f0 41    BEQ &9b7f ; leave
&9b3e a5 09    LDA &09 ; tile_flip                                  # &80 set if flipped horizontally, &40 if vertically
&9b40 0a       ASL A                                                # 84...... -> 8 4.......
&9b41 2a       ROL A                                                #          -> 4 .......8
&9b42 69 00    ADC #&00
&9b44 29 01    AND #&01                                             # 0 if neither or both set, horizontal door
&9b46 48       PHA ; door orientation                               # 1 if one set, vertical door
&9b47 b9 86 09 LDA &0986,Y ; tertiary_objects_data
&9b4a 30 01    BMI &9b4d ; is_tertiary
&9b4c 4a       LSR A                                                # If not tertiary, use &04 DOOR_FLAG_MOVING instead
; is_tertiary
&9b4d 29 02    AND #&02 ; DOOR_FLAG_OPENING                         # &02 set if door is open (&04 set if door is moving)
&9b4f 4a       LSR A                                                # ......2. -> ......2
&9b50 e9 00    SBC #&00                                             #          -> &ff if unset, &00 if set
&9b52 85 a3    STA &a3 ; door_open_fraction
&9b54 a5 2d    LDA &2d ; tile_processing_mode                       # Negative if TILE_PROCESSING_FLAG_PLOTTING set
&9b56 30 09    BMI &9b61 ; skip_setting_tile                        # If not plotting tiles,
&9b58 68       PLA ; door orientation
&9b59 48       PHA ; door orientation
&9b5a 2a       ROL A                                                # Carry set if door is open
&9b5b aa       TAX                                                  # 0: h closed, 1: h open, 2: v closed, 3: v open
&9b5c bd 2e 9b LDA &9b2e,X ; door_tiles_table                       # Use tile that is equivalent in obstruction to door
&9b5f 85 08    STA &08 ; tile_type_and_flip
; skip_setting_tile
&9b61 68       PLA ; door orientation
&9b62 24 2d    BIT &2d ; tile_processing_mode
&9b64 70 19    BVS &9b7f ; leave                                    # &40 set if TILE_PROCESSING_FLAG_OBSTRUCTION
; create_door                                                       # If not considering obstructions, create door object
&9b66 48       PHA ; door orientation                               # 0 if horizontal door, 1 if vertical door
&9b67 18       CLC
&9b68 65 9c    ADC &9c ; door_type
&9b6a 20 df 9c JSR &9cdf ; create_primary_object_from_tertiary      # Create door object if it doesn't already exist
&9b6d 68       PLA ; door orientation
&9b6e 0a       ASL A
&9b6f 99 36 09 STA &0936,Y ; objects_ty (door orientation)          # 0 for horizontal door, 2 for vertical door
&9b72 aa       TAX
&9b73 b5 95    LDA &95,X ; tile_x
&9b75 e9 00    SBC #&00
&9b77 99 76 09 STA &0976,Y ; objects_state (door tile x or y)
&9b7a a5 a3    LDA &a3 ; door_open_fraction
&9b7c 99 16 09 STA &0916,Y ; objects_tx (door open fraction)
; leave
&9b7f 60       RTS

# Transporter tiles (TILE_TRANSPORTER)
# ====================================
# Tertiary data byte is as follows:
#
# 8....... if set, transporter beam needs primary object creating
# .42..... key to unlock
#              &00 : OBJECT_YELLOW_WHITE_RED_KEY
#              &20 : (key 4, not collectable)
#              &40 : OBJECT_RED_MAGENTA_RED_KEY
#              &60 : OBJECT_BLUE_CYAN_GREEN_KEY
# ...1842. transporter destination
# .......1 if set, transporter is inactive
#
# update_transporter_tile is called when plotting tiles, processing collisions and during events

; update_transporter_tile
&9b80 a9 41    LDA #&41 ; OBJECT_TRANSPORTER_BEAM
&9b82 20 df 9c JSR &9cdf ; create_primary_object_from_tertiary      # Create beam object if it doesn't already exist
&9b85 a9 40    LDA #&40
&9b87 99 80 08 STA &0880,Y ; objects_x_fraction
&9b8a 0a       ASL A
&9b8b 99 a3 08 STA &08a3,Y ; objects_y_fraction
; leave
&9b8e 60       RTS

# Invisible switch tiles (TILE_INVISIBLE_SWITCH)
# ==============================================
# Tertiary data byte is as follows:
#
# 84218... switch effects number
# .....42. bits to clear or set in other objects
# .......1 set bits if set, clear bits if clear
#
# Tertiary type byte sets object type than can trigger switch, or negative for any object
#
# update_invisible_switch_tile is called when processing collisions

; update_invisible_switch_tile
&9b8f a6 be    LDX &be ; this_object_tertiary_type_offset
&9b91 bd 71 0a LDA &0a71,X ; tertiary_objects_type                  # Type is negative if any object can trigger switch
&9b94 30 04    BMI &9b9a ; consider_triggering_invisible_switch
&9b96 c5 41    CMP &41 ; this_object_type                           # Otherwise, type is object type required to trigger
&9b98 d0 f4    BNE &9b8e ; leave
; consider_triggering_invisible_switch
&9b9a a4 aa    LDY &aa ; this_object
&9b9c 20 5e a6 JSR &a65e ; check_if_object_can_trigger_switches     # Returns carry set if object can trigger
&9b9f 90 ed    BCC &9b8e ; leave
&9ba1 a4 bd    LDY &bd ; this_object_tertiary_data_offset
&9ba3 b9 86 09 LDA &0986,Y ; tertiary_objects_data
&9ba6 48       PHA ; data
&9ba7 4a       LSR A                                                # 84218421 -> .8421842
&9ba8 09 fc    ORA #&fc                                             #          -> ******42, i.e. bits to set or clear
&9baa 49 03    EOR #&03
&9bac aa       TAX ; data mask
&9bad 68       PLA ; data
&9bae b0 02    BCS &9bb2 ; to_process_switch_effects
&9bb0 29 f8    AND #&f8 ; switch effect number and data toggle      # 84218... switch effects number; clear bits to toggle
; to_process_switch_effects
&9bb2 4c 74 a6 JMP &a674 ; process_switch_effects

# Variable wind tiles (TILE_VARIABLE_WIND)
# ========================================
# update_variable_wind_tile is called when processing collisions and during events

; update_variable_wind_tile
&9bb5 24 20    BIT &20 ; this_object_waterline
&9bb7 30 d5    BMI &9b8e ; leave                                    # Random winds don't occur when underwater
&9bb9 24 09    BIT &09 ; tile_flip                                  # &80 set if flipped horizontally, i.e. one of the two
&9bbb 10 04    BPL &9bc1 ; not_flipped                              # square caverns below the west stone door (&184f)
&9bbd a9 70    LDA #&70                                             # If so, use a constant downdraft
&9bbf 10 23    BPL &9be4 ; apply_wind_velocities_from_A             # Always branches
; not_flipped
&9bc1 a5 c0    LDA &c0 ; frame_counter                              # In the other windy square caverns,
&9bc3 2a       ROL A
&9bc4 2a       ROL A
&9bc5 85 b5    STA &b5 ; angle                                      # Angle of wind cycles 360 degrees every 64 frames
&9bc7 a5 da    LDA &da ; rnd_state + 1
&9bc9 29 1f    AND #&1f                                             # Magnitude of wind has a random component
&9bcb 45 97    EOR &97 ; tile_y                                     # but also depends on tile position
&9bcd 0a       ASL A
&9bce 29 7f    AND #&7f
&9bd0 24 95    BIT &95 ; tile_x
&9bd2 10 04    BPL &9bd8 ; is_left_half_of_world
; is_right_half_of_world
&9bd4 29 3f    AND #&3f
&9bd6 69 28    ADC #&28
; is_left_half_of_world
&9bd8 20 8a 23 JSR &238a ; calculate_vector_from_magnitude_and_angle
&9bdb 4c ec 9b JMP &9bec ; apply_wind_velocities_from_vector

# Constant wind tiles (TILE_CONSTANT_WIND)
# ========================================
# Tertiary data byte is as follows:
# 
# 8421.... y velocity
# ....8421 x velocity
#
# update_constant_wind_tile is called when processing collisions and during events

; update_constant_wind_tile                                         # Called with Y = this_object_tertiary_data_offset
&9bde 98       TYA
&9bdf f0 59    BEQ &9c3a ; update_variable_water_tile               # Used to create the bottom river in Triax's lab
&9be1 b9 86 09 LDA &0986,Y ; tertiary_objects_data
; apply_wind_velocities_from_A
&9be4 85 b6    STA &b6 ; vector_y                                   # Top nibble of data sets y velocity
&9be6 0a       ASL A
&9be7 0a       ASL A
&9be8 0a       ASL A
&9be9 0a       ASL A
&9bea 85 b4    STA &b4 ; vector_x                                   # Bottom nibble of data sets x velocity
; apply_wind_velocities_from_vector
&9bec 20 b8 9c JSR &9cb8 ; create_invisible_debris_if_event         # Returns non-zero if event
&9bef d0 62    BNE &9c53 ; leave                                    # Otherwise, an object has collided with this tile
&9bf1 a2 02    LDX #&02
; apply_wind_velocity_loop                                          # Loop through X = 2 for y, X = 0 for x
&9bf3 a4 38    LDY &38 ; this_object_weight
&9bf5 c0 04    CPY #&04
&9bf7 b0 01    BCS &9bfa ; not_light
&9bf9 c8       INY                                                  # Halve wind effects if object is heavy
; not_light
&9bfa 24 20    BIT &20 ; this_object_waterline                      # Negative if completely under water
&9bfc 10 01    BPL &9bff ; not_underwater
&9bfe c8       INY                                                  # Halve wind effects if object is under water
; not_underwater
&9bff 24 1f    BIT &1f ; this_object_in_water                       # Positive if object is in any water
&9c01 10 06    BPL &9c09 ; skip_frame_check                         # Always add water velocity
&9c03 a5 c0    LDA &c0 ; frame_counter
&9c05 29 10    AND #&10
&9c07 f0 4a    BEQ &9c53 ; leave                                    # Add wind velocity 16 out of 32 frames
; skip_frame_check
&9c09 20 31 9c JSR &9c31 ; add_weighted_vector_component_to_this_object_velocity
&9c0c ca       DEX
&9c0d ca       DEX
&9c0e f0 e3    BEQ &9bf3 ; apply_wind_velocity_loop
; add_wind_particle_using_velocities
&9c10 20 07 23 JSR &2307 ; calculate_angle_from_vector
&9c13 a5 da    LDA &da ; rnd_state + 1
&9c15 4a       LSR A
&9c16 c5 b7    CMP &b7 ; magnitude
&9c18 b0 39    BCS &9c53 ; leave                                    # More wind particles the stronger the wind is
&9c1a a0 6e    LDY #&6e ; PARTICLE_WIND
; set_new_particles_position_from_this_object
&9c1c a2 02    LDX #&02
; set_new_particles_position_loop                                   # Loop through X = 2 for y, X = 0 for x
&9c1e b5 4f    LDA &4f,X ; this_object_x_fraction
&9c20 e9 40    SBC #&40                                             # Offset by quarter of a tile to left and up
&9c22 95 87    STA &87,X ; new_particles_x_fraction                 # This is a base to which randomness is added
&9c24 b5 53    LDA &53,X ; this_object_x
&9c26 e9 00    SBC #&00
&9c28 95 8b    STA &8b,X ; new_particles_x
&9c2a ca       DEX
&9c2b ca       DEX
&9c2c f0 f0    BEQ &9c1e ; set_new_particles_position_loop
&9c2e 4c bf 21 JMP &21bf ; add_particle

; add_weighted_vector_component_to_this_object_velocity             # Called with Y = weight factor, X = 0 for x, 2 for y
&9c31 a9 0c    LDA #&0c ; wind acceleration
&9c33 85 9c    STA &9c ; maximum_acceleration
&9c35 b5 b4    LDA &b4,X ; vector_x
&9c37 4c 5d 32 JMP &325d ; apply_weighted_acceleration_to_this_object_velocity

# Water tiles (TILE_WATER)
# ========================
# Tile &0d is still water
# Tile &4d is unused
# Tile &8d is river in Triax's lab
# Tile &cd is waterfall in Triax's lab
#
# update_water_tile is called when processing collisions and during events

; update_variable_water_tile
&9c3a a5 c0    LDA &c0 ; frame_counter
&9c3c 29 10    AND #&10                                             # 16 out of 32 frames
&9c3e d0 13    BNE &9c53 ; leave
; update_water_tile
&9c40 20 b8 9c JSR &9cb8 ; create_invisible_debris_if_event         # Returns non-zero if event
&9c43 d0 0e    BNE &9c53 ; leave
&9c45 a5 09    LDA &09 ; tile_flip
&9c47 0a       ASL A                                                # 84...... -> 4....... 8
&9c48 2a       ROL A                                                #          -> .......8 4
&9c49 2a       ROL A                                                #          -> ......84
&9c4a aa       TAX
&9c4b bd 77 1e LDA &1e77,X ; water_velocities_table                 # Use tile flip to determine velocity of water
&9c4e d0 94    BNE &9be4 ; apply_wind_velocities_from_A
; is_still_water
&9c50 38       SEC
&9c51 66 01    ROR &01 ; water_tile                                 # Set top bit to indicate this is a water tile
; leave
&9c53 60       RTS

# Tiles with objects from type (TILE_*_WITH_OBJECT_FROM_TYPE)
# ===========================================================
# Objects are created as soon as tile is considered, although may be returned
# to tertiary when they go offscreen.
#
# Tertiary type byte sets type of object.
#
# Tertiary data byte depends on type of object. The top bit is always as follows:
#
# 8....... if set, object needs primary object creating
#          if unset, object is either primary or has been collected or destroyed
#
# update_tile_with_object_from_type is called when plotting tiles, processing collisions and during events

; update_tile_with_object_from_type
&9c54 a6 be    LDX &be ; this_object_tertiary_type_offset
&9c56 bd 71 0a LDA &0a71,X ; tertiary_objects_type
&9c59 4c df 9c JMP &9cdf ; create_primary_object_from_tertiary

# Tiles with objects from data (TILE_SPACE_WITH_OBJECT_FROM_DATA)
# ===============================================================
# Objects are created as placeholders as soon as tile is considered, which are
# returned to tertiary when they go offscreen. When disturbed, these
# placeholder objects are converted to proper object types, which may be
# demoted to secondary when they go offscreen, but do not return to tertiary.
#
# Tertiary data byte is as follows:
#
# 8....... if set, object is tertiary object, not placeholder or primary / secondary
# .4218421 type of object
#
# update_tile_with_object_from_data is called when plotting tiles and during events

; update_tile_with_object_from_data
&9c5c b9 86 09 LDA &0986,Y ; tertiary_objects_data
&9c5f 29 7f    AND #&7f
&9c61 20 df 9c JSR &9cdf ; create_primary_object_from_tertiary
&9c64 a9 49    LDA #&49 ; OBJECT_PLACEHOLDER
&9c66 99 60 08 STA &0860,Y ; objects_type
&9c69 60       RTS

# Switch tiles (TILE_SWITCH)
# ==========================
# Tertiary data byte is as follows:
#
# 8....... if set, switch needs primary object creating
# .4218... switch effects number
# .....42. bits to toggle in other objects
# .......1 switch state
#
# update_switch_tile is called when plotting tiles, processing collisions and during events

; update_switch_tile
&9c6a a9 42    LDA #&42 ; OBJECT_SWITCH
&9c6c 4c df 9c JMP &9cdf ; create_primary_object_from_tertiary      # Create switch object if it doesn't already exist

# Mushroom tiles (TILE_MUSHROOMS)
# ===============================
# Tile &0d is red mushrooms
# Tile &4d is blue mushrooms
#
# update_mushroom_tile is called when processing collisions and during events

; update_mushroom_tile
&9c6f a2 33    LDX #&33 ; OBJECT_RED_MUSHROOM_BALL
&9c71 24 09    BIT &09 ; tile_flip                                  # &40 set if vertically flipped, i.e. blue mushrooms
&9c73 50 01    BVC &9c76 ; not_blue
&9c75 e8       INX ; &34 ; OBJECT_BLUE_MUSHROOM_BALL
; not_blue                                                          # If tile picked by random event, create a mushroom ball
&9c76 20 ba 9c JSR &9cba ; create_object_if_event                   # Returns zero if not event, carry set if couldn't create
;                                                                   # Returns A = &ff if event
&9c79 f0 0c    BEQ &9c87 ; is_collision
&9c7b b0 09    BCS &9c86 ; leave
; is_event
&9c7d 24 09    BIT &09 ; tile_flip                                  # &40 set if vertically flipped, i.e. blue mushrooms
&9c7f 50 02    BVC &9c83 ; set_new_mushroom_ball_y_fraction
&9c81 a9 00    LDA #&00                                             # Put blue mushroom balls on top of tile (&00)
; set_new_mushroom_ball_y_fraction
&9c83 99 a3 08 STA &08a3,Y ; objects_y_fraction                     # Put red mushroom balls on bottom of tile (&ff)
; leave
&9c86 60       RTS
; is_collision                                                      # If processing collision with tile,
&9c87 a5 09    LDA &09 ; tile_flip                                  # &40 set if vertically flipped, i.e. blue mushrooms
&9c89 0a       ASL A                                                # 84218421 -> 8 4218421.
&9c8a 0a       ASL A                                                #          -> 4 218421.., i.e. carry set if blue mushrooms
&9c8b a4 aa    LDY &aa ; this_object
; play_sound_for_mushrooms
&9c8d 98       TYA
&9c8e d0 06    BNE &9c96 ; not_player
; is_player
&9c90 69 00    ADC #&00                                             # Carry set if blue mushrooms
&9c92 aa       TAX                                                  # X = 0 for red mushrooms, 1 for blue mushrooms
&9c93 20 a2 9c JSR &9ca2 ; add_to_player_mushroom_timer
; not_player
&9c96 20 0f 14 JSR &140f ; play_sound
&9c99 33 f3 1d 03                                                   # Play sound for collision with mushroom tile
&9c9d a0 4d    LDY #&4d ; PARTICLE_STAR_OR_MUSHROOM
&9c9e 4c 1c 9c JMP &9c1c ; set_new_particles_position_from_this_object

; add_to_player_mushroom_timer
&9ca2 a9 3f    LDA #&3f
&9ca4 7d 1a 08 ADC &081a,X ; player_mushroom_timers
&9ca7 b0 03    BCS &9cac ; skip_ceiling
&9ca9 9d 1a 08 STA &081a,X ; player_mushroom_timers
; skip_ceiling
&9cac 2c 15 08 BIT &0815 ; player_mushroom_immunity_pill_collected  # Negative if mushroom immunity pill collected
&9caf 30 2d    BMI &9cde ; leave
&9cb1 d5 ba    CMP &ba,X ; player_immobility_timers
&9cb3 90 29    BCC &9cde ; leave
&9cb5 95 ba    STA &ba,X ; player_immobility_timers
&9cb7 60       RTS

; create_invisible_debris_if_event
&9cb8 a2 35    LDX #&35 ; OBJECT_INVISIBLE_DEBRIS
; create_object_if_event                                            # Called with X = object type
&9cba a9 10    LDA #&10 ; TILE_PROCESSING_FLAG_EVENTS
&9cbc 24 2d    BIT &2d ; tile_processing_mode
&9cbe f0 1e    BEQ &9cde ; leave                                    # Leave with zero if this isn't because of an event
&9cc0 8a       TXA ; object type
&9cc1 48       PHA ; object type
&9cc2 20 7f 3c JSR &3c7f ; count_objects_of_type_A                  # Returns Y = count
&9cc5 c0 04    CPY #&04                                             # Don't create if four or more such objects already exist
&9cc7 68       PLA ; object type
&9cc8 b0 12    BCS &9cdc ; leave_with_non_zero                      # Leave with carry set if object couldn't be created
&9cca 20 8d 1e JSR &1e8d ; create_new_object_even_if_no_slots_free  # Returns carry clear if object created, Y = slot
&9ccd b0 0d    BCS &9cdc ; leave_with_non_zero                      # Leave with carry set if object couldn't be created
&9ccf 20 96 28 JSR &2896 ; set_object_x_y_and_tx_ty_from_tile_x_y
&9cd2 a5 d9    LDA &d9 ; rnd_state
&9cd4 99 a3 08 STA &08a3,Y ; objects_y_fraction                     # Set random position within tile
&9cd7 a5 da    LDA &da ; rnd_state + 1
&9cd9 99 80 08 STA &0880,Y ; objects_x_fraction
; leave_with_non_zero
&9cdc a9 ff    LDA #&ff                                             # Leave with non-zero if this was because of an event
; leave
&9cde 60       RTS

; create_primary_object_from_tertiary                               # Called with A = object type
&9cdf a0 00    LDY #&00 ; free slots needed
&9ce1 2c a0 08 BIT &08a0 ; (nop)
; create_primary_object_from_tertiary_if_eight_slots_free           # Unused entry point
#9ce2          LDY #&08 ; free slots needed
&9ce4 85 a2    STA &a2 ; new_object_type
&9ce6 a6 bd    LDX &bd ; this_object_tertiary_data_offset           # Zero if not a tertiary object that has a data byte
&9ce8 f0 07    BEQ &9cf1 ; create_primary_object_from_tertiary_if_Y_slots_free
&9cea bd 86 09 LDA &0986,X ; tertiary_objects_data                  # &80 clear if object has already become primary
&9ced 10 3f    BPL &9d2e ; leave_calling_routine
&9cef a5 a2    LDA &a2 ; new_object_type
; create_primary_object_from_tertiary_if_Y_slots_free
&9cf1 20 95 1e JSR &1e95 ; create_new_object_if_Y_slots_free        # Returns carry clear if object created, X = slot
&9cf4 b0 38    BCS &9d2e ; leave_tile_update_routine
&9cf6 20 96 28 JSR &2896 ; set_object_x_y_and_tx_ty_from_tile_x_y
&9cf9 a6 a2    LDX &a2 ; new_object_type
&9cfb bd 8a 02 LDA &028a,X ; object_types_sprite_table
&9cfe aa       TAX
&9cff a5 09    LDA &09 ; tile_flip
&9d01 09 05    ORA #&05 ; OBJECT_FLAG_NEWLY_CREATED | OBJECT_FLAG_NOT_PLOTTED
&9d03 99 c6 08 STA &08c6,Y ; objects_flags                          # Give object same flip as tile
;                                                                   # This sets other properties for some types of object
&9d06 a9 00    LDA #&00
&9d08 24 09    BIT &09 ; tile_flip                                  # &80 set if flipped horizontally
&9d0a 10 03    BPL &9d0f ; set_new_object_x_fraction
&9d0c fd 0c be SBC &be0c,X ; sprites_width_and_horizontal_flip_table
; set_new_object_x_fraction
&9d0f 99 80 08 STA &0880,Y ; objects_x_fraction
&9d12 a9 00    LDA #&00
&9d14 24 09    BIT &09 ; tile_flip                                  # &40 set if flipped vertically
&9d16 70 03    BVS &9d1b ; set_new_object_y_fraction
&9d18 fd 89 be SBC &be89,X ; sprites_height_and_vertical_flip_table
; set_new_object_y_fraction
&9d1b 99 a3 08 STA &08a3,Y ; objects_y_fraction
&9d1e a5 bd    LDA &bd ; this_object_tertiary_data_offset
&9d20 99 66 09 STA &0966,Y ; objects_tertiary_data_offset
&9d23 aa       TAX
&9d24 bd 86 09 LDA &0986,X ; tertiary_objects_data
&9d27 29 7f    AND #&7f                                             # Clear &80 to indicate object is now primary
&9d29 9d 86 09 STA &0986,X ; tertiary_objects_data
&9d2c 18       CLC                                                  # Clear carry to indicate object created
&9d2d 60       RTS
; leave_calling_routine
&9d2e a6 26    LDX &26 ; stack_pointer_to_leave_to_if_unable_to_create_primary_object
&9d30 9a       TXS
&9d31 38       SEC                                                  # Set carry to indicate object couldn't be created
; leave
&9d32 60       RTS

# Explosion update routines
# =========================

; consider_teleporting_damaged_player
&9d33 a6 aa    LDX &aa ; this_object
&9d35 d0 fb    BNE &9d32 ; leave                                    # Leave if not player
&9d37 e6 15    INC &15 ; this_object_energy                         # Give player non-zero energy to prevent exploding
&9d39 20 b6 25 JSR &25b6 ; rnd
&9d3c 10 0b    BPL &9d49 ; skip_automatically_teleporting_player    # 1 in 2 chance of automatically teleporting player
; automatically_teleport_player
&9d3e 20 2f 33 JSR &332f ; handle_dropping_object
&9d41 a2 04    LDX #&04 ; player_deaths - game_time                 # Update player deaths
&9d43 20 8c 12 JSR &128c ; update_game_time_or_player_deaths
&9d46 4c c1 0c JMP &0cc1 ; handle_teleporting                       # Automatically teleport damaged player
; skip_automatically_teleporting_player
&9d49 a5 da    LDA &da ; rnd_state + 1
&9d4b c9 c0    CMP #&c0                                             # 1 in 4 chance of retrieving object
&9d4d 6a       ROR A
&9d4e 25 dd    AND &dd ; player_object_held                         # if one isn't already being held
&9d50 10 03    BPL &9d55 ; skip_retrieving_object
&9d52 20 6b 35 JSR &356b ; retrieve_object
; skip_retrieving_object
&9d55 4c 2f 33 JMP &332f ; handle_dropping_object

; explode_object_by_turning_into_fireball
&9d58 4c 60 a7 JMP &a760 ; turn_object_into_fireball_of_duration_seven

; explode_object_with_loud_squeal
&9d5b 20 0f 14 JSR &140f ; play_sound
&9d5e 57 07 43 f6                                                   # Play sound for loud squeal
; explode_object_with_squeal
&9d62 20 ca 14 JSR &14ca ; play_squeal
&9d65 a5 41    LDA &41 ; this_object_type
&9d67 20 df 2d JSR &2ddf ; get_range_for_object_type_A
&9d6a bd 27 2a LDA &2a27,X ; object_type_ranges_energy_table
&9d6d 4a       LSR A
&9d6e 4a       LSR A
&9d6f 4a       LSR A
&9d70 4a       LSR A
&9d71 4a       LSR A
&9d72 69 03    ADC #&03                                             # explosion duration = (initial energy / 32) + 3
&9d74 10 02    BPL &9d78 ; explode_object_with_duration_A           # Always branches
; explode_object_with_duration_from_energy                          # Unused entry point
&9d76 a5 15    LDA &15 ; this_object_energy
; explode_object_with_duration_A
&9d78 20 0d 14 JSR &140d ; play_sound_on_channel_zero
&9d7b 17 03 11 04                                                   # Play sound for exploding object
; explode_object_with_duration_A_but_no_sound
&9d7f 85 3d    STA &3d ; this_object_tertiary_data_offset (explosion duration)
&9d81 a9 44    LDA #&44 ; OBJECT_EXPLOSION
&9d83 85 41    STA &41 ; this_object_type
; start_explosion_timer
&9d85 a9 ce    LDA #&ce ; -50
&9d87 8d 1d 08 STA &081d ; explosion_timer                          # Negative if explosion in progress
&9d8a 60       RTS

# Object update routines
