# Cannon (OBJECT_CANNON)
# ======================

; update_cannon
&9d8b a9 4f    LDA #&4f ; OBJECT_CANNON_CONTROL_DEVICE
&9d8d 20 c7 0b JSR &0bc7 ; check_if_object_hit_by_other_control     # Returns carry clear if object hit by control
&9d90 b0 07    BCS &9d99 ; skip_firing_cannon
&9d92 a2 15    LDX #&15 ; OBJECT_CANNONBALL
&9d94 a9 40    LDA #&40 ; x velocity
&9d96 20 10 34 JSR &3410 ; create_projectile_with_zero_velocity_y
; skip_firing_cannon
&9d99 a9 0f    LDA #&0f                                             # 1 in 16 chance of flipping cannon when moving
&9d9b 4c a9 25 JMP &25a9 ; consider_flipping_object_to_match_velocity_x_A

# Lightning (OBJECT_LIGHTNING)
# ============================
# objects_state is used for size
# objects_timer is used for lifespan

; update_lightning                                                  # Called with Y = object touching, or negative if none
&9d9e 30 0e    BMI &9dae ; not_touching_other_object
&9da0 20 fc 1f JSR &1ffc ; check_if_object_Y_collides_with_lightning_or_fireball # Returns zero if object doesn't collide
&9da3 f0 0c    BEQ &9db1 ; not_touching_anything_collidable
&9da5 c9 01    CMP #&01 ; OBJECT_ACTIVE_CHATTER
&9da7 f0 05    BEQ &9dae ; skip_damaging_object
&9da9 a9 50    LDA #&50
&9dab 20 d5 24 JSR &24d5 ; damage_object                            # Lightning causes 80 damage
; skip_damaging_object
; not_touching_other_object
&9dae 98       TYA
&9daf 49 ff    EOR #&ff                                             # &80 set if touching something
; not_touching_anything_collidable
&9db1 a6 11    LDX &11 ; this_object_state (lightning size)         # Absolute value is lighting size; &fc <= size <= &04
;                                                                   # Positive if growing or full length, negative if shrinking
&9db3 05 1b    ORA &1b ; this_object_tile_top_or_bottom_collision   # &80 set if object hit tiles above or below
&9db5 49 ff    EOR #&ff                                             # &80 set if no collision with objects or tiles
&9db7 05 11    ORA &11 ; this_object_state (lightning size)         # &80 set if lightning already growing smaller
&9db9 30 09    BMI &9dc4 ; no_collision                             # If the lightning collided with something,
&9dbb 8a       TXA ; lightning size
&9dbc 20 b3 32 JSR &32b3 ; invert_if_positive                       # set it growing smaller from its current size
&9dbf aa       TAX ; lightning size
&9dc0 d0 02    BNE &9dc4 ; not_zero
&9dc2 a2 fe    LDX #&fe ; -2
; not_zero
; no_collision
&9dc4 c6 12    DEC &12 ; this_object_timer (lightning timer)        # Starts at zero, becomes negative
&9dc6 a5 12    LDA &12 ; this_object_timer (lightning timer)
&9dc8 c9 e7    CMP #&e7 ; -25                                       # Keep lightning growing or full length for 25 frames
&9dca f0 04    BEQ &9dd0 ; is_at_turning_point                      # By branching, set A negative, i.e. start shrinking
&9dcc e8       INX                                                  # Lengthen or shrink the lightning
&9dcd f0 23    BEQ &9df2 ; to_set_object_for_removal                # Remove it at zero size
&9dcf 8a       TXA
; is_at_turning_point
&9dd0 a0 04    LDY #&04 ; range
&9dd2 20 c5 32 JSR &32c5 ; keep_within_range
&9dd5 85 11    STA &11 ; this_object_state (lightning size)
&9dd7 20 bb 32 JSR &32bb ; make_positive
&9dda 69 6c    ADC #&6c ; SPRITE_LIGHTNING_QUARTER - 1              # Use SPRITE_LIGHTNING_QUARTER to SPRITE_NEST
&9ddc 20 ff 32 JSR &32ff ; change_object_sprite_to_A
&9ddf a5 06    LDA &06 ; this_object_frame_counter
&9de1 4a       LSR A
&9de2 66 39    ROR &39 ; this_object_y_flip                         # Flip lightning vertically every frame
&9de4 4a       LSR A
&9de5 66 37    ROR &37 ; this_object_x_flip                         # Flip lighting horizontally every two frames
&9de7 c6 42    DEC &42 ; this_object_acceleration_y                 # No gravity for lightning
; use_previous_velocities
&9de9 a5 44    LDA &44 ; this_object_previous_velocity_x
&9deb 85 43    STA &43 ; this_object_velocity_x
&9ded a5 46    LDA &46 ; this_object_previous_velocity_y
&9def 85 45    STA &45 ; this_object_velocity_y
&9df1 60       RTS
; to_set_object_for_removal
&9df2 4c 58 25 JMP &2558 ; set_object_for_removal

# Inactive grenades (OBJECT_INACTIVE_GRENADE)
# ===========================================
# objects_energy is used for disturbance
# objects_state is used to note if the grenade has ever been held by the player

; update_inactive_grenade
&9df5 20 47 a8 JSR &a847 ; consider_disturbing_object               # Grenades are static until touched by another object
&9df8 20 bf 0b JSR &0bbf ; check_if_object_fired
&9dfb f0 0b    BEQ &9e08 ; was_fired
&9dfd c5 dd    CMP &dd ; player_object_held
&9dff d0 03    BNE &9e04 ; not_held                                 # Note if the player has held the inactivate grenade
&9e01 85 11    STA &11 ; this_object_state (inactive grenade held)  # Set to non-zero to indicate player has held grenade
; leave
&9e03 60       RTS
; not_held
&9e04 a5 11    LDA &11 ; this_object_state (inactive grenade held)  # Non-zero if player has ever held grenade
&9e06 f0 fb    BEQ &9e03 ; leave                                    # If the grenade was held, activate it when dropped
; was_fired                                                         
&9e08 a9 12    LDA #&12 ; OBJECT_ACTIVE_GRENADE
&9e0a 4c ed 32 JMP &32ed ; change_object_type

# Gargoyles (OBJECT_GARGOYLE)
# ===========================
# Tertiary data byte is as follows:
#
# 8....... if set, gargoyle needs primary object creating
# .4218421 gargoyle type (sets projectile and firing frequency)

; update_gargoyle                                                   # Called with X = this_object_data
&9e0d bd 28 9e LDA &9e28,X ; gargoyles_projectile_frequency_table
&9e10 25 06    AND &06 ; this_object_frame_counter
&9e12 d0 0f    BNE &9e23 ; skip_firing
&9e14 bc 32 9e LDY &9e32,X ; gargoyles_projectile_velocity_y_table
&9e17 bd 2d 9e LDA &9e2d,X ; gargoyles_projectile_velocity_x_table
&9e1a 48       PHA ; x velocity
&9e1b bd 37 9e LDA &9e37,X ; gargoyles_projectile_type_table
&9e1e aa       TAX ; type
&9e1f 68       PLA ; x velocity
&9e20 20 12 34 JSR &3412 ; create_projectile
; skip_firing
&9e23 a0 5a    LDY #&5a                                             # Gargoyles have a minimum energy of 90
&9e25 4c a1 35 JMP &35a1 ; gain_energy_Y_and_flash_if_damaged

; gargoyles_projectile_frequency_table                              # Types 0 and 2 are unused
;      0  1  2  3  4
&9e28 0f 07 07 07 03

; gargoyles_projectile_velocity_x_table
;      0  1  2  3  4
&9e2d 11 7f 7f 7f 01

; gargoyles_projectile_velocity_y_table
;      0  1  2  3  4
&9e32 c0 0c 04 f9 9a

; gargoyles_projectile_type_table
;      0  1  2  3  4
&9e37 32 19 19 19 32                                                # &19 = OBJECT_PLASMA_BALL, &32 = OBJECT_LIGHTNING

# Maggot machine (OBJECT_MAGGOT_MACHINE)
# ======================================

; update_maggot_machine
&9e3c a5 c0    LDA &c0 ; frame_counter
&9e3e 29 3f    AND #&3f
&9e40 aa       TAX
&9e41 d0 17    BNE &9e5a ; consider_flashing_maggot_machine         # Every sixty four frames,
&9e43 a5 55    LDA &55 ; this_object_y
&9e45 cd e6 14 CMP &14e6 ; waterline_y
&9e48 90 0a    BCC &9e54 ; not_underwater                           # Is the maggot machine underwater?
&9e4a a9 80    LDA #&80                                             # Maggot machine explodes with 128 energy
&9e4c 8d 1f 08 STA &081f ; earthquake_state                         # Set to negative to start earthquake
&9e4f 8d 1e 08 STA &081e ; flooding_state                           # Set to negative to start flooding
&9e52 30 2e    BMI &9e82 ; flash_background_and_explode             # Always branches
; not_underwater
&9e54 20 ca 14 JSR &14ca ; play_squeal                              # Play squeal for maggot machine
&9e57 20 65 31 JSR &3165 ; flip_this_object_horizontally            # Flip maggot machine
;                                                                   # Maggots are created at &25aa
; consider_flashing_maggot_machine
&9e5a e0 08    CPX #&08                                             # Flash for 8 out of 64 frames
&9e5c 4c 89 aa JMP &aa89 ; use_damaged_palette_if_carry_clear

# Coronium crystal OBJECT_CORONIUM_CRYSTAL)
# =========================================
# objects_timer is used for lifespan
#
# Coronium boulder (OBJECT_CORONIUM_BOULDER)
# ==========================================

; update_coronium_crystal                                           # Called with Y = object touching, or negative if none
&9e5f a9 0a    LDA #&0a
&9e61 e6 12    INC &12 ; this_object_timer (coronium timer)         # Coronium crystals explode with duration 10 after 64 frames
&9e63 e6 12    INC &12 ; this_object_timer (coronium timer)
&9e65 30 1e    BMI &9e85 ; to_explode_object_with_duration_A
; update_coronium_boulder                                           # Called with Y = object touching, or negative if none
&9e67 98       TYA
&9e68 30 1e    BMI &9e88 ; not_touching_other_object
&9e6a f0 26    BEQ &9e92 ; is_touching_player
&9e6c b9 60 08 LDA &0860,Y ; objects_type
&9e6f c9 55    CMP #&55 ; OBJECT_CORONIUM_BOULDER
&9e71 f0 04    BEQ &9e77 ; coronium_explosion
&9e73 c9 58    CMP #&58 ; OBJECT_CORONIUM_CRYSTAL
&9e75 d0 11    BNE &9e88 ; not_touching_other_object
; coronium_explosion
&9e77 20 45 25 JSR &2545 ; set_object_for_removal                   # Remove object touching
&9e7a 20 53 1e JSR &1e53 ; get_object_weight                        # Get weight of object touching
&9e7d 65 38    ADC &38 ; this_object_weight                         # Add to this object's weight
&9e7f 0a       ASL A
&9e80 69 03    ADC #&03                                             # Coronium explodes with duration (weight * 2) + 3
;                                                                   # OBJECT_CORONIUM_BOULDER has weight 5
;                                                                   # OBJECT_CORONIUM_CRYSTAL has weight 2
;                                                                   # Two boulders              : explosion duration 23
;                                                                   # One boulder + one crystal : explosion duration 17
;                                                                   # Two crystals              : explosion duration 11
; flash_background_and_explode
&9e82 20 c5 1f JSR &1fc5 ; flash_background
; to_explode_object_with_duration_A
&9e85 4c 78 9d JMP &9d78 ; explode_object_with_duration_A
; not_touching_other_object
&9e88 a5 da    LDA &da ; rnd_state + 1
&9e8a 29 c0    AND #&c0                                             # 1 in 4 chance of radiation damage if holding
&9e8c 05 dd    ORA &dd ; player_object_held
&9e8e c5 aa    CMP &aa ; this_object
&9e90 d0 0e    BNE &9ea0 ; skip_radiation_damage
; is_touching_player
&9e92 ad 18 08 LDA &0818 ; player_radiation_immunity_pill_collected # Negative if radiation immunity pill collected
&9e95 05 20    ORA &20 ; this_object_waterline                      # Negative if the coronium is completely underwater
&9e97 30 07    BMI &9ea0 ; skip_radiation_damage                    # If the player doesn't have immunity,
&9e99 a9 08    LDA #&08
&9e9b a0 00    LDY #&00 ; OBJECT_SLOT_PLAYER
&9e9d 20 d5 24 JSR &24d5 ; damage_object                            # Coronium causes 8 damage to player
; skip_radiation_damage
&9ea0 20 b6 25 JSR &25b6 ; rnd                                      # Use random palette for coronium
&9ea3 4a       LSR A                                                # Clear top bit to use background plotting
&9ea4 85 73    STA &73 ; this_object_palette
&9ea6 60       RTS

# Worms (OBJECT_WORM)
# ===================

; update_worm
&9ea7 a9 86    LDA #&86 ; OBJECT_RED_FROGMAN | &80                  # Set &80 to target player too
&9ea9 a0 07    LDY #&07 ; OBJECT_GREEN_FROGMAN                      # Worms avoid green frogmen
&9eab a2 00    LDX #&00                                             # Worm causes no damage
&9ead 20 08 ab JSR &ab08 ; update_worm_or_maggot
&9eb0 4c 78 3c JMP &3c78 ; avoid_target                             # Worms avoid green frogmen and player

# Alien weapon (OBJECT_ALIEN_WEAPON)

&9eb3 20 7d 25 JSR &257d ; increase_energy_by_one_if_not_zero

&9ebf 20 10 34 JSR &3410 ; create_projectile_with_zero_velocity_y
&9ec2 b0 60    BCS &9f24 ; leave
&9ec4 4c c2 14 JMP &14c2 ; play_low_beep                            # Play low beep for firing alien weapon

# Green slimes (OBJECT_GREEN_SLIME)
# =================================
# Spawned from nests.
#
# objects_state is used for NPC behaviour and walking
# objects_timer is used for sprite offset

; update_green_slime
&9ec7 20 a7 25 JSR &25a7 ; consider_flipping_object_to_match_velocity_x
&9eca a2 08    LDX #&08 ; npc stimuli type
&9ecc 20 f8 27 JSR &27f8 ; check_for_npc_stimuli
&9ecf 20 8d 3d JSR &3d8d ; consider_updating_npc_path
&9ed2 20 31 2a JSR &2a31 ; consider_npc_burrowing
&9ed5 46 21    LSR &21 ; stimuli                                    # &01 set if green slime absorbed coronium crystal
&9ed7 90 0c    BCC &9ee5 ; skip_converting_to_yellow_slime
&9ed9 a9 0b    LDA #&0b ; OBJECT_YELLOW_SLIME
; change_slime_type
&9edb 20 0f 14 JSR &140f ; play_sound
&9ede b0 24 b6 e2                                                   # Play sound for slime changing colour
&9ee2 4c ed 32 JMP &32ed ; change_object_type
; skip_converting_to_yellow_slime
&9ee5 a2 03    LDX #&03 ; npc walking type
&9ee7 a9 0c    LDA #&0c ; speed
&9ee9 20 46 3b JSR &3b46 ; update_walking_npc_and_check_for_obstacles_with_speed_A
&9eec 20 f3 3b JSR &3bf3 ; check_if_player_or_npc_jumping           # Returns carry set if jumping
&9eef 90 04    BCC &9ef5 ; not_jumping
&9ef1 a9 0f    LDA #&0f                                             # Use SPRITE_BOULDER if green slime is jumping
&9ef3 85 12    STA &12 ; this_object_timer (sprite offset)
; not_jumping
&9ef5 a9 11    LDA #&11 ; modulus
&9ef7 20 84 25 JSR &2584 ; update_sprite_offset_using_velocities    # Returns A = sprite offset, carry clear
&9efa e9 08    SBC #&08
&9efc 20 bd 32 JSR &32bd ; invert_if_negative
&9eff 4a       LSR A
&9f00 4c f9 32 JMP &32f9 ; change_object_sprite_to_base_plus_A      # Use SPRITE_SLIME_ONE to SPRITE_SLIME_FOUR otherwise

# Yellow slimes (OBJECT_YELLOW_SLIME)
# ===================================
# Created by feeding green slimes coronium crystals.
#
# objects_timer is used as timer for conversion back to green slime

; update_yellow_slime                                               # Called with Y = object touching, or negative if none
&9f03 d0 02    BNE &9f07 ; skip_resetting_timer
&9f05 84 12    STY &12 ; this_object_timer (yellow slime waking)    # If held by player, set to zero to reset timer
; skip_resetting_timer
&9f07 a5 da    LDA &da ; rnd_state + 1
&9f09 25 1b    AND &1b ; this_object_tile_top_or_bottom_collision   # &80 set if object hit tiles above or below
&9f0b 10 08    BPL &9f15 ; skip_converting_to_green_slime           # If the yellow slime is resting on a surface,
&9f0d e6 12    INC &12 ; this_object_timer (yellow slime waking)    # increase the timer
&9f0f d0 04    BNE &9f15 ; skip_converting_to_green_slime           # When timer overflows,
&9f11 a9 0a    LDA #&0a ; OBJECT_GREEN_SLIME                        # convert back to green slime
&9f13 d0 c6    BNE &9edb ; change_slime_type                        # Always branches
; skip_converting_to_green_slime
&9f15 a2 3c    LDX #&3c ; ywY
&9f17 a5 dc    LDA &dc ; rnd_state + 3
&9f19 4a       LSR A
&9f1a 09 80    ORA #&80
&9f1c c5 12    CMP &12 ; this_object_timer (yellow slime waking)
&9f1e b0 02    BCS &9f22 ; skip_flashing_green
&9f20 a2 39    LDX #&39 ; gwY                                       # Flash green increasingly as timer increases
; skip_flashing_green
&9f22 86 73    STX &73 ; this_object_palette
; leave
&9f24 60       RTS

# Fluffy (OBJECT_FLUFFY)
# ======================
# objects_state is used for NPC behaviour and walking
# objects_timer is used for animation

; update_fluffy
&9f25 a0 29    LDY #&29                                             # Fluffy has a minimum energy of 41
&9f27 20 95 35 JSR &3595 ; give_object_minimum_energy
&9f2a a2 06    LDX #&06 ; npc stimuli type
&9f2c 20 f8 27 JSR &27f8 ; check_for_npc_stimuli
&9f2f 20 8d 3d JSR &3d8d ; consider_updating_npc_path
&9f32 20 6b 25 JSR &256b ; check_if_object_was_damaged              # Returns carry set if object has just taken >= 8 damage
&9f35 b0 24    BCS &9f5b ; is_squealing                             # Squeal if damaged
&9f37 a5 11    LDA &11 ; this_object_state (behaviour and walking)
&9f39 29 c0    AND #&c0 ; NPC_MOOD_MASK
&9f3b c9 80    CMP #&80
&9f3d f0 1c    BEQ &9f5b ; is_squealing                             # Squeal if &80 (NPC_MOOD_MINUS_TWO)
&9f3f a5 aa    LDA &aa ; this_object
&9f41 b0 02    BCS &9f45 ; skip_clearing_target                     # Branch if &c0 (NPC_MOOD_MINUS_ONE)
; is_not_negative_mood                                              # If &00 (NPC_MOOD_ZERO) or &40 (NPC_MOOD_PLUS_ONE),
&9f43 85 0e    STA &0e ; this_object_target_object                  # Set to same object to not seek target
; skip_clearing_target
&9f45 a5 06    LDA &06 ; this_object_frame_counter
&9f47 29 0b    AND #&0b ; 1011
&9f49 d0 2d    BNE &9f78 ; consider_animating_fluffy                # Every eight frames, check for enemies
&9f4b a9 2a    LDA #&2a ; OBJECT_RED_YELLOW_IMP
&9f4d a0 86    LDY #&86 ; OBJECT_RANGE_FLYING_ENEMIES
&9f4f 20 93 3c JSR &3c93 ; find_object_ignoring_obstructions        # Returns positive if object found
&9f52 30 12    BMI &9f66 ; skip_squealing                           # If an enemy is found,
&9f54 ad 8f 3c LDA &3c8f ; nearest_object_distance
&9f57 c5 da    CMP &da ; rnd_state + 1                              # squeal with greater likelihood if nearer
&9f59 b0 0b    BCS &9f66 ; skip_squealing
; is_squealing
&9f5b 20 0f 14 JSR &140f ; play_sound
&9f5e b0 24 b6 e2                                                   # Play sound for Fluffy squealing; returns carry set
&9f62 66 12    ROR &12 ; this_object_timer (fluffy active)          # Set &80 to make Fluffy active
&9f64 30 12    BMI &9f78 ; consider_animating_fluffy
; skip_squealing
&9f66 a5 11    LDA &11 ; this_object_state (behaviour and walking)  #    &80 &c0 &00 &40
&9f68 20 b3 32 JSR &32b3 ; invert_if_positive                       # -> &80 &c0 &00 &c0
&9f6b c5 da    CMP &da ; rnd_state + 1
&9f6d 66 12    ROR &12 ; this_object_timer (fluffy active)          # i.e. animate more when happy or unhappy
&9f6f 10 07    BPL &9f78 ; consider_animating_fluffy                # Don't purr if not active
&9f71 20 0f 14 JSR &140f ; play_sound
&9f74 c7 81 c1 f3                                                   # Play sound for Fluffy purring
; consider_animating_fluffy
&9f78 a5 da    LDA &da ; rnd_state + 1
&9f7a 29 02    AND #&02                                             # 0 for x, 2 for y
&9f7c aa       TAX
&9f7d a5 12    LDA &12 ; this_object_timer (fluffy active)          # If Fluffy is active,
&9f7f 55 37    EOR &37,X ; this_object_x_flip                       # Flip horizontally or vertically at random
&9f81 95 37    STA &37,X ; this_object_x_flip
&9f83 25 12    AND &12 ; this_object_timer (fluffy active)
&9f85 10 3b    BPL &9fc2 ; leave
&9f87 a5 dd    LDA &dd ; player_object_held
&9f89 45 aa    EOR &aa ; this_object
&9f8b f0 35    BEQ &9fc2 ; leave                                    # Don't move Fluffy if held by player
; not_held
&9f8d a2 02    LDX #&02 ; npc walking type
&9f8f a9 28    LDA #&28 ; speed
&9f91 4c 46 3b JMP &3b46 ; update_walking_npc_and_check_for_obstacles_with_speed_A

# Active grenades (OBJECT_ACTIVE_GRENADE)
# =======================================
# objects_timer is used as timer for detonation

; update_active_grenade
&9f94 20 bf 0b JSR &0bbf ; check_if_object_fired
&9f97 d0 09    BNE &9fa2 ; not_fired
; was_fired
&9f99 a9 00    LDA #&00
&9f9b 85 12    STA &12 ; this_object_timer (active grenade timer)
&9f9d a9 50    LDA #&50 ; OBJECT_INACTIVE_GRENADE
&9f9f 4c ed 32 JMP &32ed ; change_object_type
; not_fired

&9fa4 a6 15    LDX &15 ; this_object_energy
&9fa6 f0 08    BEQ &9fb0 ; to_explode_object_with_duration_A
&9fa8 a5 12    LDA &12 ; this_object_timer (active grenade timer)
&9faa c9 60    CMP #&60                                             # Grenades explode after 96 frames
&9fac 90 05    BCC &9fb3 ; skip_exploding_grenade
&9fae a9 10    LDA #&10 ; explosion duration                        # Grenades explode with duration 16 on detonation
; to_explode_object_with_duration_A
&9fb0 4c 78 9d JMP &9d78 ; explode_object_with_duration_A
; skip_exploding_grenade
&9fb3 e6 12    INC &12 ; this_object_timer (active grenade timer)
&9fb5 20 7e aa JSR &aa7e ; rotate_colour_from_A                     # Animate colour of active grenade
&9fb8 8a       TXA                                                  # Returns X = 0 once every sixteen frames
&9fb9 d0 07    BNE &9fc2 ; skip_sound
&9fbb 20 0f 14 JSR &140f ; play_sound
&9fbe 57 07 cb 82                                                   # Play sound for active grenade
; skip_sound
&9fc2 60       RTS

# Full flask (OBJECT_FULL_FLASK)
# ==============================
# objects_timer is used for emptying

; update_full_flask                                                 # Called with Y = object touching, or negative if none
&a04b 30 07    BMI &a054 ; not_touching_other_object
&a04d 20 1d 3c JSR &3c1d ; get_maximum_of_this_object_velocities    # Returns A = larger of x and y velocities
&a050 c9 0a    CMP #&0a
&a052 b0 06    BCS &a05a ; start_emptying_flask                     # If the flask hit another object too quickly,
; not_touching_other_object
&a054 a5 1d    LDA &1d ; this_object_pre_collision_velocity_magnitude
&a056 c9 14    CMP #&14
&a058 90 04    BCC &a05e ; skip_starting_timer                      # or hit a tile too quickly,
; start_emptying_flask
&a05a a9 10    LDA #&10
&a05c 85 12    STA &12 ; this_object_timer (full flask emptying)    # Set to non-zero to start emptying it
; not_disturbed
&a05e a5 12    LDA &12 ; this_object_timer (full flask emptying)    # Non-zero if emptying
&a060 f0 e8    BEQ &a04a ; leave
&a062 a4 3b    LDY &3b ; this_object_touching

&a06b d0 03    BNE &a070 ; not_extinguishing_fireball
&a06d 20 45 25 JSR &2545 ; set_object_for_removal                   # Remove the fireball
; not_extinguishing_fireball
&a070 a9 c0    LDA #&c0                                             # Particles move upwards
&a072 85 b5    STA &b5 ; angle
&a074 a0 58    LDY #&58 ; PARTICLE_FLASK
&a076 a9 08    LDA #&08
&a078 20 c1 21 JSR &21c1 ; add_particles                            # Add 8 water particles
&a07b c6 12    DEC &12 ; this_object_timer (full flask emptying)
&a07d d0 cb    BNE &a04a ; leave
&a07f a9 4c    LDA #&4c ; OBJECT_EMPTY_FLASK
; to_change_object_type
&a081 4c ed 32 JMP &32ed ; change_object_type

# Hovering balls (OBJECT_HOVERING_BALL)

; update_invisible_hovering_ball                                    # Called with Y = object touching, or negative if none

&a099 33 03 85 02                                                   # Play sound for hovering ball damaging object
; not_touching_other_object

&a09f 29 04    AND #&04
&a0a1 85 15    STA &15 ; this_object_energy
&a0a3 c6 12    DEC &12 ; this_object_timer (hovering ball ttl)
&a0a5 d0 0b    BNE &a0b2 ; move_hovering_ball                       # Hovering balls disappear after 256 frames
&a0a7 20 3a 2a JSR &2a3a ; set_object_as_far_away                   # Will return hovering ball to nest
; play_sound_for_teleporting
&a0aa 20 0f 14 JSR &140f ; play_sound
&a0ad 29 c2 37 f3                                                   # Play sound for object teleporting
&a0b1 60       RTS

# Pistol bullets (OBJECT_PISTOL_BULLET)
# =====================================

; update_pistol_bullet                                              # Called with Y = object touching, or negative if none
&a0b8 20 e2 1f JSR &1fe2 ; check_if_object_Y_damaged_by_projectiles # Returns negative if not, or Y = object
&a0bb 30 14    BMI &a0d1 ; move_bullet
&a0bd a9 0a    LDA #&0a
&a0bf 20 d5 24 JSR &24d5 ; damage_object                            # Pistol bullets cause 10 damage
; explode_bullet
&a0c2 20 0d 14 JSR &140d ; play_sound_on_channel_zero
&a0c5 17 03 1b 02                                                   # Play sound for exploding bullet
&a0c9 20 63 a7 JSR &a763 ; turn_object_into_fireball_of_duration_two
&a0cc a9 02    LDA #&02 ; explosion duration
&a0ce 4c 7f 9d JMP &9d7f ; explode_object_with_duration_A_but_no_sound

; move_bullet
&a0d1 20 4e 25 JSR &254e ; reduce_energy_by_one                     # Bullets have a limited lifespan
&a0d4 f0 ec    BEQ &a0c2 ; explode_bullet
&a0d6 24 1b    BIT &1b ; this_object_tile_top_or_bottom_collision   # &80 set if object hit tiles above or below
&a0d8 10 0a    BPL &a0e4 ; no_collision
&a0da c9 3e    CMP #&3e
&a0dc b0 e4    BCS &a0c2 ; explode_bullet                           # Bullets explode when hitting tiles that are close
&a0de e9 14    SBC #&14
&a0e0 90 e0    BCC &a0c2 ; explode_bullet                           # and when hitting tiles that are far away
&a0e2 85 15    STA &15 ; this_object_energy                         # Otherwise, collision reduces bullet's lifespan
; no_collision
&a0e4 20 ff 22 JSR &22ff ; calculate_angle_from_this_object_velocities
&a0e7 85 39    STA &39 ; this_object_y_flip (bullet angle)          # &80 set if bullet moving up
;                                                                   # Flip vertically if moving up
&a0e9 24 39    BIT &39 ; this_object_y_flip (bullet angle)          # &40 set if bullet moving left
&a0eb 50 02    BVC &a0ef ; is_moving_left
; is_moving_left
&a0ed 49 ff    EOR #&ff                                             # Flip horizontally if moving left
; is_moving_right
&a0ef 85 37    STA &37 ; this_object_x_flip
&a0f1 29 7f    AND #&7f ; ~180 degrees
&a0f3 4a       LSR A                                                # Divide angle by 8 (11.25 degrees) to give &00 - &0f
&a0f4 4a       LSR A
&a0f5 4a       LSR A
&a0f6 c9 04    CMP #&04                                             # If &00,        use &00 : SPRITE_BULLET_HORIZONTAL
;                                                                   #    &01,        use &01 : SPRITE_BULLET_TWENTY_TWO
;                                                                   #    &02,        use &02 : SPRITE_BULLET_FORTY_FIVE
;                                                                   #    &03,        use &03 : SPRITE_BULLET_SIXTY
&a0f8 90 03    BCC &a0fd ; to_change_object_sprite_to_base_plus_A
&a0fa 4a       LSR A
&a0fb 49 06    EOR #&06                                             # If &04 or &05, use &04 : SPRITE_BULLET_SEVENTY_FIVE
;                                                                   # If &06 or &07, use &05 : SPRITE_BULLET_VERTICAL
;                                                                   # If &08 or &09, use &02 : SPRITE_BULLET_FORTY_FIVE
;                                                                   # If &0a or &0b, use &03 : SPRITE_BULLET_SIXTY
;                                                                   # If &0c or &0d, use &00 : SPRITE_BULLET_HORIZONTAL
;                                                                   # If &0e or &0f, use &01 : SPRITE_BULLET_TWENTY_TWO
; to_change_object_sprite_to_base_plus_A
&a0fd 4c f9 32 JMP &32f9 ; change_object_sprite_to_base_plus_A      # Use SPRITE_BULLET_HORIZONTAL to SPRITE_BULLET_VERTICAL

# Red frogman (OBJECT_RED_FROGMAN)
# Green frogmen (OBJECT_GREEN_FROGMAN)
# Invisible frogman (OBJECT_INVISIBLE_FROGMAN)
# ============================================
# Spawned from nests. Red frogman doesn't return.
#
# objects_state is used for NPC behaviour and walking
# objects_timer is used for jump cooldown

; update_red_frogman
&a100 a2 09    LDX #&09 ; npc stimuli type
&a102 20 f8 27 JSR &27f8 ; check_for_npc_stimuli
&a105 a9 33    LDA #&33 ; OBJECT_RED_MUSHROOM_BALL                  # Red frogman avoids red mushroom balls
&a107 a8       TAY
&a108 20 73 3c JSR &3c73 ; avoid_object_type_Y
&a10b 20 8d 3d JSR &3d8d ; consider_updating_npc_path
&a10e a0 64    LDY #&64                                             # Red frogman has a minimum energy of 100
&a110 d0 15    BNE &a127 ; set_frogman_minimum_energy               # Always branches

; update_invisible_frogman
&a112 46 2b    LSR &2b ; this_object_visibility                     # Clear top bit to make invisible frogman invisible
; update_green_frogman
&a114 a6 3b    LDX &3b ; this_object_touching
&a116 d0 0d    BNE &a125 ; not_touching_player
; is_touching_player
&a118 20 a2 9c JSR &9ca2 ; add_to_player_mushroom_timer             # X = 0, so treat cyan and green frogmen as red mushrooms
&a11b a9 07    LDA #&07
&a11d 85 12    STA &12 ; this_object_timer (npc jump cooldown)      # Suppress jumping and animate kicking legs for 7 frames
&a11f 0a       ASL A
&a120 a0 00    LDY #&00 ; OBJECT_SLOT_PLAYER
&a122 20 d5 24 JSR &24d5 ; damage_object                            # Cyan and green frogmen cause 14 damage to player
; not_touching_player
&a125 a0 5a    LDY #&5a                                             # Cyan and green frogmen have a minimum energy of 90
; set_frogman_minimum_energy
&a127 20 95 35 JSR &3595 ; give_object_minimum_energy
&a12a a0 14    LDY #&14 ; 1010
&a12c 84 04    STY &04 ; walking_speed
&a12e a2 01    LDX #&01 ; npc walking type
&a130 20 6f 3b JSR &3b6f ; update_walking_npc
&a133 20 a7 25 JSR &25a7 ; consider_flipping_object_to_match_velocity_x
&a136 20 ed 3b JSR &3bed ; check_if_npc_can_walk                    # Returns carry set if not on walkable surface
&a139 b0 0e    BCS &a149 ; consider_starting_jump_if_not_underwater
&a13b 20 14 3c JSR &3c14 ; check_if_slope_is_too_sleep_for_npc      # Returns A = absolute tile_collision_angle
&a13e c9 28    CMP #&28 ; 56.25 degrees
&a140 90 0f    BCC &a151 ; consider_starting_jump
&a142 20 9c 25 JSR &259c ; set_npc_facing_tile_collision
&a145 a2 ff    LDX #&ff                                             # Negative to suppress jumping if slope too steep
&a147 d0 0a    BNE &a153 ; consider_starting_jump_with_X            # Always branches
; consider_starting_jump_if_not_underwater
&a149 24 1f    BIT &1f ; this_object_in_water                       # Positive if object is in any water
&a14b 30 2c    BMI &a179 ; skip_starting_jump                       # Don't jump if not in water
&a14d a5 07    LDA &07 ; this_object_frame_counter_sixteen
&a14f d0 28    BNE &a179 ; skip_starting_jump                       # Every sixteen frames,
; consider_starting_jump
&a151 a2 04    LDX #&04
; consider_starting_jump_with_X
&a153 a5 12    LDA &12 ; this_object_timer (npc jump cooldown)
&a155 d0 22    BNE &a179 ; skip_starting_jump                       # Don't jump if jumped or touched player recently
&a157 a9 09    LDA #&09
&a159 2c 14 2a BIT &2a14 ; this_object_object_collision_y_flags     # &80 set if collision to bottom from other objects
&a15c 30 10    BMI &a16e ; set_frogman_jumping_with_speed_A         # If so, jump with speed 9 * 4
&a15e a5 04    LDA &04 ; walking_speed
&a160 4a       LSR A
&a161 4a       LSR A
&a162 a4 db    LDY &db ; rnd_state + 2
&a164 c0 20    CPY #&20                                             # 7 in 8 chance of jumping with speed 4 * 4
&a166 b0 07    BCS &a16f ; set_frogman_jumping_with_speed_X
&a168 c0 0a    CPY #&0a                                             # 22 in 256 chance of jumping with speed 20 * 4
&a16a b0 02    BCS &a16e ; set_frogman_jumping_with_speed_A
&a16c 69 05    ADC #&05                                             # 10 in 256 chance of jumping with speed 25 * 4
; set_frogman_jumping_with_speed_A
&a16e aa       TAX
; set_frogman_jumping_with_speed_X
&a16f 85 12    STA &12 ; this_object_timer (npc jump cooldown)
&a171 8a       TXA
&a172 30 05    BMI &a179 ; skip_starting_jump
&a174 0a       ASL A
&a175 0a       ASL A
&a176 20 c2 3a JSR &3ac2 ; set_npc_jumping_with_speed_A
; skip_starting_jump
; animate_sprite_from_timer                                         # Also used for worms and maggots
&a179 a5 12    LDA &12 ; this_object_timer (npc jump cooldown)
&a17b 18       CLC                                                  # Use SPRITE_FROGMAN_ONE
&a17c f0 0b    BEQ &a189 ; to_change_object_sprite_to_base_plus_A_plus_carry
&a17e c6 12    DEC &12 ; this_object_timer (npc jump cooldown)
&a180 a5 12    LDA &12 ; this_object_timer (npc jump cooldown)      # unless kicking legs immediately after jump
&a182 f0 05    BEQ &a189 ; to_change_object_sprite_to_base_plus_A_plus_carry
&a184 4a       LSR A                                                # 84218421 -> .8421842
&a185 4a       LSR A                                                #          -> ..842184
&a186 29 01    AND #&01                                             #          -> .......4
;                                                                   # Use SPRITE_FROGMAN_TWO or SPRITE_FROGMAN_THREE
&a188 38       SEC                                                  # Set carry to add one to sprite
; to_change_object_sprite_to_base_plus_A_plus_carry
&a189 4c fa 32 JMP &32fa ; change_object_sprite_to_base_plus_A_plus_carry

Red/magenta imps (OBJECT_RED_MAGENTA_IMP)
Red/yellow imps (OBJECT_RED_YELLOW_IMP)

# Spawned from pipes.
#
# objects_state is used for NPC behaviour and walking
# objects_timer is used for sprite offset

; update_imp
&a18c a5 6f    LDA &6f ; this_object_flags
&a18e 29 04    AND #&04 ; OBJECT_FLAG_NEWLY_CREATED
&a190 f0 04    BEQ &a196 ; not_newly_created
&a192 a9 80    LDA #&80 ; NPC_MOOD_MINUS_TWO                        # Set mood of newly spawned imps
&a194 85 11    STA &11 ; this_object_state (behaviour and walking)
; not_newly_created
&a196 a0 28    LDY #&28                                             # Imps move with speed &28 if in non-zero mood
&a198 a5 11    LDA &11 ; this_object_state (behaviour and walking)
&a19a 0a       ASL A                                                # Set &80 if NPC_MOOD_MINUS_ONE or NPC_MOOD_PLUS_ONE
&a19b 45 11    EOR &11 ; this_object_state (behaviour and walking)  # Set &80 if NPC_MOOD_MINUS_TWO
&a19d 30 02    BMI &a1a1 ; not_zero_mood
&a19f a0 10    LDY #&10                                             # Otherwise, imps move with speed &10
; not_zero_mood
&a1a1 84 04    STY &04 ; walking_speed
&a1a3 a5 41    LDA &41 ; this_object_type
&a1a5 38       SEC
&a1a6 e9 29    SBC #&29 ; OBJECT_RED_MAGENTA_IMP                    # Convert object type into NPC stimuli type
&a1a8 aa       TAX ; npc stimuli type
&a1a9 a5 11    LDA &11 ; this_object_state (behaviour and walking)
&a1ab 29 10    AND #&10 ; NPC_WAS_FED
&a1ad 08       PHP ; not equal if was fed
&a1ae a5 08    LDA &08 ; tile_type
&a1b0 c9 0a    CMP #&0a ; TILE_PIPE
&a1b2 d0 2b    BNE &a1df ; not_home
&a1b4 46 04    LSR &04 ; walking_speed                              # Slow imps down near pipe
&a1b6 46 04    LSR &04 ; walking_speed
&a1b8 20 bb 22 JSR &22bb ; get_this_object_centre
&a1bb a5 87    LDA &87 ; this_object_centre_x_fraction
&a1bd 20 bd 32 JSR &32bd ; invert_if_negative
&a1c0 c9 68    CMP #&68
&a1c2 90 1b    BCC &a1df ; not_home
&a1c4 24 18    BIT &18 ; this_object_tile_collision_y_flags         # &80 set if collision to bottom, i.e. imp touching pipe
&a1c6 10 17    BPL &a1df ; not_home
&a1c8 28       PLP ; not equal if was fed
&a1c9 f0 11    BEQ &a1dc ; skip_spawning_gift
&a1cb de 3a 08 DEC &083a,X ; imp_types_gifts_remaining
&a1ce 30 0c    BMI &a1dc ; skip_spawning_gift
&a1d0 bd 0e 32 LDA &320e,X ; imp_types_gift_table
&a1d3 aa       TAX                                                  # A = x velocity, X =type
&a1d4 a0 c8    LDY #&c8 ; -&38                                      # Y = y velocity
&a1d6 20 12 34 JSR &3412 ; create_projectile
&a1d9 20 ca 14 JSR &14ca ; play_squeal                              # Play squeal for imp spawning gift
; skip_spawning_gift
&a1dc 4c 3a 2a JMP &2a3a ; set_object_as_far_away
; not_home
&a1df bc 09 32 LDY &3209,X ; imp_types_minimum_energy_table
&a1e2 20 95 35 JSR &3595 ; give_object_minimum_energy               # Imps have a minimum energy depending on type
&a1e5 20 f8 27 JSR &27f8 ; check_for_npc_stimuli                    # Called with X = npc stimuli type
&a1e8 28       PLP ; not equal if was fed
&a1e9 d0 04    BNE &a1ef ; was_fed
&a1eb 46 21    LSR &21 ; stimuli                                    # &01 set if imp fed
&a1ed 90 08    BCC &a1f7 ; not_fed
; was_fed
&a1ef a5 11    LDA &11 ; this_object_state (behaviour and walking)
&a1f1 29 3f    AND #&3f                                             # Clear existing mood
&a1f3 09 90    ORA #&90 ; NPC_MOOD_MINUS_TWO | NPC_WAS_FED
&a1f5 85 11    STA &11 ; this_object_state (behaviour and walking)
; not_fed
&a1f7 20 8d 3d JSR &3d8d ; consider_updating_npc_path
&a1fa a2 02    LDX #&02 ; npc walking type
&a1fc 20 48 3b JSR &3b48 ; update_walking_npc_and_check_for_obstacles
&a1ff a4 3b    LDY &3b ; this_object_touching
&a201 c4 0e    CPY &0e ; this_object_target_object
&a203 d0 16    BNE &a21b ; skip_attacking_target
&a205 24 11    BIT &11 ; this_object_state (behaviour and walking)
&a207 30 12    BMI &a21b ; skip_attacking_target
&a209 20 3c 3c JSR &3c3c ; check_object_touching_angle              # Returns positive if object can be picked up
&a20c 30 0d    BMI &a21b ; skip_attacking_target
&a20e a4 3b    LDY &3b ; this_object_touching
&a210 a9 05    LDA #&05
&a212 20 d5 24 JSR &24d5 ; damage_object                            # Imps cause 5 damage
&a215 20 b4 0b JSR &0bb4 ; set_this_object_velocities_from_object_Y # Imp follows touching object when damaging it
&a218 4c 3c a2 JMP &a23c ; is_at_target
; skip_attacking_target
&a21b 20 f3 3b JSR &3bf3 ; check_if_player_or_npc_jumping           # Returns carry set if jumping
&a21e b0 30    BCS &a250 ; is_jumping
&a220 29 0f    AND #&0f                                             # A = frames since standing on walkable surface
&a222 d0 0f    BNE &a233 ; is_walking
&a224 20 14 3c JSR &3c14 ; check_if_slope_is_too_sleep_for_npc      # Returns A = absolute tile_collision_angle
&a227 c9 28    CMP #&28 ; 56.25 degrees
&a229 a5 11    LDA &11 ; this_object_state (behaviour and walking)
&a22b 29 df    AND #&df ; !NPC_CLIMBING
&a22d 90 02    BCC &a231 ; skip_climbing                            # Branch if slope is shallower than &28 (56.25 degrees)
&a22f 09 20    ORA #&20 ; NPC_CLIMBING
; skip_climbing
&a231 85 11    STA &11 ; this_object_state (behaviour and walking)
; is_walking
&a233 a5 11    LDA &11 ; this_object_state (behaviour and walking)
&a235 29 20    AND #&20 ; NPC_CLIMBING
&a237 f0 25    BEQ &a25e ; skip_starting_jump
; is_climbing
&a239 20 9c 25 JSR &259c ; set_npc_facing_tile_collision
; is_at_target
&a23c a9 0c    LDA #&0c ; modulus
&a23e a2 02    LDX #&02 ; divide velocities by 8
&a240 20 86 25 JSR &2586 ; update_sprite_offset_using_scaled_velocities # Returns A = sprite offset
&a243 4a       LSR A                                                # ....8421 -> ....842 1
&a244 4a       LSR A                                                #          -> .....84 2
&a245 4a       LSR A                                                #          -> ......8 4, i.e. set carry from &04 of sprite offset
&a246 a9 67    LDA #&67 ; SPRITE_IMP_CLIMBING_ONE                   # Use SPRITE_CLIMBING_ONE or SPRITE_IMP_CLIMBING_TWO
&a248 69 00    ADC #&00
&a24a d0 3e    BNE &a28a ; set_imp_sprite                           # Always branches
; not_in_water
&a24c a9 69    LDA #&69 ; SPRITE_IMP_JUMPING                        # Use SPRITE_IMP_JUMPING if jumping, but not in water
&a24e d0 35    BNE &a285 ; consider_flipping_imp_and_set_sprite     # Always branches
; is_jumping
&a250 24 1f    BIT &1f ; this_object_in_water                       # Positive if object is in any water
&a252 30 f8    BMI &a24c ; not_in_water
; is_in_water
&a254 20 b6 25 JSR &25b6 ; rnd
&a257 29 1f    AND #&1f                                             # 1 in 32 chance of imp starting jump
&a259 d0 03    BNE &a25e ; skip_starting_jump
&a25b 20 c0 3a JSR &3ac0 ; set_npc_jumping
; skip_starting_jump
&a25e a4 3b    LDY &3b ; this_object_touching
&a260 c4 0e    CPY &0e ; this_object_target_object
&a262 f0 d8    BEQ &a23c ; is_at_target
; consider_firing_at_player
&a264 a0 00    LDY #&00 ; OBJECT_TYPE_PLAYER
&a266 84 9d    STY &9d ; target_type
&a268 a6 22    LDX &22 ; npc_type
&a26a bd 04 32 LDA &3204,X ; imp_types_projectile_table
&a26d aa       TAX
&a26e a9 08    LDA #&08                                             # 3 in 128 chance of firing at player (see &279e)
&a270 20 9c 27 JSR &279c ; find_a_target_and_fire_at_it_with_likelihood_A_divided_by_four
&a273 a9 64    LDA #&64 ; SPRITE_IMP_WALKING_ONE
&a275 a6 43    LDX &43 ; this_object_velocity_x
&a277 f0 11    BEQ &a28a ; set_imp_sprite
&a279 a9 0c    LDA #&0c ; modulus
&a27b a2 02    LDX #&02 ; divide velocities by 8
&a27d 20 86 25 JSR &2586 ; update_sprite_offset_using_scaled_velocities # Returns A = sprite offset
&a280 4a       LSR A                                                # ....8421 -> ....842
&a281 4a       LSR A                                                #          -> .....84
&a282 18       CLC
&a283 69 64    ADC #&64 ; SPRITE_IMP_WALKING_ONE                    # Use SPRITE_IMP_ONE to SPRITE_IMP_WALKING_THREE
; consider_flipping_imp_and_set_sprite
&a285 48       PHA ; sprite
&a286 20 a7 25 JSR &25a7 ; consider_flipping_object_to_match_velocity_x
&a289 68       PLA ; sprite
; set_imp_sprite
&a28a 20 ff 32 JSR &32ff ; change_object_sprite_to_A
&a28d 20 6b 25 JSR &256b ; check_if_object_was_damaged              # Returns carry set if object has just taken >= 8 damage
&a290 a9 a5    LDA #&a5 ; pitch
&a292 b0 12    BCS &a2a6 ; play_imp_sound                           # Branch to scream if imp is damaged
&a294 a5 07    LDA &07 ; this_object_frame_counter_sixteen
&a296 d0 18    BNE &a2b0 ; skip_sound
&a298 20 b6 25 JSR &25b6 ; rnd                                      # Random pitch for imp
&a29b 4a       LSR A                                                # 84218421 -> .8421842 1
&a29c 6a       ROR A                                                #          -> 1.842184
&a29d 10 11    BPL &a2b0 ; skip_sound                               # 1 in 2 chance of playing sound otherwise
&a29f 45 11    EOR &11 ; this_object_state (behaviour and walking)
&a2a1 29 e0    AND #&e0 ; NPC_MOOD_MINUS_TWO | NPC_CLIMBING         # Pitch depends on mood and if the imp is climbing
&a2a3 4a       LSR A
&a2a4 09 05    ORA #&05
; play_imp_sound
&a2a6 8d af a2 STA &a2af ; imp_sound_parameters + 3 (pitch)
&a2a9 20 0f 14 JSR &140f ; play_sound
; imp_sound_parameters
&a2ac 9c 05 a6 a5                                                   # Play sound for imp (pitch is modified)
; skip_sound
&a2b0 60       RTS

# Green/yellow birds (OBJECT_GREEN_YELLOW_BIRD)

# Red/magenta birds (OBJECT_RED_MAGENTA_BIRD)
# Invisible birds (OBJECT_INVISIBLE_BIRD)
# =======================================
# Spawned from nests or pipes.
#
# objects_state is used for visibility
# objects_timer is used for sprite offset

; update_whistling_bird                                             # Called with Y = object touching, or negative if none
&a2be a5 da    LDA &da ; rnd_state + 1
&a2c0 d0 1a    BNE &a2dc ; skip_sound                               # 1 in 256 chance of red/magenta bird whistling
&a2c2 20 cd 2c JSR &2ccd ; play_whistle_two_sound                   # This will deactivate Chatter
&a2c5 4c dc a2 JMP &a2dc ; skip_sound
; update_invisible_bird                                             # Called with Y = object touching, or negative if none
&a2c8 a5 11    LDA &11 ; this_object_state (bird visibility)        # Non-zero if bird has recently taken damage
&a2ca d0 02    BNE &a2ce ; update_bird
&a2cc 46 2b    LSR &2b ; this_object_visibility                     # Clear top bit to make invisible
; not_invisible
; update_bird                                                       # Called with Y = object touching, or negative if none
&a2ce 20 b6 25 JSR &25b6 ; rnd
&a2d1 29 3f    AND #&3f                                             # 1 in 64 chance of bird calling
&a2d3 d0 07    BNE &a2dc ; skip_sound
&a2d5 20 0f 14 JSR &140f ; play_sound
&a2d8 57 07 43 f6                                                   # Play sound for bird calling
; skip_sound
&a2dc a6 41    LDX &41 ; this_object_type
&a2de 98       TYA ; object touching
&a2df d0 06    BNE &a2e7 ; not_touching_player
&a2e1 bd ff a2 LDA &a2ff,X ; birds_damage_table - &2e (OBJECT_GREEN_YELLOW_BIRD)
&a2e4 20 d5 24 JSR &24d5 ; damage_object                            # Birds cause damage to player depending on type
; not_touching_player
&a2e7 bc 03 a3 LDY &a303,X ; birds_energy_table - &2e (OBJECT_GREEN_YELLOW_BIRD)
&a2ea 20 95 35 JSR &3595 ; give_object_minimum_energy               # Birds have minimum energy depending on type
&a2ed 29 7f    AND #&7f
&a2ef 85 15    STA &15 ; this_object_energy
&a2f1 20 6b 25 JSR &256b ; check_if_object_was_damaged              # Returns carry set if object has just taken >= 8 damage
&a2f4 66 11    ROR &11 ; this_object_state (bird visibility)        # If so, set to non-zero to make invisible bird visible
&a2f6 d0 2d    BNE &a325 ; dampen_velocity_if_underwater
&a2f8 a9 14    LDA #&14 ; modulus
&a2fa 20 84 25 JSR &2584 ; update_sprite_offset_using_velocities    # Returns A = sprite offset
&a2fd 4a       LSR A                                                # ...184.. -> ....184.
&a2fe 4a       LSR A                                                #          -> .....184, i.e. &00 - &13 -> &00 -> &04
&a2ff c9 04    CMP #&04
&a301 d0 02    BNE &a305 ; set_bird_sprite                          # Use SPRITE_BIRD_ONE to SPRITE_BIRD_FOUR for &00 - &03
&a303 a9 02    LDA #&02                                             # Use SPRITE_BIRD_THREE for &04
; set_bird_sprite
&a305 20 f9 32 JSR &32f9 ; change_object_sprite_to_base_plus_A
&a308 a9 11    LDA #&11 ; OBJECT_WASP                               # Birds eat wasps
&a30a 20 48 3c JSR &3c48 ; consider_absorbing_object_touched
&a30d a9 11    LDA #&11 ; OBJECT_WASP
; move_fireball
&a30f a0 00    LDY #&00 ; no range
&a311 20 5f 3c JSR &3c5f ; consider_finding_target                  # Birds target wasps
&a314 20 70 3c JSR &3c70 ; avoid_fireballs                          # Birds avoid fireballs
; consider_moving_towards_player
&a317 20 8d 3d JSR &3d8d ; consider_updating_npc_path
&a31a a0 08    LDY #&08 ; maximum acceleration
&a31c a9 40    LDA #&40 ; magnitude
&a31e a2 40    LDX #&40 ; 1 in 4 chance
&a320 20 41 32 JSR &3241 ; move_towards_target_with_probability_X
&a323 c6 42    DEC &42 ; this_object_acceleration_y                 # No gravity for birds
; dampen_velocity_if_underwater
&a325 24 1f    BIT &1f ; this_object_in_water                       # Positive if object is in any water
&a327 30 03    BMI &a32c ; leave
&a329 20 86 32 JSR &3286 ; dampen_this_object_velocities_twice
; leave
&a32c 60       RTS

; birds_damage_table
&a32d 00 ; &2e (OBJECT_GREEN_YELLOW_BIRD)                           # Green/yellow birds cause no damage to player

&a32f 40 ; &30 (OBJECT_RED_MAGENTA_BIRD)                            # Red/magenta birds cause 64 damage to player
&a330 14 ; &31 (OBJECT_INVISIBLE_BIRD)                              # Invisible birds cause 20 damage to player

&a331 00 ; &2e (OBJECT_GREEN_YELLOW_BIRD)
&a332 00 ; &2f (OBJECT_WHITE_YELLOW_BIRD)
&a333 1e ; &30 (OBJECT_RED_MAGENTA_BIRD)                            # Red/magenta birds have a minimum energy of 30
&a334 00 ; &31 (OBJECT_INVISIBLE_BIRD)

; update_mushroom_ball                                              # Called with Y = object touching, or negative if none
&a335 30 0c    BMI &a343 ; not_touching_other_object

# Icer bullets (OBJECT_ICER_BULLET)
# =================================

; update_icer_bullet
&a35c a9 02    LDA #&02 ; explosion duration
&a35e a2 14    LDX #&14 ; damage
; update_bullet_with_particle_trail                                 # Called with A = explosion duration, X = damage
&a360 48       PHA ; explosion duration
&a361 20 e2 1f JSR &1fe2 ; check_if_object_Y_damaged_by_projectiles # Returns negative if not, or Y = object
&a364 68       PLA ; explosion duration
&a365 c0 00    CPY #&00
&a367 30 0a    BMI &a373 ; no_collision_with_object
&a369 20 78 9d JSR &9d78 ; explode_object_with_duration_A           # Explode bullet
&a36c 8a       TXA ; damage
&a36d 20 d5 24 JSR &24d5 ; damage_object                            # Bullets cause damage depending on type
&a370 4c d2 28 JMP &28d2 ; set_this_object_velocities_to_zero       # Bullet explosions on collision with objects don't move
; no_collision_with_object
&a373 20 d1 a0 JSR &a0d1 ; move_bullet                              # May explode bullet
; create_projectile_particle_trail
&a376 a5 b5    LDA &b5 ; angle
&a378 49 80    EOR #&80                                             # Particles leave rear of bullet
&a37a 85 b5    STA &b5 ; angle
&a37c a6 41    LDX &41 ; this_object_type
&a37e bd 76 a3 LDA &a376,X ; bullet_particles_colour_and_flags_table - &13 (OBJECT_ICER_BULLET)
&a381 8d 36 02 STA &0236 ; particle_types_colour_and_flags_table + &2c (PARTICLE_PROJECTILE_TRAIL)
&a384 a0 2c    LDY #&2c ; PARTICLE_PROJECTILE_TRAIL
&a386 4c bf 21 JMP &21bf ; add_particle                             # Add particle trail of colour depending on type of bullet

; bullet_particles_colour_and_flags_table                           # &0237 = &01, so randomly choose one of two colours
&a389 02 ; &13 (OBJECT_ICER_BULLET)     : green or yellow
&a38a 04 ; &14 (OBJECT_TRACER_BULLET)   : blue or magenta
&a38b 08 ; &15 (OBJECT_CANNONBALL)      : cycle colours
&a38c 08 ; &16 (OBJECT_BLUE_DEATH_BALL) : cycle colours

# Triax (OBJECT_TRIAX)
# ====================

; update_triax
&a3a1 a9 4a    LDA #&4a ; OBJECT_OBJECT_DESTINATOR
&a3a3 20 48 3c JSR &3c48 ; consider_absorbing_object_touched        # Returns zero if object absorbed
&a3a6 d0 07    BNE &a3af ; not_absorbed
; triax_absorbed_destinator
&a3a8 a9 80    LDA #&80
&a3aa 8d 23 0a STA &0a23 ; tertiary_objects_data + &9d              # Tertiary object &a8, OBJECT_DESTINATOR at (&64, &d6)
;                                                                   # Return destinator to default location in Triax's lab
&a3ad d0 4a    BNE &a3f9 ; make_triax_teleport_away                 # Always branches
; not_absorbed
&a3af a5 3e    LDA &3e ; this_object_target_object_and_flags        # &80 set if TARGET_FLAG_DIRECTNESS_TWO or TARGET_FLAG_DIRECTNESS_THREE
&a3b1 30 04    BMI &a3b7 ; can_see_or_has_seen_player               # i.e. if Triax can see or has recently seen the player
&a3b3 a5 06    LDA &06 ; this_object_frame_counter
&a3b5 f0 42    BEQ &a3f9 ; make_triax_teleport_away                 # Triax teleports away 1 in 256 frames otherwise
; can_see_or_has_seen_player
&a3b7 a5 15    LDA &15 ; this_object_energy
&a3b9 c9 40    CMP #&40
&a3bb b0 06    BCS &a3c3 ; not_weak
&a3bd a5 d9    LDA &d9 ; rnd_state
&a3bf c9 04    CMP #&04
&a3c1 90 36    BCC &a3f9 ; make_triax_teleport_away                 # 1 in 64 chance of Triax teleporting away if energy < &40
; not_weak
&a3c3 a5 db    LDA &db ; rnd_state + 2
&a3c5 c9 08    CMP #&08                                             # 1 in 32 chance of Triax firing a grenade
&a3c7 a9 13    LDA #&13 ; OBJECT_ICER_BULLET
&a3c9 b0 02    BCS &a3cd ; not_grenade
&a3cb a9 12    LDA #&12 ; OBJECT_ACTIVE_GRENADE
; not_grenade
&a3cd 20 fa a4 JSR &a4fa ; consider_firing_at_player_and_move_triax # Triax fires and moves like a clawed robot
&a3d0 a5 15    LDA &15 ; this_object_energy
&a3d2 c9 05    CMP #&05
&a3d4 2a       ROL A                                                # Set &01 if energy >= &05
&a3d5 05 dc    ORA &dc ; rnd_state + 3
&a3d7 05 da    ORA &da ; rnd_state + 1
&a3d9 4a       LSR A                                                # Set carry if &01 was set
&a3da 90 1d    BCC &a3f9 ; make_triax_teleport_away                 # 1 in 4 chance of Triax teleporting away if energy < &05
&a3dc ad ce 19 LDA &19ce ; player_is_east_of_76
&a3df 49 80    EOR #&80                                             # &80 set if player is west of &80
&a3e1 0d 1e 08 ORA &081e ; flooding_state                           # &80 set if world is being flooded
&a3e4 30 06    BMI &a3ec ; skip_random_teleport
&a3e6 a5 dc    LDA &dc ; rnd_state + 3
&a3e8 29 03    AND #&03
&a3ea f0 0d    BEQ &a3f9 ; make_triax_teleport_away                 # If neither, 1 in 4 chance of Triax teleporting away
; skip_random_teleport
&a3ec 20 b6 25 JSR &25b6 ; rnd
&a3ef f0 08    BEQ &a3f9 ; make_triax_teleport_away                 # 1 in 256 chance of Triax teleporting away regardless
&a3f1 20 8d a3 JSR &a38d ; update_crew_member                       # Triax screams, walks, and is always upright
&a3f4 a0 00    LDY #&00                                             # Allow Triax to teleport into player's spaceship
&a3f6 4c 24 a5 JMP &a524 ; consider_teleporting_to_random_tile_near_player
; make_triax_teleport_away
&a3f9 a9 00    LDA #&00                                             # Set target y to zero to remove Triax
&a3fb 4c 37 a5 JMP &a537 ; set_this_object_ty_and_set_teleporting

&a48f a9 04    LDA #&04
&a491 99 f6 08 STA &08f6,Y ; objects_velocity_y

; set_red_slime_sprite

# Green clawed robot (OBJECT_GREEN_CLAWED_ROBOT)

&a4d2 c9 8c    CMP #&8c

; not_low_on_energy

&a4d8 29 c0    AND #&c0 ; TARGET_FLAG_DIRECTNESS_TWO | TARGET_FLAG_DIRECTNESS_ONE

&a4dc d0 0a    BNE &a4e8 ; skip_teleporting_away                    # hasn't recently seen the plan, teleport it away
; set_clawed_robot_teleporting_away 
&a4de a5 11    LDA &11 ; this_object_state (clawed robot damaged)
&a4e0 d0 06    BNE &a4e8 ; skip_teleporting_away                    # Don't teleport if recently damaged
&a4e2 9d 1d 08 STA &081d,X ; clawed_robots_availability - &22 (OBJECT_MAGENTA_CLAWED_ROBOT) # Set to zero to indicate clawed
&a4e5 4c 37 a5 JMP &a537 ; set_this_object_ty_and_set_teleporting   # robot has teleported away
; skip_teleporting_away
&a4e8 a0 46    LDY #&46                                             # Limit clawed robots to below top of Pericles spaceship
&a4ea 20 24 a5 JSR &a524 ; consider_teleporting_to_random_tile_near_player
&a4ed 20 b6 25 JSR &25b6 ; rnd
&a4f0 4a       LSR A                                                # 1 in 128 chance of playing sound
&a4f1 d0 05    BNE &a4f8 ; skip_sound
&a4f3 a2 05    LDX #&05                                         *** # Play "Destroy!" sample
&a4f5 20 ec 99 JSR &99ec ; play_sample                          ***
; skip_sound
&a4f8 a9 13    LDA #&13 ; OBJECT_ICER_BULLET
; consider_firing_at_player_and_move_triax
&a4fa 20 78 25 JSR &2578 ; increase_energy_by_one                   # Triax and clawed robots gain two energy per update
; consider_firing_at_player_and_move_robot
&a4fd 20 78 25 JSR &2578 ; increase_energy_by_one                   # Hovering robot gains one energy per update
&a500 aa       TAX ; projectile type
&a501 a8       TAY ; range                                          # Set to positive to suppress checking for range/
&a502 a9 81    LDA #&81 ; OBJECT_ACTIVE_CHATTER | &80               # &80 set to target player too
&a504 20 97 27 JSR &2797 ; find_a_target_and_fire_at_it
; move_hovering_npc                                                 # Also hovering balls and hovering robots

&a50e a9 07    LDA #&07                                             # 1 in 32 chance of flipping hovering NPC
&a510 20 a9 25 JSR &25a9 ; consider_flipping_object_to_match_velocity_x_A
; thrust_towards_target                                             # Also Chatter
&a513 a9 1c    LDA #&1c ; magnitude
&a515 a0 04    LDY #&04 ; maximum acceleration
&a517 a2 80    LDX #&80 ; 1 in 2 chance
&a519 20 41 32 JSR &3241 ; move_towards_target_with_probability_X
&a51c c6 42    DEC &42 ; this_object_acceleration_y                 # No gravity for hovering NPCs
&a51e 20 85 3a JSR &3a85 ; consider_hovering_over_ground
&a521 4c 70 1f JMP &1f70 ; add_jetpack_thrust_particles

; consider_teleporting_to_random_tile_near_player

&a528 a9 40    LDA #&40 ; TARGET_FLAG_DIRECTNESS_ONE

; clawed_robots_energy_table
&a53c 46 ; &22 (OBJECT_MAGENTA_CLAWED_ROBOT)                        # Magenta clawed robot has minimum energy of 70

&a53e 80 ; &24 (OBJECT_GREEN_CLAWED_ROBOT)                          # Green clawed robot has minimum energy of 128
&a53f 82 ; &25 (OBJECT_RED_CLAWED_ROBOT)                            # Red clawed robot has minimum energy of 130

# Active chatter (OBJECT_ACTIVE_CHATTER)
# Inactive chatter (OBJECT_INACTIVE_CHATTER)
# ==========================================
# objects_state is used for NPC behaviour
# objects_timer is used chattering timer and activation flag

; update_inactive_or_active_chatter
&a540 a5 27    LDA &27 ; whistle_one_active                         # &80 set if whistle one played
&a542 29 80    AND #&80
&a544 10 04    BPL &a54a ; not_whistled
&a546 85 12    STA &12 ; this_object_timer (chatter chattering timer and activation flag) # Set to negative to activate Chatter
&a548 85 11    STA &11 ; this_object_state (behaviour and walking) # Set to NPC_MOOD_MINUS_TWO
; not_whistled
&a54a a2 07    LDX #&07 ; npc stimuli type
&a54c 20 f8 27 JSR &27f8 ; check_for_npc_stimuli
&a54f 20 8d 3d JSR &3d8d ; consider_updating_npc_path
&a552 46 21    LSR &21 ; stimuli                                    # &01 set if Chatter fed coronium crystal
&a554 90 03    BCC &a559 ; leave                                    # If so, increase Chatter's energy reserve
; increase_chatter_energy_reserve
&a556 ee 1c 08 INC &081c ; chatter_energy_reserve
; leave
&a559 60       RTS

; update_inactive_chatter
&a55a 20 40 a5 JSR &a540 ; update_inactive_or_active_chatter
&a55d a5 12    LDA &12 ; this_object_timer (chatter chattering timer and activation flag) # Negative if whistle one played
&a55f 10 f8    BPL &a559 ; leave
&a561 85 15    STA &15 ; this_object_energy
&a563 ce 1c 08 DEC &081c ; chatter_energy_reserve                   # Reduce energy reserve by one each activation
&a566 30 ee    BMI &a556 ; increase_chatter_energy                  # Keep Chatter's energy from falling below zero
;                                                                   # but don't activate Chatter if energy reserve is zero
; activate_chatter
&a568 a9 01    LDA #&01 ; OBJECT_ACTIVE_CHATTER
&a56a 2c a9 38 BIT &38a9 ; (nop)
; deactivate_chatter
#a56b          LDA #&38 ; OBJECT_INACTIVE_CHATTER
&a56d 4c ed 32 JMP &32ed ; change_object_type

; update_active_chatter
&a570 20 40 a5 JSR &a540 ; update_inactive_or_active_chatter
&a573 a0 00    LDY #&00 ; minimum energy                            # Chatter has a minimum energy of 0, but cannot be destroyed
&a575 20 ae 35 JSR &35ae ; flash_if_damaged
&a578 a5 15    LDA &15 ; this_object_energy
&a57a f0 ef    BEQ &a56b ; deactivate_chatter                       # Deactivate Chatter if damaged too much
&a57c a9 1f    LDA #&1f                                             # 1 in 8 chance of flipping active Chatter
&a57e 20 a9 25 JSR &25a9 ; consider_flipping_object_to_match_velocity_x_A
&a581 24 c4    BIT &c4 ; every_eight_frames
&a583 10 22    BPL &a5a7 ; skip_firing_lightning                    # Every eight frames, consider firing lightning
&a585 a9 20    LDA #&20 ; OBJECT_CYAN_RED_TURRET
&a587 a0 86    LDY #&86 ; OBJECT_RANGE_FLYING_ENEMIES
&a589 20 91 3c JSR &3c91 ; find_object
&a58c 30 19    BMI &a5a7 ; skip_firing_lightning
&a58e a5 b5    LDA &b5 ; angle                                      # Angle of target, set by find_object
&a590 69 40    ADC #&40 ; 90 degrees
&a592 85 37    STA &37 ; this_object_x_flip
&a594 45 6f    EOR &6f ; this_object_flags
&a596 30 0f    BMI &a5a7 ; skip_firing_lightning                    # Don't fire backwards
&a598 a5 b5    LDA &b5 ; angle
&a59a 29 7f    AND #&7f ; ~180 degrees
&a59c e9 0a    SBC #&0a
&a59e c9 6c    CMP #&6c                                             # If &0a <= angle < &76
&a5a0 90 05    BCC &a5a7 ; skip_firing_lightning                    #    ~14 degrees <= angle < ~165 degrees, don't fire
;                                                                   # i.e. only fire within ~14 degrees of horizontal
&a5a2 85 12    STA &12 ; this_object_timer (chatter chattering timer and activation flag) # Chatter chatters when firing
&a5a4 20 0c 34 JSR &340c ; create_lightning
; skip_firing_lightning
&a5a7 a5 12    LDA &12 ; this_object_timer (chatter chattering timer and activation flag)
&a5a9 f0 21    BEQ &a5cc ; skip_chattering
&a5ab c6 12    DEC &12 ; this_object_timer (chatter chattering timer and activation flag)
&a5ad a5 d9    LDA &d9 ; rnd_state
&a5af c9 c0    CMP #&c0                                             # 1 in 4 chance of playing a sound if chattering
&a5b1 90 19    BCC &a5cc ; skip_chattering
&a5b3 a5 dc    LDA &dc ; rnd_state + 3
&a5b5 4a       LSR A                                                # 84218421 -> .8421842 1
&a5b6 4a       LSR A                                                #          -> ..842184 2
&a5b7 45 11    EOR &11 ; this_object_state (behaviour and walking)  # Set two top bits from Chatter's mood, rest as random
&a5b9 69 40    ADC #&40                                             # &80 &c0 &00 &40 -> &c0 &00 &40 &80
&a5bb 49 c0    EOR #&c0                                             #                 -> &00 &c0 &80 &40
&a5bd 4a       LSR A                                                #                 -> &00 &60 &40 &20
&a5be 8d b7 2e STA &2eb7 ; envelopes_table + &cf (chatter_pitch)    # Set delta for first stage of pitch envelope. This
;                                                                   # stage is duration one (&2eb6), so this sets the pitch
&a5c1 20 0f 14 JSR &140f ; play_sound
&a5c4 33 f3 cd 82                                                   # Play sound for Chatter chattering
&a5c8 a9 4b    LDA #&4b ; cyB
&a5ca 85 73    STA &73 ; this_object_palette
; skip_chattering
&a5cc ae 07 2a LDX &2a07 ; whistle_two_activating_object            # Positive if whistle two played
&a5cf 30 14    BMI &a5e5 ; skip_producing_power_pod
&a5d1 20 01 36 JSR &3601 ; check_for_obstruction_between_objects_80
&a5d4 b0 0f    BCS &a5e5 ; skip_producing_power_pod                 # If Chatter can see the source of the whistle,
&a5d6 a0 4b    LDY #&4b ; OBJECT_POWER_POD
&a5d8 a9 40    LDA #&40 ; &10 (firing velocity) * 4
&a5da 20 c0 27 JSR &27c0 ; fire_at_target_with_velocity             # Returns carry set if unable to calculate velocities
&a5dd 10 06    BPL &a5e5 ; skip_deactivating                        #         positive if firing would have been backwards
&a5df b0 04    BCS &a5e5 ; skip_deactivating                        # Bug: carry clear doesn't mean power pod was created
&a5e1 a9 00    LDA #&00
&a5e3 85 15    STA &15 ; this_object_energy                         # Set to zero to deactivate Chatter
; skip_deactivating
; skip_producing_power_pod
&a5e5 a5 3b    LDA &3b ; this_object_touching                       # If Chatter isn't touching anything
&a5e7 05 0e    ORA &0e ; this_object_target_object                  # or targeting any other object
&a5e9 d0 02    BNE &a5ed ; to_thrust_towards_target
&a5eb 85 3e    STA &3e ; this_object_target_object_and_flags        # then set player as target
; to_thrust_towards_target
&a5ed 4c 13 a5 JMP &a513 ; thrust_towards_target

; unused
&a5f0 60       RTS

# Switches (OBJECT_SWITCH)
# ========================
# Tertiary data byte is as follows:
#
# 8....... if set, switch needs primary object creating
# .4218... switch effects number
# .....42. bits to toggle in other objects
# .......1 switch state
#
# objects_tx is used to suppress auto-repeat

; switch_effects_table
&a5f1 00 b0 bb 84    ; &00 : &0a36, &0a41, &0a0a        # switch at (&d5, &73)
&a5f5 00 0f 29       ; &01 : &0995, &09af               # switch at (&9d, &3b)
&a5f8 00 c5          ; &02 : &0a4b                      # switch at (&95, &5d)
&a5fa 00 e7 8f       ; &03 : &0a6d, &0a15               # switch at (&29, &c8)
&a5fd 00 8a          ; &04 : &0a10                      # switch at (&7c, &c0)
&a5ff 00 13          ; &05 : &0999                      # switch at (&4d, &80)
&a601 00 8e 32       ; &06 : &0a14, &09b8               # switch at (&a1, &58)
&a604 00 c2          ; &07 : &0a48                      # switch at (&6a, &de)
&a606 00 11 aa bd    ; &08 : &0997, &0a30, &0a43        # switches at (&46, &56), (&8b, &71)
&a60a 00 58 cc 55 bc : &09 : &09de, &0a52, &09db, &0a42 # switch at (&ab, &6b)
&a60f 00 55          ; &0a : &09db                      # invisible switch at (&a8, &69)
&a611 00 46 a9       ; &0b : &09cc, &0a2f               # switches at (&d4, &6f), (&d5, &73)1
&a614 00 6a 8b       ; &0c : &09f0, &0a11               # switch at (&e3, &9c) or (&e3, &bc)
&a617 00 e6 85 d8    ; &0d : &0a6c, &0a0b, &0a5e        # switch at (&67, &cb)
&a61b 00 c7 88       ; &0e : &0a4d, &0a0e               # invisible switch at (&b4, &c2), switch at (&b8, &c3)
&a61e 00 68          ; &0f : &09ee                      # switch at (&c4, &c4)
&a620 00 14          ; &10 : &099a                      # invisible switch at (&c1, &7c)
&a622 00 28 4c       ; &11 : &09ae, &09d2               # invisible switch at (&9b, &3b)
&a625 00 65          ; &12 : &09eb                      # invisible switch at (&c6, &7c)
&a627 00 89          ; &13 : &0a0f                      # invisible switch at (&80, &c2)
&a629 00 8d          ; &14 : &0a13                      # invisible switch at (&67, &da)
&a62b 00 64 2a       ; &15 : &09ea, &09b0               # invisible switch at (&a9, &9c)
&a62e 00 6b          ; &16 : &09f1                      # invisible switch at (&eb, &bc)
&a630 00 a7 b9 10    ; &17 : &0a2d, &0a3f, &0996        # invisible switches at (&87, &77), (&7f, &77), (&83, &76)
&a634 00             ; (end of list)

; copy_protection_seed
&a635 ea

; update_switch                                                     # Called with Y = object touching, or negative if none
&a636 18       CLC                                                  # Clear carry to indicate not triggered if not touched
&a637 30 03    BMI &a63c ; not_touched
&a639 20 5e a6 JSR &a65e ; check_if_object_can_trigger_switches     # Returns carry set if object can trigger
; not_touched
&a63c 66 14    ROR &14 ; this_object_tx (switch repeat suppressor)  # Set &80 if switch is pressed, clear if not
&a63e 10 16    BPL &a656 ; skip_triggering_switch
&a640 a5 14    LDA &14 ; this_object_tx (switch repeat suppressor)  # &80 set if switch has just been pressed
&a642 0a       ASL A
&a643 d0 11    BNE &a656 ; skip_triggering_switch                   # Branch if switch is pressed a second time
&a645 2a       ROL A
&a646 45 bc    EOR &bc ; this_object_data                           # Toggle &01 to indicate whether switch is on
&a648 85 bc    STA &bc ; this_object_data                           # Remaining bits of data set switch effect
&a64a a2 ff    LDX #&ff ; data mask
&a64c 20 74 a6 JSR &a674 ; process_switch_effects
&a64f 20 0f 14 JSR &140f ; play_sound
&a652 3d 04 11 d4                                                   # Play sound for switch being pressed
; skip_triggering_switch
; set_this_object_x_flip_from_data
&a656 a5 bc    LDA &bc ; this_object_data                           # If &01 set, switch is on
&a658 4a       LSR A
&a659 66 37    ROR &37 ; this_object_x_flip                         # If so, set top bit to flip switch horizontally
&a65b 4c 9f 35 JMP &359f ; gain_energy_and_flash_if_damaged         # Switches have a minimum energy of 30

; check_if_object_can_trigger_switches                              # Called with X = object type
&a65e 20 53 1e JSR &1e53 ; get_object_weight
&a661 c9 02    CMP #&02                                             # If object is very light,
&a663 90 0e    BCC &a673 ; leave                                    # Leave with carry clear to indicate not triggering
&a665 e0 35    CPX #&35 ; OBJECT_INVISIBLE_DEBRIS
&a667 f0 06    BEQ &a66f ; is_invisible_debris                      # Set carry if invisible debris
&a669 e0 27    CPX #&27 ; OBJECT_MAGGOT
&a66b b0 06    BCS &a673 ; leave                                    # Leave with carry set to indicate triggering
&a66d e0 22    CPX #&22 ; OBJECT_MAGENTA_CLAWED_ROBOT               # Set carry if clawed robots or Triax, clear otherwise
; is_invisible_debris
&a66f 2a       ROL A
&a670 49 01    EOR #&01                                             # Invert carry,
&a672 6a       ROR A                                                # i.e. invisible debris, clawed robots and Triax don't trigger switches
; leave
&a673 60       RTS

; process_switch_effects                                            # Called with A 84218... switch effects number
;                                                                   #               .....42. bits to toggle in other objects
&a674 86 9c    STX &9c ; mask                                       #             X = data mask
&a676 4a       LSR A                                                # 84218421 -> .8421842 1
&a677 48       PHA ; switch effect byte
&a678 29 03    AND #&03                                             #             ......42
&a67a 85 9d    STA &9d ; toggle
&a67c 68       PLA ; switch effect byte
&a67d 4a       LSR A                                                # .8421842 -> ..842184
&a67e 4a       LSR A                                                #          -> ...84218
&a67f aa       TAX ; switch effects number
&a680 a0 ff    LDY #&ff
; find_switch_effect_loop
&a682 c8       INY
&a683 b9 f1 a5 LDA &a5f1,Y ; switch_effects_table                   # &00 indicates start of set of switch effects
&a686 d0 fa    BNE &a682 ; find_switch_effect_loop
&a688 ca       DEX
&a689 10 f7    BPL &a682 ; find_switch_effect_loop
&a68b aa       TAX
; process_switch_effects_loop                                       # Otherwise, byte is offset into tertiary_objects_data
&a68c bd 86 09 LDA &0986,X ; tertiary_objects_data
&a68f 85 9a    STA &9a ; previous_data
&a691 25 9c    AND &9c ; mask
&a693 45 9d    EOR &9d ; toggle
&a695 9d 86 09 STA &0986,X ; tertiary_objects_data
&a698 c8       INY
&a699 be f1 a5 LDX &a5f1,Y ; switch_effects_table                   # Consider next byte of set of switch effects
&a69c d0 ee    BNE &a68c ; process_switch_effects_loop              # &00 indicates end of set of switch effects
&a69e c5 9a    CMP &9a ; previous_data
&a6a0 f0 07    BEQ &a6a9 ; leave                                    # No sound if switch had no effect on last object
&a6a2 20 0f 14 JSR &140f ; play_sound
&a6a5 c7 c3 c1 03                                                   # Play sound for switch having an effect
; leave
&a6a9 60       RTS

# Player (OBJECT_PLAYER)

; update_player                                                     # Called with Y = object touching, or negative if none
&a6aa 30 09    BMI &a6b5 ; skip_picking_up_touched_object           #        carry set
&a6ac b9 60 08 LDA &0860,Y ; objects_type
&a6af 49 03    EOR #&03 ; OBJECT_FLUFFY

&a6b3 84 dd    STY &dd ; player_object_held                         # Pick up Fluffy if touched

&a6b5 6e 06 2a ROR &2a06 ; player_object_fired                      # Set to negative to indicate no object fired by default

#     actually LDA welcome_samples_remaining                    ***

&a6bc ee b9 a6 INC &a6b9 ; welcome_samples_remaining            ***

&a6c1 a2 00    LDX #&00                                         *** # Play "Welcome to the land of the exile" sample
&a6c3 20 ec 99 JSR &99ec ; play_sample                          ***
; skip_sound
&a6c6 a5 53    LDA &53 ; this_object_x
&a6c8 c9 76    CMP #&76
&a6ca 6e ce 19 ROR &19ce ; player_is_east_of_76                     # Set &80 if player x >= &76, clear if not
&a6cd 20 66 35 JSR &3566 ; consider_retrieving_object
&a6d0 a5 dd    LDA &dd ; player_object_held
&a6d2 48       PHA ; player_object_held
&a6d3 20 a8 01 JSR &01a8 ; process_actions
&a6d6 a9 10    LDA #&10 ; OBJECT_FLAG_TELEPORTING
&a6d8 24 6f    BIT &6f ; this_object_flags
&a6da d0 03    BNE &a6df ; skip_updating_player_angle_facing_and_sprite
; not_teleporting
&a6dc 20 fc 37 JSR &37fc ; update_player_angle_facing_and_sprite
; skip_updating_player_angle_facing_and_sprite
&a6df 20 2b 31 JSR &312b ; update_player_aiming_angle
&a6e2 68       PLA ; player_object_held
&a6e3 a8       TAY
&a6e4 30 18    BMI &a6fe ; not_holding_object
; is_holding_object
&a6e6 a5 37    LDA &37 ; this_object_x_flip                         # &80 set if flipped horizontally
&a6e8 45 6f    EOR &6f ; this_object_flags                          # &80 set if flipped horizontally
&a6ea 10 12    BPL &a6fe ; skip_setting_held_object_position        # If either, position held object on left of player
&a6ec be 70 08 LDX &0870,Y ; objects_sprite
&a6ef bd 0c be LDA &be0c,X ; sprites_width_and_horizontal_flip_table
&a6f2 24 37    BIT &37 ; this_object_x_flip
&a6f4 20 b3 32 JSR &32b3 ; invert_if_positive
&a6f7 a2 00    LDX #&00 ; OBJECT_SLOT_PLAYER
&a6f9 24 6f    BIT &6f ; this_object_flags
&a6fb 20 67 2a JSR &2a67 ; add_A_to_position
; skip_setting_held_object_position
; not_holding_object
&a6fe 24 c5    BIT &c5 ; every_four_frames
&a700 10 0d    BPL &a70f ; skip_playing_energy_level_bells          # Once every four frames,
&a702 a5 25    LDA &25 ; energy_level_bells_remaining
&a704 f0 09    BEQ &a70f ; skip_playing_energy_level_bells          # if bells for energy level are pending,
&a706 c6 25    DEC &25 ; energy_level_bells_remaining
&a708 20 0f 14 JSR &140f ; play_sound
&a70b 17 e3 2f 82                                                   # Play sound for energy level bell
; skip_playing_energy_level_bells
&a70f ce 05 2a DEC &2a05 ; player_firing_cooldown                   # Non-zero if player has fired recently
&a712 d0 03    BNE &a717 ; skip_suppressing_fire
&a714 4e 70 12 LSR &1270 ; action_keys_pressed + &0d (SPACE)        # Clear top bit to prevent firing again
; skip_suppressing_fire
&a717 a5 36    LDA &36 ; player_blaster_timer                       # Negative if player has fired blaster recently
&a719 10 13    BPL &a72e ; skip_discharging_blaster
&a71b e6 36    INC &36 ; player_blaster_timer
&a71d 20 85 9d JSR &9d85 ; start_explosion_timer
&a720 a9 0a    LDA #&0a                                             # Blaster behaves as explosion of duration 10
;                                                                   # i.e. causes damage to other objects (but not player,
;                                                                   # because the player is the exploding object)
&a722 85 3d    STA &3d ; this_object_tertiary_data_offset (explosion duration)
&a724 20 0d 14 JSR &140d ; play_sound_on_channel_zero
&a727 17 03 11 04                                                   # Play sound for discharging blaster
&a72b 20 46 ac JSR &ac46 ; update_explosion
&a72e 20 6d 31 JSR &316d ; consider_changing_viewpoint_object   ***
; skip_discharging_blaster
