# Plasma balls (OBJECT_PLASMA_BALL)
# =================================

; update_plasma_ball                                                # Called with Y = object touching, or negative if none
&a732 30 08    BMI &a73c ; not_touching_other_object
&a734 b9 60 08 LDA &0860,Y ; objects_type
&a737 20 03 20 JSR &2003 ; check_if_object_collides_with_plasma_ball
&a73a d0 21    BNE &a75d ; turn_object_into_fireball_of_duration_thirteen # Plasma balls turn into fireballs on contact
; not_touching_other_object
&a73c a5 1f    LDA &1f ; this_object_in_water                       # Positive if object is in any water
&a73e 05 d9    ORA &d9 ; rnd_state
&a740 05 dc    ORA &dc ; rnd_state + 3
&a742 10 2e    BPL &a772 ; remove_plasma_ball_or_fireball           # If underwater, 1 in 4 chance of removing plasma ball
&a744 20 4e 25 JSR &254e ; reduce_energy_by_one                     # Plasma balls have a limited lifespan; returns A = energy
&a747 f0 2c    BEQ &a775 ; to_set_object_for_removal
&a749 c9 03    CMP #&03
&a74b a0 a0    LDY #&a0                                             # Clear &01 to not add object velocity to particles
&a74d a9 03    LDA #&03
&a74f b0 04    BCS &a755 ; set_plasma_particles_flag                # Branch if plasma ball has energy >= &03
; add_plasma_particles
&a751 a0 a1    LDY #&a1                                             # Set &01 to add object velocity to particles
&a753 a9 1e    LDA #&1e
; set_plasma_particles_flags
&a755 8c 0c 02 STY &020c ; particle_types_flags_table + &00 (PARTICLE_PLASMA)
&a758 a0 00    LDY #&00 ; PARTICLE_PLASMA
&a75a 4c c1 21 JMP &21c1 ; add_particles

; turn_object_into_fireball_of_duration_thirteen
&a75d a9 0d    LDA #&0d                                             # &0d > &08, so will cause big damage at start (&4aee)
&a75f 2c a9 07 BIT &07a9 ; (nop)
; turn_object_into_fireball_of_duration_seven
#a760          LDA #&07                                             # &07 < &08, so will not cause big damage
&a762 2c a9 02 BIT &02a9 ; (nop)
; turn_object_into_fireball_of_duration_two
#a763          LDA #&02                                             # &02 < &08, so will not cause big damage
&a765 85 12    STA &12 ; this_object_timer
&a767 85 15    STA &15 ; this_object_energy
&a769 a9 00    LDA #&00                                             # Zero to indicate fireball is from exploding object
&a76b 85 0e    STA &0e ; this_object_target_object (fireball type)
&a76d a9 37    LDA #&37 ; OBJECT_FIREBALL
&a76f 4c ed 32 JMP &32ed ; change_object_type

; remove_plasma_ball_or_fireball
&a772 20 51 a7 JSR &a751 ; add_plasma_particles
; to_set_object_for_removal
&a775 4c 58 25 JMP &2558 ; set_object_for_removal

# Fireballs (OBJECT_FIREBALL)
# Moving fireballs (OBJECT_MOVING_FIREBALL)
# =========================================
# Spawned from pipes or nest, also created by explosions.
#
# objects_target_object_and_flags is used for fireball type; non-zero if permanent fireball, zero if temporary
# objects_timer is used for lifespan for temporary fireballs

; fireball_palettes_table
;      0  1  2  3  4  5  6  7
&a778 10 34 34 34 10 34 10 34                                       # &10 = kyR, &34 = rwY

; update_fireball
&a780 a5 20    LDA &20 ; this_object_waterline                      # Negative if completely under water
&a782 25 db    AND &db ; rnd_state + 2
&a784 25 da    AND &da ; rnd_state + 1                              # If completely underwater, 1 in chance of removing fireball
&a786 30 ea    BMI &a772 ; remove_plasma_ball_or_fireball
&a788 a5 0e    LDA &0e ; this_object_target_object (fireball type)  # Non-zero if spawned from tertiary object,
&a78a d0 68    BNE &a7f4 ; update_permanent_fireball                # zero if from exploding object
; update_temporary_fireball
&a78c c6 12    DEC &12 ; this_object_timer (fireball timer)         # Temporary fireballs have a limited lifespan
&a78e 30 e5    BMI &a775 ; to_set_object_for_removal
&a790 a2 0a    LDX #&0a                                             # Temporary fireballs causes 10 damage
; consider_fireball_damage_and_animate
&a792 a5 07    LDA &07 ; this_object_frame_counter_sixteen
&a794 d0 08    BNE &a79e ; not_big_damage                           # Every sixteen frames,
&a796 a5 12    LDA &12 ; this_object_timer (fireball timer)
&a798 c9 08    CMP #&08
&a79a 90 02    BCC &a79e ; not_big_damage
&a79c a2 5a    LDX #&5a                                             # Fireball causes 90 damage at start of long explosions
;                                                                   # Permanent fireballs cause 90 damage 248 frames in 256
; not_big_damage
&a79e 98       TYA ; object touching
&a79f 30 14    BMI &a7b5 ; not_touching_other_object
&a7a1 d0 06    BNE &a7a9 ; not_touching_player
&a7a3 2c 14 08 BIT &0814 ; player_fire_immunity_device_collected    # Negative if fire immunity device collected
&a7a6 10 01    BPL &a7a9 ; not_immune_to_fire
&a7a8 aa       TAX                                                  # Set to zero to cause no damage if so
; not_immune_to_fire
; not_touching_player
&a7a9 8a       TXA ; damage
&a7aa 20 d5 24 JSR &24d5 ; damage_object                            # Fireball causes damage depending on type and frame
&a7ad 20 fc 1f JSR &1ffc ; check_if_object_Y_collides_with_lightning_or_fireball
&a7b0 f0 03    BEQ &a7b5 ; not_touching_other_object
&a7b2 20 b4 0b JSR &0bb4 ; set_this_object_velocities_from_object_Y # Fireball follows touching object when damaging it
; not_touching_other_object
&a7b5 20 b6 25 JSR &25b6 ; rnd
&a7b8 85 37    STA &37 ; this_object_x_flip                         # Flip horizontally at random
&a7ba 0a       ASL A
&a7bb 85 39    STA &39 ; this_object_y_flip                         # Flip vertically at random
&a7bd a5 12    LDA &12 ; this_object_timer (fireball timer)
&a7bf 29 07    AND #&07
&a7c1 aa       TAX
&a7c2 bd 78 a7 LDA &a778,X ; fireball_palettes_table
&a7c5 85 73    STA &73 ; this_object_palette
&a7c7 a9 c0    LDA #&c0                                             # Set fireball particles moving upwards
&a7c9 85 b5    STA &b5 ; angle
&a7cb a0 21    LDY #&21 ; PARTICLE_FIREBALL
&a7cd 4c bf 21 JMP &21bf ; add_particle

; update_moving_fireball
&a7d0 a2 04    LDX #&04                                             # Moving fireball causes 4 damage
&a7d2 20 92 a7 JSR &a792 ; consider_fireball_damage_and_animate
&a7d5 20 d9 28 JSR &28d9 ; set_this_object_position_from_previous_position
&a7d8 a5 3b    LDA &3b ; this_object_touching
&a7da f0 03    BEQ &a7df ; is_touching_player                       # Moving fireball follows player if touching
&a7dc 20 e9 9d JSR &9de9 ; use_previous_velocities
; is_touching_player
&a7df 20 0f a3 JSR &a30f ; move_fireball
&a7e2 24 1f    BIT &1f ; this_object_in_water                       # Positive if object is in any water
&a7e4 30 08    BMI &a7ee ; not_underwater
&a7e6 a9 fc    LDA #&fc                                             # Accelerate upwards, out of water
&a7e8 85 42    STA &42 ; this_object_acceleration_y
&a7ea 24 20    BIT &20 ; this_object_waterline                      # Negative if completely under water
&a7ec 30 84    BMI &a772 ; remove_plasma_ball_or_fireball
; not_underwater
&a7ee 20 34 1f JSR &1f34 ; apply_acceleration_to_velocities
&a7f1 4c 60 2a JMP &2a60 ; add_velocities_to_position

; update_permanent_fireball
&a7f4 20 dd 28 JSR &28dd ; set_this_object_position_from_previous_position_except_y_fraction
&a7f7 24 da    BIT &da ; rnd_state + 1                              # 1 in 2 chance of moving permanent fireball
&a7f9 10 58    BPL &a853 ; set_this_object_velocities_to_zero_and_position_from_previous_position
&a7fb a5 da    LDA &da ; rnd_state + 1
&a7fd 29 0f    AND #&0f                                             # Random component
&a7ff 65 07    ADC &07 ; this_object_frame_counter_sixteen          # plus rhythmic component
&a801 0a       ASL A
&a802 65 51    ADC &51 ; this_object_y_fraction
&a804 69 18    ADC #&18
&a806 85 51    STA &51 ; this_object_y_fraction
&a808 c6 12    DEC &12 ; this_object_timer (fireball timer)         # Only used for animation, not lifespan
&a80a a2 14    LDX #&14                                             # Permanent fireballs causes 20 damage
&a80c d0 84    BNE &a792 ; consider_fireball_damage_and_animate     # Always branches

# Placeholders (OBJECT_PLACEHOLDER)
# =================================
# Tertiary data byte is as follows:
#
# 8....... if set, placeholder needs primary object creating
# .4218421 type of object

; update_placeholder_object                                         # Called with Y = object touching, or negative if none
&a80e 30 05    BMI &a815 ; not_touching_other_object
&a810 20 5e a6 JSR &a65e ; check_if_object_can_trigger_switches     # Returns carry set if object can trigger
&a813 b0 14    BCS &a829 ; convert_placeholder_object
; not_touching_other_object
&a815 a4 07    LDY &07 ; this_object_frame_counter_sixteen          # Every sixteen frames,
&a817 d0 3a    BNE &a853 ; set_this_object_velocities_to_zero_and_position_from_previous_position
&a819 a5 bc    LDA &bc ; this_object_data
&a81b 20 df 2d JSR &2ddf ; get_range_for_object_type_A
&a81e e0 09    CPX #&09 ; OBJECT_RANGE_EQUIPMENT                    # If it isn't a collectable,
&a820 f0 31    BEQ &a853 ; set_this_object_velocities_to_zero_and_position_from_previous_position
;                                                                   # i.e. it is a robot, Chatter or the boulder,
&a822 a2 00    LDX #&00 ; OBJECT_SLOT_PLAYER                        # Can the placeholder object see the player?
&a824 20 01 36 JSR &3601 ; check_for_obstruction_between_objects_80 # Returns carry clear if no obstruction to player
&a827 b0 2a    BCS &a853 ; set_this_object_velocities_to_zero_and_position_from_previous_position
; convert_placeholder_object                                        # If so, convert into a proper object
&a829 a5 bc    LDA &bc ; this_object_data                           # Top bit is clear, as placeholder is already primary
&a82b 85 41    STA &41 ; this_object_type
&a82d a9 ff    LDA #&ff
&a82f 85 15    STA &15 ; this_object_energy
&a831 60       RTS

# Cyan/yellow/green key (OBJECT_CYAN_YELLOW_GREEN_KEY)
# Red/yellow/green key (OBJECT_RED_YELLOW_GREEN_KEY)
# Green/yellow/red key (OBJECT_GREEN_YELLOW_RED_KEY)
# Yellow/white/red key (OBJECT_YELLOW_WHITE_RED_KEY)
# Red/magenta/red key (OBJECT_RED_MAGENTA_RED_KEY)
# Blue/cyan/green key (OBJECT_BLUE_CYAN_GREEN_KEY)
# Jetpack booster (OBJECT_JETPACK_BOOSTER)
# Pistol (OBJECT_PISTOL)
# Icer (OBJECT_ICER)
# Blaster (OBJECT_BLASTER)
# Plasma gun (OBJECT_PLASMA_GUN)
# Protection suit (OBJECT_PROTECTION_SUIT)
# Fire immunity device (OBJECT_FIRE_IMMUNITY_DEVICE)
# Mushroom immunity pill (OBJECT_MUSHROOM_IMMUNITY_PILL)
# Whistle one (OBJECT_WHISTLE_ONE)
# Whistle two (OBJECT_WHISTLE_TWO)
# Radiation immunity pill (OBJECT_RADIATION_IMMUNITY_PILL)
# ========================================================
# objects_energy is used for disturbance

; update_collectable_object
&a832 a5 dd    LDA &dd ; player_object_held
&a834 c5 aa    CMP &aa ; this_object
&a836 d0 0f    BNE &a847 ; not_holding
; is_holding
&a838 a6 41    LDX &41 ; this_object_type
&a83a de b5 07 DEC &07b5,X ; player_collected - &51 (OBJECT_CYAN_YELLOW_GREEN_KEY) # Set to negative to indicate object collected
&a83d 20 0f 14 JSR &140f ; play_sound
&a840 72 a5 7b 85                                                   # Play sound for collecting object
&a844 4c 58 25 JMP &2529 ; set_object_for_removal
; not_holding
; consider_disturbing_object                                        # Collectables are static until touched by another object
&a847 a4 3b    LDY &3b ; this_object_touching                       # Negative if not touching another object
&a849 30 04    BMI &a84f ; not_touching_other_object
; is_touching
&a84b 06 15    ASL &15 ; this_object_energy (collectable disturbed)
&a84d 46 15    LSR &15 ; this_object_energy (collectable disturbed) # Clear top bit to indicate object disturbed
; not_touching_other_object
&a84f 24 15    BIT &15 ; this_object_energy (collectable disturbed) # Top bit clear if object has been disturbed
&a851 10 6b    BPL &a8be ; leave                                    # Leave if object touching another object
; update_bush
; set_this_object_velocities_to_zero_and_position_from_previous_position
&a853 20 d2 28 JSR &28d2 ; set_this_object_velocities_to_zero
&a856 4c d9 28 JMP &28d9 ; set_this_object_position_from_previous_position

# Small hive (OBJECT_SMALL_HIVE)
# Large hive (OBJECT_LARGE_HIVE)
# ==============================
# Tertiary data byte is as follows:
#
# 8....... if set, hive needs primary object creating
# .42184.. object type of spawn
# ......21 if non-zero, hive is inactive
#
# Tertiary tile byte sets aggressiveness of spawn
#
# 8....... if set, consider spawning when nest or pipe is plotted
#
# objects_state is used for spawn type

; update_hive                                                       # Called with A = this_object_data
&a859 4a       LSR A                                                # .42184.. -> ..42184.
&a85a 4a       LSR A                                                #          -> ...42184
&a85b 85 11    STA &11 ; this_object_state (hive spawn type)
&a85d 20 48 3c JSR &3c48 ; consider_absorbing_object_touched        # Hives absorb their spawn
&a860 a9 46    LDA #&46
&a862 20 95 35 JSR &3595 ; give_object_minimum_energy               # Hives have a minimum energy of 70
&a865 24 c5    BIT &c5 ; every_four_frames
&a867 10 55    BPL &a8be ; leave                                    # Once every four frames,
&a869 a5 bc    LDA &bc ; this_object_data
&a86b 29 03    AND #&03
&a86d d0 4f    BNE &a8be ; leave                                    # If the hive is active,
&a86f a5 11    LDA &11 ; this_object_state (hive spawn type)
&a871 20 7f 3c JSR &3c7f ; count_objects_of_type_A                  # Count the number of spawned objects currently present
&a874 20 b6 25 JSR &25b6 ; rnd
&a877 25 d9    AND &d9 ; rnd_state
&a879 25 db    AND &db ; rnd_state + 2
&a87b 29 07    AND #&07
&a87d c5 9f    CMP &9f ; count
&a87f 90 3d    BCC &a8be ; leave                                    # Less likely to spawn when more spawn already present
&a881 a9 0e    LDA #&0e ; OBJECT_BIG_FISH
&a883 a0 86    LDY #&86 ; OBJECT_RANGE_FLYING_ENEMIES
&a885 20 91 3c JSR &3c91 ; find_object                              # Returns positive if object found
&a888 10 34    BPL &a8be ; leave                                    # Don't spawn if big fish or flying enemies are present
&a88a 20 0f 14 JSR &140f ; play_sound
&a88d 33 f3 4f 35                                                   # Play sound for hive spawning
&a891 a5 37    LDA &37 ; this_object_x_flip
&a893 29 80    AND #&80
&a895 85 b5    STA &b5 ; angle                                      # Spawn comes out either left or right, depending on flip
&a897 a9 20    LDA #&20 ; magnitude
&a899 20 8a 23 JSR &238a ; calculate_vector_from_magnitude_and_angle
&a89c a5 11    LDA &11 ; this_object_state (hive spawn type)
&a89e 20 1f 34 JSR &341f ; create_child_object                      # Returns carry clear if object created, X = slot
&a8a1 b0 1b    BCS &a8be ; leave
&a8a3 a5 aa    LDA &aa ; this_object
&a8a5 9d 06 09 STA &0906,X ; objects_target_object_and_flags        # Set hive as target for spawn
&a8a8 a9 20    LDA #&20 ; 1 in 8 chance of targeting player
&a8aa 24 37    BIT &37 ; this_object_x_flip
&a8ac 30 02    BMI &a8b0 ; set_aggressiveness                       # If the hive isn't horizontally flipped,
&a8ae a9 a0    LDA #&a0 ; 5 in 8 chance of targeting player         # its spawn are more aggressive
; set_aggressiveness
&a8b0 9d 76 09 STA &0976,X ; (wasp or piranha aggressiveness)
&a8b3 0a       ASL A                                                # Set carry if more aggressive
&a8b4 90 08    BCC &a8be ; leave
&a8b6 bd d6 08 LDA &08d6,X ; objects_palette
&a8b9 49 3b    EOR #&3b                                             # More aggressive wasps use palette &7c (ywW)
;                                                                   # More aggressive piranha use palette &68 (ggC)
&a8bb 9d d6 08 STA &08d6,X ; objects_palette
; leave
&a8be 60       RTS

# Engine fire (OBJECT_ENGINE_FIRE)
# ================================
# Tertiary data byte is as follows:
#
# 8....... if set, engine fire needs primary object creating
# ......21 if non-zero, engine is inactive
#
# objects_timer is used for timer

; update_engine_fire                                                # Called with A = X = this_object_data, Y = object touching 
&a8bf 29 03    AND #&03                                             # Non-zero if engine switched off
&a8c1 d0 5f    BNE &a922 ; reset_and_hide_fire
&a8c3 e6 11    INC &11 ; this_object_state (engine fire timer)      # Switch off engine fire after 256 frames
&a8c5 10 04    BPL &a8cb ; skip_switching_off
&a8c7 e8       INX                                                  # Set lowest two bits to &02 to switch off fire
&a8c8 e8       INX
&a8c9 86 bc    STX &bc ; this_object_data
; skip_switching_off
&a8cb a5 dc    LDA &dc ; rnd_state + 3
&a8cd c5 11    CMP &11 ; this_object_state (engine fire timer)
&a8cf 90 53    BCC &a924 ; hide_fire                                # Fire more likely to be hidden later in burn
&a8d1 0a       ASL A
&a8d2 85 37    STA &37 ; this_object_x_flip                         # Set horizontal flip at random
&a8d4 0a       ASL A
&a8d5 85 39    STA &39 ; this_object_y_flip                         # Set vertical flip at random
&a8d7 98       TYA
&a8d8 30 04    BMI &a8de ; not_touching_other_object
&a8da aa       TAX
&a8db fe e6 08 INC &08e6,X ; objects_velocity_x                     # Accelerate any object touching the fire
; not_touching_other_object
&a8de a0 ff    LDY #&ff
&a8e0 84 87    STY &87 ; new_particles_x_fraction
&a8e2 a5 db    LDA &db ; rnd_state + 2
&a8e4 85 89    STA &89 ; new_particles_y_fraction
&a8e6 a5 53    LDA &53 ; this_object_x
&a8e8 85 8b    STA &8b ; new_particles_x
&a8ea a5 55    LDA &55 ; this_object_y
&a8ec 85 8d    STA &8d ; new_particles_y
&a8ee c8       INY ; 0
&a8ef 84 b5    STY &b5 ; angle                                      # Engine fire particles move right
&a8f1 a0 37    LDY #&37 ; PARTICLE_ENGINE
&a8f3 20 bf 21 JSR &21bf ; add_particle                             # Add particle for engine fire
&a8f6 a5 c0    LDA &c0 ; frame_counter
&a8f8 18       CLC
&a8f9 65 55    ADC &55 ; this_object_y
&a8fb 29 03    AND #&03
&a8fd d0 13    BNE &a912 ; skip_blowing_other_objects_away          # 1 in 4 chance of fire blowing other objects away
&a8ff 38       SEC
&a900 66 28    ROR &28 ; acceleration_damages_targets               # Set &80 to damage targets accelerated too much
&a902 a9 50    LDA #&50 ; 10 tiles
&a904 85 35    STA &35 ; acceleration_power
&a906 a9 14    LDA #&14 ; angle range                               # +/- 28.125 degrees
&a908 20 a3 34 JSR &34a3 ; accelerate_all_objects_within_angle
&a90b 20 0d 14 JSR &140d ; play_sound_on_channel_zero
&a90e 70 c2 6e a3                                                   # Play sound for engine fire
; skip_blowing_other_objects_away
&a912 a0 34    LDY #&34 ; rwY                                       # Make fire visible
&a914 a5 c0    LDA &c0 ; frame_counter
&a916 2a       ROL A
&a917 2a       ROL A
&a918 2a       ROL A
&a919 2a       ROL A
&a91a 65 c0    ADC &c0 ; frame_counter
&a91c 29 3f    AND #&3f                                             # Set random x fraction between &90 and &cf
&a91e 69 90    ADC #&90
&a920 d0 06    BNE &a928 ; set_palette_and_x_fraction               # Always branches
; reset_and_hide_fire
&a922 85 11    STA &11 ; this_object_state (engine fire timer)      # Set to zero to reset fire
; hide_fire
&a924 a0 00    LDY #&00 ; kyK
&a926 a9 40    LDA #&40                                             # Hide fire behind foreground part of tile
; set_palette_and_x_fraction
&a928 84 73    STY &73 ; this_object_palette
&a92a 85 4f    STA &4f ; this_object_x_fraction
&a92c 60       RTS

# Horizontal metal door (OBJECT_HORIZONTAL_METAL_DOOR)
# Vertical metal door (OBJECT_VERTICAL_METAL_DOOR)
# Horizontal stone door (OBJECT_HORIZONTAL_STONE_DOOR)
# Vertical stone door (OBJECT_VERTICAL_STONE_DOOR)
# ====================================================
# Tertiary data byte is as follows:
#
# 8....... if set, door needs primary object creating
# .421.... door colour
# ....8... if set, door is slow, is being or has been destroyed (DOOR_FLAG_SLOW_OR_DESTROYED)
# .....4.. if set, door is moving (DOOR_FLAG_MOVING)
# ......2. if set, door is opening; if clear, door is closing (DOOR_FLAG_OPENING)
# .......1 if set, door is locked (DOOR_FLAG_LOCKED)
#
# objects_state is used for tile x or tile y
# objects_tx is used for open fraction
# objects_ty is used for orientation

; update_door                                                       # Called with Y = object touching, or negative if none
&a92d 30 08    BMI &a937 ; not_touched
&a92f 20 5e a6 JSR &a65e ; check_if_object_can_trigger_switches     # Returns carry set if object can trigger door
&a932 b0 03    BCS &a937 ; was_touched
&a934 38       SEC
&a935 66 3b    ROR &3b ; this_object_touching                       # Otherwise, set top bit to indicate door wasn't touched
; was_touched
; not_touched
&a937 46 39    LSR &39 ; this_object_y_flip                         # Clear top bit to prevent door being flipped vertically
&a939 a6 16    LDX &16 ; this_object_ty (door orientation)          # 0 for x, 2 for y
&a93b a4 11    LDY &11 ; this_object_state (door tile x or y)
&a93d 94 53    STY &53,X ; this_object_x                            # Fix door to tile
&a93f a9 ff    LDA #&ff
&a941 95 4f    STA &4f,X ; this_object_x_fraction                   # Fraction will be updated later
&a943 a5 3d    LDA &3d ; this_object_tertiary_data_offset           # Stop door being created as tertiary object when tile considered
&a945 8d 00 36 STA &3600 ; door_to_suppress                         # Unnecessary code; performed in check_for_obstruction_between_objects
&a948 20 c5 0b JSR &0bc5 ; check_if_object_hit_by_remote_control    # Returns carry clear if object hit by remote control
&a94b b0 05    BCS &a952 ; skip_toggling_door_lock
&a94d a9 40    LDA #&40                                             # &40 set to use data bits for door
&a94f 20 13 32 JSR &3213 ; consider_toggling_lock                   # If so, consider unlocking door
; skip_toggling_door_lock
&a952 6e 00 36 ROR &3600 ; door_to_suppress                         # Allow door to be created when tile next considered
&a955 a5 bc    LDA &bc ; this_object_data
&a957 09 04    ORA #&04 ; DOOR_FLAG_MOVING                          # Set door moving by default
&a959 48       PHA ; data
&a95a 4a       LSR A                                                # 84218421 -> .8421842 1
&a95b 6a       ROR A                                                #          -> 1.842184 2
&a95c 6a       ROR A                                                #          -> 21.84218 4 ; &80 set if &02 was set
&a95d 85 9f    STA &9f ; door_opening_and_locked_flags              #                        ; &40 set if &01 was set
&a95f 4a       LSR A                                                # 21.84218 -> 421.8421 8 ; carry set if &08 was set
&a960 29 07    AND #&07                                             #          -> .....421 door colour
&a962 85 9e    STA &9e ; door_colour
&a964 29 03    AND #&03                                             #          -> ......21 door colour pair
&a966 aa       TAX ; door colour pair
&a967 08       PHP ; carry set if slow or being destroyed
&a968 a5 15    LDA &15 ; this_object_energy
&a96a dd 20 aa CMP &aa20,X ; doors_energy_table                     # If the door has more than the energy needed to
&a96d a9 ff    LDA #&ff                                             # destroy its type, give it full energy
&a96f b0 0e    BCS &a97f ; set_door_energy
&a971 28       PLP ; carry set if slow or being destroyed
&a972 08       PHP ; carry set if slow or being destroyed
&a973 a9 00    LDA #&00                                             # If DOOR_FLAG_SLOW_OR_DESTROYED (&08) was
&a975 b0 08    BCS &a97f ; set_door_energy                          # set, give the door zero energy to set it exploding
&a977 28       PLP ; carry set if slow or being destroyed
&a978 68       PLA ; data
&a979 09 08    ORA #&08 ; DOOR_FLAG_SLOW_OR_DESTROYED               # Otherwise, mark it as being destroyed
&a97b 48       PHA ; data
&a97c 08       PHP ; carry set if slow or being destroyed
&a97d b0 02    BCS &a981 ; skip_setting_door_energy                 # Bug: never branches
; set_door_energy
&a97f 85 15    STA &15 ; this_object_energy
; skip_setting_door_energy
&a981 bd 1c aa LDA &aa1c,X ; doors_speed_table                      # Doors move at a speed depending on their colour
&a984 28       PLP ; carry set if slow or being destroyed
&a985 90 02    BCC &a989 ; not_slow_or_being_destroyed
&a987 a9 01    LDA #&01                                             # unless slow or being destroyed
; not_slow_or_being_destroyed
&a989 24 9f    BIT &9f ; door_opening_and_locked_flags              # &80 set if DOOR_FLAG_OPENING (&02) was set
&a98b 30 09    BMI &a996 ; set_door_speed
; is_closing
&a98d 4a       LSR A                                                # Doors close at half the speed they open
&a98e 49 ff    EOR #&ff
&a990 24 3b    BIT &3b ; this_object_touching                       # Positive if door is touching something
&a992 30 02    BMI &a996 ; set_door_speed
&a994 a9 ff    LDA #&ff                                             # unless touching something, when they close very slowly
; set_door_speed
&a996 85 9c    STA &9c ; door_speed
&a998 a5 14    LDA &14 ; this_object_tx (door open fraction)
&a99a 49 80    EOR #&80
&a99c 38       SEC
&a99d e5 9c    SBC &9c ; door_speed                                 # Set V if (fraction EOR &80) crosses &80, i.e. if
&a99f 50 44    BVC &a9e5 ; not_at_end_of_track                      # fraction crosses &00, i.e. door is at end of track
; at_end_of_track
&a9a1 20 e6 32 JSR &32e6 ; prevent_overflow
&a9a4 a8       TAY ; door open fraction EOR &80
&a9a5 10 0c    BPL &a9b3 ; stop_door                                # Positive if door has closed; otherwise, door is open
&a9a7 8a       TXA ; door colour pair                               # Door types 0 (cyG, metal) and 4 (rmb, stone) open, then close
&a9a8 d0 0d    BNE &a9b7 ; skip_stopping_door                       # Other door types stay open or closed
&a9aa ad 19 08 LDA &0819 ; door_timer
&a9ad c9 14    CMP #&14 ; 20                                        # After forty frames,
&a9af b0 06    BCS &a9b7 ; skip_stopping_door
&a9b1 90 19    BCC &a9cc ; toggle_door_opening                      # Always branches
; stop_door
&a9b3 68       PLA ; data
&a9b4 29 fb    AND #&fb ; !DOOR_FLAG_MOVING                         # Clear DOOR_FLAG_MOVING (&04) to stop door moving
&a9b6 48       PHA ; data
; skip_stopping_door
&a9b7 24 3b    BIT &3b ; this_object_touching
&a9b9 30 29    BMI &a9e4 ; set_door_open_fraction_from_Y
&a9bb 24 9f    BIT &9f ; door_opening_and_locked_flags              # &40 set if DOOR_FLAG_LOCKED (&01) was set
&a9bd 70 25    BVS &a9e4 ; set_door_open_fraction_from_Y
; not_locked
&a9bf 8a       TXA ; door colour pair
&a9c0 d0 0a    BNE &a9cc ; toggle_door_opening                      # If door type 0 (cyG, metal) or 4 (rmb, stone),
&a9c2 ad 19 08 LDA &0819 ; door_timer
&a9c5 d0 1d    BNE &a9e4 ; set_door_open_fraction_from_Y
&a9c7 a9 3c    LDA #&3c ; 60                                        # prepare timer for automatic closing
&a9c9 8d 19 08 STA &0819 ; door_timer
; toggle_door_opening
&a9cc 68       PLA ; data
&a9cd 49 02    EOR #&02 ; DOOR_FLAG_OPENING                         # Swap between opening and closing
&a9cf 48       PHA ; data
&a9d0 29 02    AND #&02 ; DOOR_FLAG_OPENING
&a9d2 f0 09    BEQ &a9dd ; is_closing
; is_opening
&a9d4 20 0f 14 JSR &140f ; play_sound
&a9d7 c7 c3 c1 13                                                   # Play sound for door opening; returns carry set
&a9db b0 07    BCS &a9e4 ; set_door_open_fraction_from_Y            # Always branches
; is_closing
&a9dd 20 0f 14 JSR &140f ; play_sound
&a9e0 c7 c3 c1 03                                                   # Play sound for door closing
; set_door_open_fraction_from_Y
&a9e4 98       TYA ; door open fraction EOR &80
; not_at_end_of_track
&a9e5 49 80    EOR #&80                                             # Undo EOR at &a99a
&a9e7 a6 16    LDX &16 ; this_object_ty (door orientation)
&a9e9 a8       TAY ; door open fraction
&a9ea 38       SEC
&a9eb e5 14    SBC &14 ; this_object_tx (door open fraction)
&a9ed c9 80    CMP #&80
&a9ef 6a       ROR A                                                # Set sign of velocity from door open fraction
&a9f0 95 43    STA &43,X ; this_object_velocity_x
&a9f2 98       TYA ; door open fraction
&a9f3 85 14    STA &14 ; this_object_tx (door open fraction)
&a9f5 18       CLC
&a9f6 69 10    ADC #&10 ; 1 pixel                                   # Offset door by one pixel horizontally
&a9f8 95 4f    STA &4f,X ; this_object_x_fraction
&a9fa a5 11    LDA &11 ; this_object_state (door tile x or y)
&a9fc 69 00    ADC #&00
&a9fe 95 53    STA &53,X ; this_object_x
&aa00 68       PLA ; data
&aa01 a4 15    LDY &15 ; this_object_energy
&aa03 d0 02    BNE &aa07 ; not_being_destroyed
&aa05 09 04    ORA #&04 ; DOOR_FLAG_MOVING                          # Set door moving when being destroyed
; not_being_destroyed
&aa07 85 bc    STA &bc ; this_object_data
&aa09 a6 3d    LDX &3d ; this_object_tertiary_data_offset
&aa0b 9d 86 09 STA &0986,X ; tertiary_objects_data
&aa0e a6 9e    LDX &9e ; door_colour
&aa10 bd 24 aa LDA &aa24,X ; doors_palette_table
&aa13 24 9f    BIT &9f ; door_opening_and_locked_flags              # &40 set if DOOR_FLAG_LOCKED (&01) was set
&aa15 70 02    BVS &aa19 ; set_door_palette
; is_unlocked
&aa17 29 0f    AND #&0f                                             # Set colour three of door to black if unlocked
; set_door_palette
&aa19 85 73    STA &73 ; this_object_palette
&aa1b 60       RTS

; doors_speed_table                                                 # 0 = type &00 (cyG) and &04 (rmB)
;      0  1  2  3                                                   # 1 = type &01 (ryG) and &05 (rmR)
&aa1c 20 10 08 20                                                   # 2 = type &02 (gyR) and &06 (bcG)
                                                                    # 3 = type &03 (ywR) and &07 (mwB)

; doors_energy_table                                                # Minimum amount of energy door can have before exploding
;      0  1  2  3                                                   # This gives the following effective minimum energies:
&aa20 80 74 c0 80                                                   # cyG metal doors have a minimum energy of 128
                                                                    # ryG metal doors have a minimum energy of 140
                                                                    # gyR metal doors have a minimum energy of 64
                                                                    # ywR metal doors have a minimum energy of 128
                                                                    # rmB stone doors have a minimum energy of 128
                                                                    # rmR metal doors have a minimum energy of 140
                                                                    # mwB metal and stone doors have a minimum energy of 128

; doors_palette_table
&aa24 2b ; &00 : cyG (becomes &0b, cyK)                             # Cyan frame, yellow highlights, green lock (metal)
&aa25 2d ; &01 : ryG (becomes &0d, ryK)                             # Red frame, yellow highlights, green lock (metal)
&aa26 15 ; &02 : gyR (becomes &05, gyK)                             # Green frame, yellow highlights, red lock (metal)
&aa27 1c ; &03 : ywR (becomes &0c, ywK)                             # Yellow frame, white highlights, red lock (metal)
&aa28 42 ; &04 : rmB (becomes &02, rmK)                             # Red frame, magenta highlights, blue lock (stone)
&aa29 12 ; &05 : rmR (becomes &02, rmK)                             # Red frame, magenta highlights, red lock (metal)
&aa2a 26 ; &06 : bcG (becomes &06, bcK)                             # Blue frame, cyan highlights, green lock (unused)
&aa2b 4e ; &07 : mwB (becomes &0e, mwK)                             # Magenta frame, white highlights, blue lock (metal or stone)

# Transporter beam (OBJECT_TRANSPORTER_BEAM)
# ==========================================
# Tertiary data byte is as follows:
#
# 8....... if set, transporter beam needs primary object creating
# .42..... key to unlock
#              &00 : OBJECT_YELLOW_WHITE_RED_KEY
#              &20 : (key 4, not collectable)
#              &40 : OBJECT_RED_MAGENTA_RED_KEY
#              &60 : OBJECT_BLUE_CYAN_GREEN_KEY
# ...1842. transporter destination
# .......1 if set, transporter is inactive
#
# objects_state is used for y fraction

; transporter_beams_palette_table
&aa2c 52 ; rmM
&aa2d 63 ; rcC
&aa2e 35 ; gyB
&aa2f 21 ; rgG

; update_transporter_beam                                           # Called with A = X = this_object_data, Y = object touching
&aa30 4a       LSR A                                                # 84218421 -> .8421842 1
&aa31 29 0f    AND #&0f                                             #          -> ....1842 1
&aa33 aa       TAX ; destination
&aa34 a9 b0    LDA #&b0 ; y fraction
&aa36 b0 2f    BCS &aa67 ; is_stationary                            # Lowest bit of data set if transporter is stationary
; is_moving
&aa38 98       TYA
&aa39 30 1c    BMI &aa57 ; not_touching_other_object
; is_touching_other_object
&aa3b b9 c6 08 LDA &08c6,Y ; objects_flags
&aa3e 29 10    AND #&10 ; OBJECT_FLAG_TELEPORTING
&aa40 d0 15    BNE &aa57 ; not_touching_object                      # If the touching object isn't already teleporting,
&aa42 bd b1 31 LDA &31b1,X ; transporter_destinations_x_table
&aa45 99 16 09 STA &0916,Y ; objects_tx                             # Used as destination for teleporting objects
&aa48 bd c1 31 LDA &31c1,X ; transporter_destinations_y_table
&aa4b 99 36 09 STA &0936,Y ; objects_ty                             # Used as destination for teleporting objects
&aa4e 20 f0 0c JSR &0cf0 ; set_object_teleporting
&aa51 20 a9 0b JSR &0ba9 ; set_object_Y_velocities_from_this_object
&aa54 20 aa a0 JSR &a0aa ; play_sound_for_teleporting
; not_touching_other_object
&aa57 a5 6f    LDA &6f ; this_object_flags
&aa59 29 04    AND #&04 ; OBJECT_FLAG_NEWLY_CREATED
&aa5b d0 15    BNE &aa72 ; skip_updating_beam_position
&aa5d a5 11    LDA &11 ; this_object_state (beam y fraction)
&aa5f 69 20    ADC #&20
&aa61 c9 b1    CMP #&b1
&aa63 90 02    BCC &aa67 ; skip_wraparound
&aa65 e9 b0    SBC #&b0
; skip_wraparound
; is_stationary
&aa67 85 11    STA &11 ; this_object_state (beam y fraction)
&aa69 24 39    BIT &39 ; this_object_y_flip                         # If transporter base in ceiling, beam moves up
&aa6b 20 b3 32 JSR &32b3 ; invert_if_positive                       # If transporter base in floor, beam moves down
&aa6e 85 51    STA &51 ; this_object_y_fraction
&aa70 c6 51    DEC &51 ; this_object_y_fraction
; skip_updating_beam_position
&aa72 20 c5 0b JSR &0bc5 ; check_if_object_hit_by_remote_control    # Returns carry clear if object hit by remote control
&aa75 b0 05    BCS &aa7c ; not_hit_by_remote_control
&aa77 a9 00    LDA #&00                                             # &00 clear to use data bits for transporter beam
&aa79 20 13 32 JSR &3213 ; consider_toggling_lock
; not_hit_by_remote_control
; rotate_colour_from_frame_counter
&aa7c a5 06    LDA &06 ; this_object_frame_counter
; rotate_colour_from_A
&aa7e 4a       LSR A
&aa7f 4a       LSR A
&aa80 29 03    AND #&03
&aa82 aa       TAX
&aa83 bd 2c aa LDA &aa2c,X ; transporter_beams_palette_table
&aa86 85 73    STA &73 ; this_object_palette
; leave
&aa88 60       RTS

; use_damaged_palette_if_carry_clear
&aa89 a4 41    LDY &41 ; this_object_type
&aa8b b9 ef 02 LDA &02ef,Y ; object_types_palette_and_pickup_table
&aa8e 29 7f    AND #&7f                                             # .4218421 palette
&aa90 b0 02    BCS &aa94 ; set_palette
&aa92 49 30    EOR #&30                                             # Toggle colour 3 : KRGYBMCW -> YGRKWCMB
; set_palette
&aa94 85 73    STA &73 ; this_object_palette
&aa96 60       RTS

# Sucking nests (OBJECT_SUCKING_NEST)
# ===================================
# Tertiary data byte is as follows:
#
# 8....... if set, sucking nest needs primary object creating
# .4218421 sucking nest type
#              &00 : cyan/green,   triggered by all objects, attracts with power &50
#              &01 : blue/magenta, triggered by OBJECT_HORIZONTAL_STONE_DOOR, repels with power &30
#              &02 : cyan/magenta, triggered by OBJECT_WASP, attracts with power &7f
#              &03 : magenta/red,  triggered by triggered by OBJECT_CORONIUM_BOULDER, attracts with power &40
#              &04 : yellow/white, triggered by OBJECT_PIRANHA, attracts with power &50
#              &05 : yellow/green, triggered by all objects, repels with power &7f
#              &06 : red/magenta,  triggered by OBJECT_CORONIUM_BOULDER, repels with power &7f
#              &07 : yellow/cyan,  triggered by OBJECT_PIRANHA, repels with power &50
#              &08 : green/green,  triggered by OBJECT_WORM, attracts with power &40
#
# objects_state is used for activeness

; update_sucking_nest                                               # Called with X = this_object_data, Y = object touching
&aa97 bd f3 aa LDA &aaf3,X ; sucking_nests_palette_direction_table  # Top seven bits set palette
&aa9a 4a       LSR A                                                # Clear top bit to use background plotting
&aa9b 85 73    STA &73 ; this_object_palette
&aa9d a5 07    LDA &07 ; this_object_frame_counter_sixteen
&aa9f f0 14    BEQ &aab5 ; consider_sucking
&aaa1 bd e1 aa LDA &aae1,X ; sucking_nests_trigger_table            # Negative if sucking nest is triggered by all objects
&aaa4 30 0d    BMI &aab3 ; set_state                                # If so, &80 is set, so sucking nest is always active
&aaa6 a8       TAY
&aaa7 c9 55    CMP #&55 ; OBJECT_CORONIUM_BOULDER
&aaa9 d0 02    BNE &aaad ; not_coronium_boulder
&aaab a0 0b    LDY #&0b ; OBJECT_YELLOW_SLIME
; not_coronium_boulder
&aaad 20 91 3c JSR &3c91 ; find_object                              # Returns X = object, or negative if none
&aab0 8a       TXA
&aab1 49 ff    EOR #&ff                                             # Set &80 if object found, to make sucking nest active
; set_state
&aab3 85 11    STA &11 ; this_object_state (sucking nest active)
; consider_sucking
&aab5 a5 11    LDA &11 ; this_object_state (sucking nest active)    # &80 set if sucking nest is active
&aab7 10 18    BPL &aad1 ; skip_accelerating_other_objects
&aab9 a6 bc    LDX &bc ; this_object_data
&aabb bd f3 aa LDA &aaf3,X ; sucking_nests_palette_direction_table  # Lowest bit set if attracting, clear if repelling
&aabe 4a       LSR A
&aabf 66 29    ROR &29 ; acceleration_sign                          # &80 set if accelerating towards nest
&aac1 bd ea aa LDA &aaea,X ; sucking_nests_power_table
&aac4 85 35    STA &35 ; acceleration_power
&aac6 20 a1 34 JSR &34a1 ; accelerate_all_objects                   # Accelerate all objects within line of sight,
;                                                                   # depending on weight and distance from sucking nest
&aac9 a5 db    LDA &db ; rnd_state + 2
&aacb 85 37    STA &37 ; this_object_x_flip                         # Flip sucking nest horizontally at random
&aacd c9 50    CMP #&50                                             # 1 in 256 chance of sucking nest causing 80 damage
&aacf f0 02    BEQ &aad3 ; consider_damaging_target
; skip_accelerating_other_objects
&aad1 a9 02    LDA #&02                                             # Otherwise, sucking nest causes 2 damage
; consider_damaging_target
&aad3 a4 3b    LDY &3b ; this_object_touching                       # Negative if not touching any object
&aad5 30 b1    BMI &aa88 ; leave
&aad7 20 0f 14 JSR &140f ; play_sound
&aada 57 07 57 97                                                   # Play sound for sucking nest touching object
&aade 4c d5 24 JMP &24d5 ; damage_object                            # Sucking nest causes damage depending on chance

; sucking_nests_trigger_table
&aae1 ff ; type &00 : all objects
&aae2 3e ; type &01 : OBJECT_HORIZONTAL_STONE_DOOR
&aae3 11 ; type &02 : OBJECT_WASP
&aae4 55 ; type &03 : OBJECT_CORONIUM_BOULDER (and OBJECT_YELLOW_SLIME)
&aae5 10 ; type &04 : OBJECT_PIRANHA
&aae6 ff ; type &05 : all objects
&aae7 55 ; type &06 : OBJECT_CORONIUM_BOULDER (and OBJECT_YELLOW_SLIME)
&aae8 10 ; type &07 : OBJECT_PIRANHA
&aae9 0f ; type &08 : OBJECT_WORM

; sucking_nests_power_table
&aaea 50 ; type &00 :  10 tiles
&aaeb 30 ; type &01 :   6 tiles
&aaec 7f ; type &02 : ~16 tiles
&aaed 40 ; type &03 :   8 tiles
&aaee 50 ; type &04 :  10 tiles
&aaef 7f ; type &05 : ~16 tiles
&aaf0 7f ; type &06 : ~16 tiles
&aaf1 50 ; type &07 :  10 tiles
&aaf2 40 ; type &08 :   8 tiles

; sucking_nests_palette_direction_table                             # 8421842. palette
&aaf3 5f ; type &00 : &2f (cwG), attracts                           # .......1 if set attract, if clear repel
&aaf4 ac ; type &01 : &56 (bcM). repels
&aaf5 bf ; type &02 : &5f (cwM). attracts
&aaf6 3d ; type &03 : &1e (mwR). attracts
&aaf7 f9 ; type &04 : &7c (ywW). attracts
&aaf8 58 ; type &05 : &2c (ywG). repels
&aaf9 a2 ; type &06 : &51 (rgM). repels
&aafa d8 ; type &07 : &6c (ywC). repels
&aafb 4b ; type &08 : &25 (gyG). attracts

# Maggots (OBJECT_MAGGOT)
# =======================
# Spawned from earth tiles by random events, and created by maggot machine.
#
# objects_state is used for NPC behaviour and walking
# objects_timer is used for jump cooldown

; update_maggot
&aafc a5 15    LDA &15 ; this_object_energy
&aafe 29 7f    AND #&7f
&ab00 85 15    STA &15 ; this_object_energy
&ab02 a9 82    LDA #&82 ; OBJECT_CREW_MEMBER | &80                  # Set &80 to target player too
&ab04 a0 2f    LDY #&2f ; OBJECT_WHITE_YELLOW_BIRD
&ab06 a2 14    LDX #&14                                             # Maggots cause 20 damage
; update_worm_or_maggot
&ab08 8e 30 ab STX &ab30 ; worm_or_maggot_damage
&ab0b 20 5f 3c JSR &3c5f ; consider_finding_target
&ab0e 20 8d 3d JSR &3d8d ; consider_updating_npc_path
&ab11 20 31 2a JSR &2a31 ; consider_npc_burrowing                   # Returns negative if NPC removed or burrowing
&ab14 30 57    BMI &ab6d ; animate_worm_or_maggot
&ab16 20 b6 25 JSR &25b6 ; rnd
&ab19 24 1f    BIT &1f ; this_object_in_water                       # Positive if object is in any water
&ab1b 30 02    BMI &ab1f ; not_underwater
; is_underwater
&ab1d a9 ff    LDA #&ff                                             # Worms and maggots always want to borrow underwater
; not_underwater
&ab1f 06 11    ASL &11 ; this_object_state (behaviour and walking)
&ab21 c9 f6    CMP #&f6                                             # Otherwise, 10 in 256 chance of wanting to burrow
&ab23 66 11    ROR &11 ; this_object_state (behaviour and walking)  # Set top bit if worm or maggot wants to burrow
&ab25 a4 3b    LDY &3b ; this_object_touching
&ab27 c4 0e    CPY &0e ; this_object_target_object
&ab29 d0 09    BNE &ab34 ; not_touching_target
&ab2b a9 0a    LDA #&0a
&ab2d 85 12    STA &12 ; this_object_timer (npc jump cooldown)
&ab2f a9 14    LDA #&14
#     actually LDA worm_or_maggot_damage
&ab31 20 d5 24 JSR &24d5 ; damage_object                            # Maggot or worm damage depends on object type
; not_touching_target
&ab34 a6 20    LDX &20 ; this_object_waterline
&ab36 ca       DEX                                                  # &00 becomes &ff if entirely above water
;                                                                   # &ff becomes &fe is entirely under water
&ab37 30 05    BMI &ab3e ; not_at_waterline                         # Positive if maggot is close to waterline
&ab39 a6 c3    LDX &c3 ; every_sixteen_frames                       # Negative every sixteen frames
&ab3b 8e 14 2a STX &2a14 ; this_object_object_collision_y_flags     # Set &80 to simulate collision to bottom
;                                                                   # This makes the maggot or worm jump off the waterline
; not_at_waterline
&ab3e a9 10    LDA #&10 ; speed
&ab40 24 3e    BIT &3e ; this_object_target_object_and_flags        # &80 set if TARGET_FLAG_DIRECTNESS_TWO or TARGET_FLAG_DIRECTNESS_THREE
&ab42 10 1e    BPL &ab62 ; not_seen_target
; has_seen_target
&ab44 0a       ASL A                                                # Move twice as fast if can see or has seen target
&ab45 48       PHA ; speed
&ab46 20 c0 35 JSR &35c0 ; get_object_distance_from_screen_centre
&ab49 c9 0f    CMP #&0f
&ab4b b0 14    BCS &ab61 ; skip_sound                               # Don't play sound if far away
&ab4d 49 0f    EOR #&0f
&ab4f c5 db    CMP &db ; rnd_state + 2                              # More likely to play sound if nearer
&ab51 90 0e    BCC &ab61 ; skip_sound                               # (1 in 16 chance at middle of screen)
&ab53 20 0f 14 JSR &140f ; play_sound
&ab56 33 f3 09 b4                                                   # Play sound for worm or maggot squealing
&ab5a 20 0f 14 JSR &140f
&ab5d 33 f3 07 b5                                                   # Play sound for worm or maggot squealing
; skip_sound_and_pull
&ab61 68       PLA ; speed
; not_seen_target
&ab62 a2 06    LDX #&06 ; npc walking type
&ab64 20 46 3b JSR &3b46 ; update_walking_npc_and_check_for_obstacles_with_speed_A # Returns carry clear if jumping
&ab67 b0 04    BCS &ab6d ; animate_worm_or_maggot
&ab69 a9 06    LDA #&06
&ab6b 85 12    STA &12 ; this_object_timer (npc jump cooldown)
; animate_worm_or_maggot
&ab6d 20 a7 25 JSR &25a7 ; consider_flipping_object_to_match_velocity_x
&ab70 a5 45    LDA &45 ; this_object_velocity_y
&ab72 e9 04    SBC #&04
&ab74 85 39    STA &39 ; this_object_y_flip                         # Flip vertically if y velocity < 4
&ab76 a5 06    LDA &06 ; this_object_frame_counter
&ab78 29 04    AND #&04
&ab7a 4a       LSR A                                                # Set &02 4 frames in 8 to make worm or maggot wriggle
&ab7b 05 12    ORA &12 ; this_object_timer (npc jump cooldown)
&ab7d 85 12    STA &12 ; this_object_timer (npc jump cooldown)
&ab7f 4c 79 a1 JMP &a179 ; animate_sprite_from_timer                # Use SPRITE_WORM_ONE if not jumping or wriggling,
;                                                                   # SPRITE_WORM_TWO or SPRITE_WORM_THREE otherwise

# Green/white turrets (OBJECT_GREEN_WHITE_TURRET)
# Cyan/red turrets (OBJECT_CYAN_RED_TURRET)
# ===============================================
# Tertiary data byte is as follows:
#
# 8....... if set, turret needs primary object creating
# .421842. projectile type
# .......1 if set, turret is inactive
#
# Magenta rolling robot (OBJECT_MAGENTA_ROLLING_ROBOT)
# Red rolling robot (OBJECT_RED_ROLLING_ROBOT)
# Blue rolling robot (OBJECT_BLUE_ROLLING_ROBOT)
# ====================================================

; update_turret                                                     # Called with A = this_object_data
&ab82 4a       LSR A                                                # .......1 set if turret is inactive
&ab83 b0 35    BCS &abba ; set_turret_or_robot_energy
; is_active
&ab85 aa       TAX                                                  # .421842. projectile type
&ab86 d0 1b    BNE &aba3 ; consider_firing                          # Always branches
; update_rolling_robot
&ab88 24 15    BIT &15 ; this_object_energy
&ab8a 10 2e    BPL &abba ; set_turret_or_robot_energy               # Magenta and red rolling robots don't move if energy < 128
; update_blue_rolling_robot
&ab8c a2 05    LDX #&05 ; npc stimuli type
&ab8e 20 f8 27 JSR &27f8 ; check_for_npc_stimuli
&ab91 20 8d 3d JSR &3d8d ; consider_updating_npc_path
&ab94 a2 04    LDX #&04 ; npc walking type
&ab96 a9 18    LDA #&18 ; speed
&ab98 20 46 3b JSR &3b46 ; update_walking_npc_and_check_for_obstacles_with_speed_A
&ab9b 20 a7 25 JSR &25a7 ; consider_flipping_object_to_match_velocity_x
&ab9e a4 41    LDY &41 ; this_object_type
&aba0 be ac ab LDX &abac,Y ; rolling_robots_bullet_table - &1c (OBJECT_MAGENTA_ROLLING_ROBOT)
; consider_firing
&aba3 24 15    BIT &15 ; this_object_energy
&aba5 10 13    BPL &abba ; set_turret_or_robot_energy               # Rolling robots and turrets don't fire if energy < 128
&aba7 a0 84    LDY #&84 ; OBJECT_RANGE_PROJECTILES
&aba9 a5 55    LDA &55 ; this_object_y
&abab c9 b4    CMP #&b4                                             # Is this the turret at the top of Triax's lab?
&abad b0 06    BCS &abb5 ; not_triax_lab_turret
&abaf 24 db    BIT &db ; rnd_state + 2
&abb1 70 02    BVS &abb5 ; not_triax_lab_turret
&abb3 a0 86    LDY #&86 ; OBJECT_RANGE_FLYING_ENEMIES
; not_triax_lab_turret
&abb5 a9 81    LDA #&81 ; OBJECT_ACTIVE_CHATTER | &80               # &80 set to target player too
&abb7 20 97 27 JSR &2797 ; find_a_target_and_fire_at_it
; set_turret_or_robot_energy
&abba a6 41    LDX &41 ; this_object_type
&abbc bc a6 ab LDY &aba6,X ; rolling_robots_minimum_energy_table
&abbf 4c a1 35 JMP &35a1 ; gain_energy_Y_and_flash_if_damaged       # Returns carry clear if energy < &80

; rolling_robots_minimum_energy_table
&abc2 14 ; &1c (OBJECT_MAGENTA_ROLLING_ROBOT)                       # Magenta rolling robots have a minimum energy of 20
&abc3 46 ; &1d (OBJECT_RED_ROLLING_ROBOT)                           # Red rolling robots have a minimum energy of 70
&abc4 46 ; &1e (OBJECT_BLUE_ROLLING_ROBOT)                          # Blue rolling robots have a minimum energy of 70
&abc5 14 : &1f (OBJECT_GREEN_WHITE_TURRET)                          # Green/white turrets have a minimum energy of 20
&abc6 7f : &1f (OBJECT_CYAN_RED_TURRET)                             # Cyan/red turrets have a minimum energy of 127
&abc7 14 : &1f (OBJECT_HOVERING_ROBOT)                              # Hovering robots have a minimum energy of 20

; rolling_robots_bullet_table
&abc8 18 ; &1c (OBJECT_MAGENTA_ROLLING_ROBOT) : OBJECT_PISTOL_BULLET
&abc9 13 ; &1d (OBJECT_RED_ROLLING_ROBOT)     : OBJECT_ICER_BULLET
&abca 14 ; &1e (OBJECT_BLUE_ROLLING_ROBOT)    : OBJECT_TRACER_BULLET

# Piranhas (OBJECT_PIRANHA)
# Wasps (OBJECT_WASP)
# =========================
# objects_state is used for aggressiveness
# objects_timer is used for sprite offset

; update_piranha_or_wasp
&abcb a9 05    LDA #&05 ; OBJECT_LARGE_HIVE                         # Wasps use large hives as default target
&abcd a4 41    LDY &41 ; this_object_type
&abcf c0 11    CPY #&11 ; OBJECT_WASP                               # Set carry if wasp, clear carry if piranha
&abd1 66 39    ROR &39 ; this_object_y_flip                         # Negative if wasp, positive if piranha
&abd3 30 06    BMI &abdb ; is_wasp
; is_piranha
&abd5 a9 04    LDA #&04                                             # Piranhas sink
&abd7 85 42    STA &42 ; this_object_acceleration_y
&abd9 a9 04    LDA #&04 ; OBJECT_SMALL_HIVE                         # Piranhas target small hives as home
; is_wasp
&abdb c6 42    DEC &42 ; this_object_acceleration_y                 # No gravity for wasps
&abdd 24 db    BIT &db ; rnd_state + 2
&abdf 70 0b    BVS &abec ; skip_finding_target                      # 1 in 2 chance of considering finding target every sixteen frames
&abe1 a6 11    LDX &11 ; this_object_state (wasp or piranha aggressiveness)
&abe3 e4 da    CPX &da ; rnd_state + 1                              # More aggressive spawn more likely to target player than hive
&abe5 b0 02    BCS &abe9 ; find_target_for_piranha_or_wasp
; target_player
&abe7 a9 00    LDA #&00 ; OBJECT_PLAYER
; find_target_for_piranha_or_wasp
&abe9 20 5f 3c JSR &3c5f ; consider_finding_target
; skip_finding_target
&abec 20 8d 3d JSR &3d8d ; consider_updating_npc_path
&abef 20 b6 25 JSR &25b6 ; rnd
&abf2 f0 0d    BEQ &ac01 ; play_sound_for_piranha_or_wasp           # 1 in 256 chance of playing sound when not damaging
&abf4 c5 11    CMP &11 ; this_object_state (wasp or piranha aggressiveness)
&abf6 90 10    BCC &ac08 ; skip_damaging_player                     # More aggressive spawn more likely to damage player
&abf8 a4 3b    LDY &3b ; this_object_touching
&abfa d0 0c    BNE &ac08 ; skip_damaging_player
&abfc a9 18    LDA #&18
&abfe 20 d5 24 JSR &24d5 ; damage_object                            # Piranhas and wasps cause 24 damage
; play_sound_for_piranha_or_wasp                                    # Always play sound if player was damaged
&ac01 20 0f 14 JSR &140f ; play_sound
&ac04 33 f3 4f 35                                                   # Play sound for piranha or wasp
; skip_damaging_player
&ac08 a9 0c    LDA #&0c ; modulus
&ac0a 20 84 25 JSR &2584 ; update_sprite_offset_using_velocities    # Returns A = sprite offset
&ac0d 4a       LSR A                                                # ....8421 -> .....842
&ac0e 4a       LSR A                                                #          -> ......84, i.e. &00 - &0b -> &00 - &02
&ac0f 20 f9 32 JSR &32f9 ; change_object_sprite_to_base_plus_A      # Use SPRITE_PIRANHA_ONE to SPRITE_PIRANHA_THREE
;                                                                   # or SPRITE_WASP_ONE to SPRITE_WASP_THREE
&ac12 20 ad 25 JSR &25ad ; flip_object_to_match_velocity_x
&ac15 24 1b    BIT &1b ; this_object_tile_top_or_bottom_collision   # &80 set if object hit tiles above or below
&ac17 30 06    BMI &ac1f ; move_wasp_or_piranha
&ac19 a5 39    LDA &39 ; this_object_y_flip                         # &80 is set if wasp, clear if piranha
&ac1b 45 1f    EOR &1f ; this_object_in_water                       # &80 set if not in any water
&ac1d 30 26    BMI &ac45 ; leave                                    # Piranhas and wasps don't move if out of element
; move_wasp_or_piranha
&ac1f a9 30    LDA #&30 ; magnitude
&ac21 a0 18    LDY #&18 ; maximum acceleration
&ac23 a2 28    LDX #&28 ; 5 in 32 chance
&ac25 20 41 32 JSR &3241 ; move_towards_target_with_probability_X
&ac28 24 c4    BIT &c4 ; every_eight_frames
&ac2a 10 19    BPL &ac45 ; leave                                    # Every eight frames,
&ac2c 20 b6 25 JSR &25b6 ; rnd
&ac2f 29 02    AND #&02                                             # 0 for x, 2 for y
&ac31 aa       TAX
&ac32 a5 db    LDA &db ; rnd_state + 2
&ac34 29 1f    AND #&1f                                             # add between -&10 to &0f to either x or y acceleration
&ac36 e9 10    SBC #&10
&ac38 75 40    ADC &40,X ; this_object_acceleration_x
&ac3a 95 40    STA &40,X ; this_object_acceleration_x
&ac3c a5 15    LDA &15 ; this_object_energy
&ac3e c9 0a    CMP #&0a
&ac40 b0 03    BCS &ac45 ; leave
&ac42 4c 7d 25 JMP &257d ; increase_energy_by_one_if_not_zero
; leave
&ac45 60       RTS

# Explosions (OBJECT_EXPLOSION)
# =============================
# objects_tertiary_data_offset is used for explosion duration

; update_explosion
&ac46 a9 80    LDA #&80 ; TILE_PROCESSING_FLAG_PLOTTING             # Create primary objects for any tertiary objects
&ac48 85 2d    STA &2d ; tile_processing_mode                       # that are present in tiles surrounding explosion
&ac4a a6 53    LDX &53 ; this_object_x
&ac4c ca       DEX
&ac4d 86 95    STX &95 ; tile_x
; check_for_tertiary_objects_around_explosion_y_loop                # Loop through x - 1, x and x + 1
&ac4f a6 55    LDX &55 ; this_object_y
&ac51 ca       DEX
&ac52 86 97    STX &97 ; tile_y
; check_for_tertiary_objects_around_explosion_x_loop                # Loop through y - 1, y and y + 1
&ac54 20 39 17 JSR &1739 ; get_tile_and_check_for_tertiary_objects
&ac57 a6 55    LDX &55 ; this_object_y
&ac59 e4 97    CPX &97 ; tile_y
&ac5b e6 97    INC &97 ; tile_y
&ac5d b0 f5    BCS &ac54 ; check_for_tertiary_objects_around_explosion_y_loop
&ac5f a6 53    LDX &53 ; this_object_x
&ac61 e4 95    CPX &95 ; tile_x
&ac63 e6 95    INC &95 ; tile_x
&ac65 b0 e8    BCS &ac4f ; check_for_tertiary_objects_around_explosion_x_loop
&ac67 a5 da    LDA &da ; rnd_state + 1                              # Use random palette for explosion
&ac69 29 13    AND #&13 ; &00 &01 &02 &03 &10 &11 &12 &13           # &00 &01 &02 &03 &10 &11 &12 &13
&ac6b 85 73    STA &73 ; this_object_palette                        # kyK rgK rmK rcK kyR rgR rmR rcR
&ac6d a9 0a    LDA #&0a ; 10
&ac6f a0 16    LDY #&16 ; PARTICLE_EXPLOSION
&ac71 20 c1 21 JSR &21c1 ; add_particles                            # Add ten explosion particles
&ac74 a5 3d    LDA &3d ; this_object_tertiary_data_offset (explosion duration)
&ac76 f0 17    BEQ &ac8f ; to_set_object_for_removal
&ac78 c6 3d    DEC &3d ; this_object_tertiary_data_offset (explosion duration)
&ac7a a5 dc    LDA &dc ; rnd_state + 3
&ac7c 29 07    AND #&07
&ac7e c5 3d    CMP &3d ; this_object_tertiary_data_offset (explosion duration)
&ac80 b0 c3    BCS &ac45 ; leave
&ac82 a5 3d    LDA &3d ; this_object_tertiary_data_offset (explosion duration)
&ac84 c9 08    CMP #&08                                             # At the start of a long explosion,
&ac86 66 28    ROR &28 ; acceleration_damages_targets               # Set &80 to damage targets accelerated too much
&ac88 0a       ASL A                                                # Explosion duration sets distance
&ac89 0a       ASL A                                                # 1 duration = 0.25 tiles
&ac8a 85 35    STA &35 ; acceleration_power                         # Explosion acceleration reduces with time
&ac8c 4c a1 34 JMP &34a1 ; accelerate_all_objects                   # Accelerate all other objects away from explosion
; to_set_object_for_removal
&ac8f 4c 58 25 JMP &2558 ; set_object_for_removal

; video_ula_and_register_values
&69e7 14 ; video ULA control register                               # Use MODE 2, disable cursor
&69e8 7f ; R0: Horizontal total register

&69eb 28 ; R3: Sync width register

# Word data
# =========
# First byte:
#    8421.... total number of words on line, or zero to skip pages
#    ....8421 line, or number of pages to skip
#
# Second byte is first checksum of expected word, checked against entered word to give "correct" or "wrong" message
# Third byte is obfuscated second checksum of expected word, checked in check_copy_protection to hang game if incorrect
#
# expected_word_obfuscated_second_checksum = expected_word_second_checksum ^ (word number & &7f) ^ &65

;     wl c1 c2
